<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-50294702" data-id="50294702" data-tags="Entertainment" data-score="3103" data-comments="661" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294702" data-time="1793302899">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/29/arts/television/patrick-stewart-dead.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#c084fc' data-tag='Entertainment'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=nytimes.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>nytimes.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/29/arts/television/patrick-stewart-dead.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Patrick Stewart has died</span><span class='zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特去世</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Patrick Stewart, beloved for Star Trek&#x27;s Captain Picard and a long career in theatre and film, has died.</span><span class='zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特因病去世，曾因星际迷航中帕克船长等角色而家喻户晓。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Patrick Stewart, beloved for Star Trek&#x27;s Captain Picard and a long career in theatre and film, has died. His work helped redefine leadership in science fiction and mentored generations of actors. The news prompts reflection on a prolific career that bridged stage, screen, and iconic franchises.</span><span class='zh'>帕特里克·斯图尔特因病去世，曾因星际迷航中帕克船长等角色而家喻户晓。其横跨舞台、电视和电影的职业生涯对科幻与表演行业产生深远影响。人们由此缅怀这位多产演员及其对文化的贡献。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Entertainment' style='--c:#c084fc'>Entertainment</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/29/top_stories_10292026'><span class='en'>Oct 29</span><span class='zh'>10月29日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>15.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>3103</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50294702' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>661</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50467087" data-id="50467087" data-tags="Business" data-score="2979" data-comments="2806" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50467087" data-time="1794491549">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://apnews.com/article/nvidia-megaspeed-china-singapore-huang-5403f152a601cf82ce939f1d24fffa0f" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang resigns</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋辞职</span></a><span class="dom">apnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang has resigned. The available material does not provide reliable details about the timing, reasons, or succession.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋已辞职。现有材料没有提供有关时间、原因或继任安排的可靠细节。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang has resigned. The available material does not provide reliable details about the timing, reasons, or succession. The departure marks a leadership change at Nvidia.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋已辞职。现有材料没有提供有关时间、原因或继任安排的可靠细节。这意味着英伟达将发生领导层变动。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>25.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>2979</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50467087' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2806</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50378228" data-id="50378228" data-tags="Security" data-score="2978" data-comments="747" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50378228" data-time="1793881142">
<a class="thumb" href="https://sshhole.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://sshhole.com/receipts/3bsd-passwd.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sshhole.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sshhole.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>sshole</span><span class='zh'>sshole</span></a><span class="dom">sshhole.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenSSH on musl Linux has reportedly allowed access to passwordless accounts since 2016.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，musl Linux 上的 OpenSSH 自 2016 年起可能允许进入无密码账户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenSSH on musl Linux has reportedly allowed access to passwordless accounts since 2016. The issue relates to how Unix historically represented accounts that should never permit login, including accounts marked with an asterisk in the password file. The behavior can therefore expose accounts that were intended to be inaccessible.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，musl Linux 上的 OpenSSH 自 2016 年起可能允许进入无密码账户。问题与 Unix 处理本应禁止登录的账户的历史方式有关，包括在密码文件中用星号标记的账户。因此，原本应不可访问的账户可能被暴露。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>2978</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50378228' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>747</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50341174" data-id="50341174" data-tags="AI" data-score="2928" data-comments="880" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50341174" data-time="1793643688">
<a class="thumb" href="https://rikiyat.github.io/blog/weight-space-programming/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://rikiyat.github.io/images/blog/weight-space-programming.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='rikiyat.github.io' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rikiyat.github.io/blog/weight-space-programming/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Astra can read and write weights to improve Qwen</span><span class='zh'>Astra 可读写权重以改进 Qwen</span></a><span class="dom">rikiyat.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The source reports that frontier LLMs can read and write neural-network weights directly.</span><span class='zh'>资料称，前沿大语言模型已经能够直接读取和写入神经网络权重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The source reports that frontier LLMs can read and write neural-network weights directly. GPT-6 Astra can infer what a network was trained to do from its raw weights and create small game-playing networks that outperform ones trained with PPO for 100 million steps. The capability could matter for model optimization and interpretability.</span><span class='zh'>资料称，前沿大语言模型已经能够直接读取和写入神经网络权重。GPT-6 Astra 可以仅凭原始权重判断网络的训练目标，还能从零编写击败经过 1 亿步 PPO 训练的小型游戏网络。这种能力可能对模型优化和可解释性具有重要意义。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://karpathy.bearblog.dev/hacker-tube/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>HackerTube: YouTube built from videos recommended by HN</span><span class='zh'>HackerTube：由 HN 推荐视频构成的 YouTube</span></a><span class="dom">karpathy.bearblog.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HackerTube presents a YouTube-style interface for 6,454 videos sourced from 39,834 Hacker News comments dating back to 2007.</span><span class='zh'>HackerTube 提供了一个 YouTube 风格的界面，收录了自 2007 年以来从 39,834 条 Hacker News 评论中提取的 6,454 个视频。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HackerTube presents a YouTube-style interface for 6,454 videos sourced from 39,834 Hacker News comments dating back to 2007. The collection is available through hackertube.org. It turns years of community recommendations into a browsable video archive.</span><span class='zh'>HackerTube 提供了一个 YouTube 风格的界面，收录了自 2007 年以来从 39,834 条 Hacker News 评论中提取的 6,454 个视频。该视频集合可通过 hackertube.org 浏览。它将多年社区推荐整理成了一个可探索的视频档案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepInfer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepInfer: High-performance inference framework for DeepSeek models</span><span class='zh'>DeepInfer：面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepInfer is a new high-performance inference framework designed for DeepSeek models, aiming to maximize throughput on modern hardware.</span><span class='zh'>DeepInfer 是面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架，旨在于现代硬件上实现最大的吞吐量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepInfer is a new high-performance inference framework designed for DeepSeek models, aiming to maximize throughput on modern hardware. It emphasizes modular backends and optimized runtimes to speed up deployment of large-scale AI.</span><span class='zh'>DeepInfer 是面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架，旨在于现代硬件上实现最大的吞吐量。它通过模块化后端与优化运行时来提升大规模 AI 推理的部署效率。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jjmaxwell4.com/writing/things-to-know-when-you-get-cancer/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Things to know when you get cancer</span><span class='zh'>患癌时应知道的事——2026 版</span></a><span class="dom">jjmaxwell4.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 2026 edition updates what cancer patients need to know, highlighting advances in treatment options and decision-making.</span><span class='zh'>2026 版更新了癌症患者应了解的要点，着重介绍治疗选项的进展与决策过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 2026 edition updates what cancer patients need to know, highlighting advances in treatment options and decision-making. It also flags access to care, side-effect management, and the role of digital health resources.</span><span class='zh'>2026 版更新了癌症患者应了解的要点，着重介绍治疗选项的进展与决策过程。还提到就医获取、副作用管理，以及数字健康资源的作用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hackerbooks.org" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: HackerBooks – HackerTube, but for books mentioned on HN</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：HackerBooks——面向 HN 提及书籍的 HackerTube</span></a><span class="dom">hackerbooks.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HackerBooks catalogs about 16,500 books mentioned by at least two different Hacker News commenters between 2007 and today.</span><span class='zh'>HackerBooks 收录了 2007 年至今至少被两名不同 Hacker News 评论者提到过的约 1.65 万本书。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HackerBooks catalogs about 16,500 books mentioned by at least two different Hacker News commenters between 2007 and today. Each book links back to the comments that mentioned it, and the dataset can be downloaded from the about page. The creator notes that the system counts mentions without distinguishing praise from criticism, so some entries may be missing or mismatched.</span><span class='zh'>HackerBooks 收录了 2007 年至今至少被两名不同 Hacker News 评论者提到过的约 1.65 万本书。每本书都链接回提及它的评论，数据集也可从关于页面下载。创建者说明，系统只统计提及次数，不区分赞扬和批评，因此部分书籍可能遗漏或匹配错误。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arkm.com/explorer/entity/21a94755-f604-45e5-9501-6542e23005e8" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Binance-hacker-1029 – $5.6B stolen from Binance</span><span class='zh'>Binance 黑客案1029：从币安被盗5.6亿美元</span></a><span class="dom">arkm.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A $5.6B theft linked to Binance highlights the ongoing scale of crypto-exchange breaches.</span><span class='zh'>涉及币安的巨额盗窃案，金额约56亿美元，凸显集中交易所安全风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A $5.6B theft linked to Binance highlights the ongoing scale of crypto-exchange breaches. On-chain tracing attempts to map the stolen funds as they move through wallets and mixers, showing how investigators pursue assets long after a hack. The lesson for builders is to strengthen custody security (multi-sig, cold storage) and incident response readiness.</span><span class='zh'>涉及币安的巨额盗窃案，金额约56亿美元，凸显集中交易所安全风险。链上追踪尝试绘出赃款去向，显示即便多年后仍有追踪的可能。对系统设计者的启示是加强资产托管安全（多签、冷存储）与快速事件响应能力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/29/top_stories_10292026'><span class='en'>Oct 29</span><span class='zh'>10月29日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>1750</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50296470' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1240</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50289418" data-id="50289418" data-tags="AI|Open Source" data-score="1724" data-comments="440" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289418" data-time="1793283201">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-4-oss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mistral.ai&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mistral.ai</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">10</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-4-oss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;re releasing the weights of Mistral Large 4</span><span class='zh'>发布 Mistral Large 4 的权重</span></a><span class="dom">mistral.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mistral AI has released the weights for Mistral Large 4, enabling researchers to run and fine-tune the model locally.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral Large 4 的权重已对外公开，研究者和开发者可以在本地部署与微调。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mistral AI has released the weights for Mistral Large 4, enabling researchers to run and fine-tune the model locally. This move reduces reliance on hosted APIs and accelerates experimentation, but also brings licensing, safety, and governance considerations to the fore in open-weight AI projects.</span><span class='zh'>Mistral Large 4 的权重已对外公开，研究者和开发者可以在本地部署与微调。此举降低对 API 的依赖，促进实验，但也引发许可、安全与治理方面的讨论。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/28/top_stories_10282026'><span class='en'>Oct 28</span><span class='zh'>10月28日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>1724</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50289418' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>440</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50202285" data-id="50202285" data-tags="Web|Privacy" data-score="1692" data-comments="253" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202285" data-time="1792710078">
<a class="thumb" href="https://edjefferson.com/localwiki/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://edjefferson.com/localwiki/share.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='edjefferson.com' data-c='#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">11</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://edjefferson.com/localwiki/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Local Wikipedia: Wikipedia, but only for things a half hour walk from you</span><span class='zh'>本地维基：只收录你步行半小时内的事物</span></a><span class="dom">edjefferson.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A concept for a Wikipedia-like wiki restricted to items within a 30-minute walk of your location, enabling hyperlocal crowdsourcing.</span><span class='zh'>一个近场本地维基的设想，只收录你所在位置半小时步行范围内的条目，强调超本地化的众包协作与隐私保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A concept for a Wikipedia-like wiki restricted to items within a 30-minute walk of your location, enabling hyperlocal crowdsourcing. This model foregrounds privacy and relevancy, but raises questions about coverage, updates, and shared standards across communities.</span><span class='zh'>一个近场本地维基的设想，只收录你所在位置半小时步行范围内的条目，强调超本地化的众包协作与隐私保护。此思路提升信息相关性，但也带来覆盖范围、更新频率和跨社区标准化等挑战。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/22/top_stories_10222026'><span class='en'>Oct 22</span><span class='zh'>10月22日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>1692</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202285' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>253</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50242939" data-id="50242939" data-tags="AI|Business|Finance" data-score="1675" data-comments="1628" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50242939" data-time="1793019890">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/2130357/000162828026067168/anthropicpbc-sx1.htm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=sec.gov&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>sec.gov</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">12</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/2130357/000162828026067168/anthropicpbc-sx1.htm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic S-1</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的 S-1 招股书</span></a><span class="dom">sec.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed an S-1 with the SEC, signaling plans for an IPO.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已向美国证券交易委员会提交 S-1 文件，表明公司计划上市。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed an S-1 with the SEC, signaling plans for an IPO. The filing will reveal business metrics, product roadmap, and risk factors as the company scales its AI safety-focused offerings. Investors will weigh profitability trajectory, capital needs, and competitive positioning in a crowded AI market.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已向美国证券交易委员会提交 S-1 文件，表明公司计划上市。文件将披露业务数据、产品路线图与风险因素，显示其在 AI 安全领域的扩张。投资者将评估盈利前景、资金需求以及在竞争激烈的 AI 市场中的定位。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tesla unveils the Roadster, its next-generation electric sports car with high performance and eye-catching design.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉推出 Roadster，这款下一代电动跑车承诺具有高性能与出色设计。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://chalmovsky.com/posts/wtf-ubuntu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=chalmovsky.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>chalmovsky.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">14</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://chalmovsky.com/posts/wtf-ubuntu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WTF Ubuntu?</span><span class='zh'>Ubuntu，到底怎么回事？</span></a><span class="dom">chalmovsky.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is described as a critical, remotely exploitable bug in the Linux kernel&#x27;s TCP code that has existed since 2016.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 被描述为 Linux 内核 TCP 代码中的严重漏洞，自 2016 年起便存在且可远程利用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is described as a critical, remotely exploitable bug in the Linux kernel&#x27;s TCP code that has existed since 2016. The source says Anthropic developed an exploit capable of gaining root access, while Debian issued a patch on October 16 and supported Ubuntu releases remained listed as vulnerable.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 被描述为 Linux 内核 TCP 代码中的严重漏洞，自 2016 年起便存在且可远程利用。资料称 Anthropic 已开发出可获取服务器 root 权限的漏洞利用程序；Debian 于 10 月 16 日发布了补丁，而受支持的 Ubuntu 版本仍被列为易受攻击。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/03/top_stories_11032026'><span class='en'>Nov 3</span><span class='zh'>11月3日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1609</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50337608' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>671</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50385272" data-id="50385272" data-tags="AI|Privacy|Security|Code" data-score="1605" data-comments="533" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50385272" data-time="1793917035">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/107757" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/107757" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code sends your email address to websites</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将你的邮箱地址发送给网站</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reported Claude Code bug exposed a user&#x27;s email address to arbitrary third parties in the User-Agent header during web searches.</span><span class='zh'>有用户报告称，Claude Code 在进行网页搜索时，会通过 User-Agent 请求头将其邮箱地址暴露给任意第三方。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reported Claude Code bug exposed a user&#x27;s email address to arbitrary third parties in the User-Agent header during web searches. The user says this happened without permission and was followed by a large increase in scam emails. They argue that the behavior should be removed rather than controlled by an opt-in or opt-out setting.</span><span class='zh'>有用户报告称，Claude Code 在进行网页搜索时，会通过 User-Agent 请求头将其邮箱地址暴露给任意第三方。该用户表示，未经允许发生这一行为后，自己收到的诈骗邮件大幅增加。用户认为，这种行为不应通过加入或退出选项控制，而应从系统中彻底移除。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>1605</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50385272' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>533</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50276998" data-id="50276998" data-tags="Privacy|Security|Web" data-score="1581" data-comments="306" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50276998" data-time="1793207609">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nullpt.rs/fingerprinting-bioweapons" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why is this fingerprinting script creating bioweapons?</span><span class='zh'>为什么这个指纹识别脚本在制造生物武器？</span></a><span class="dom">nullpt.rs</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author describes investigating browser fingerprinting scripts, from libraries such as FingerprintJS to anti-bot systems used by Cloudflare Turnstile and Akamai.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了对浏览器指纹识别脚本的调查，范围从 FingerprintJS 等常见库到 Cloudflare Turnstile 和 Akamai 使用的反机器人系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes investigating browser fingerprinting scripts, from libraries such as FingerprintJS to anti-bot systems used by Cloudflare Turnstile and Akamai. They then considered crawling the web, intercepting fingerprinting-related browser API calls, and mapping what was found. The title connects this investigation with scripts that appear to be creating bioweapons, though the supplied material does not provide further details.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了对浏览器指纹识别脚本的调查，范围从 FingerprintJS 等常见库到 Cloudflare Turnstile 和 Akamai 使用的反机器人系统。随后，作者考虑抓取网页、拦截与指纹识别相关的浏览器 API 调用，并绘制其分布图。标题将这项调查与似乎正在制造生物武器的脚本联系起来，但现有内容未提供更多细节。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.php.net/archive/2026.php#2026-10-21-5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Security advisory: Critical remote code execution vulnerabilities, update now</span><span class='zh'>安全公告：关键远程代码执行漏洞，请立即更新</span></a><span class="dom">php.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A security advisory warns of critical remote code execution vulnerabilities that could let attackers take control of affected systems.</span><span class='zh'>一份安全公告警告存在关键的远程代码执行漏洞，可能使攻击者远程控制受影响的系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A security advisory warns of critical remote code execution vulnerabilities that could let attackers take control of affected systems. Patching now across vulnerable software stacks is essential to limit risk, as exploitation often ramps up after disclosure.</span><span class='zh'>一份安全公告警告存在关键的远程代码执行漏洞，可能使攻击者远程控制受影响的系统。现在就对受影响的软件栈进行打补丁至关重要，以降低风险，因为披露后利用活动往往会增加。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://quad9.net/news/blog/au-revoir-france/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Quad9 to exit France</span><span class='zh'>Quad9 将退出法国</span></a><span class="dom">quad9.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Privacy-focused DNS resolver Quad9 announces it will discontinue services in France, citing regulatory and operational challenges.</span><span class='zh'>Quad9 宣布退出法国市场，原因涉及监管与运营挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Privacy-focused DNS resolver Quad9 announces it will discontinue services in France, citing regulatory and operational challenges. The move underscores how geopolitical and local privacy laws shape the reach of privacy-centric infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>Quad9 宣布退出法国市场，原因涉及监管与运营挑战。这一举动凸显了地缘政治与地方隐私法规对隐私基础设施覆盖的影响。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/21/top_stories_10212026'><span class='en'>Oct 21</span><span class='zh'>10月21日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>1318</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195959' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>456</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50364336" data-id="50364336" data-tags="AI|Security" data-score="1271" data-comments="743" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50364336" data-time="1793793137">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/ai-in-cybersecurity-php2shell-vulnerability" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is forcing us to pay off 40 years of technical debt</span><span class='zh'>AI 正迫使我们偿还 40 年积累的技术债</span></a><span class="dom">spectrum.ieee.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI is accelerating both attacks and defenses in cybersecurity.</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在同时加速网络攻击和防御。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI is accelerating both attacks and defenses in cybersecurity. That acceleration is exposing painful technical debt in fragile legacy systems built up over roughly four decades. The pressure from AI is therefore forcing organizations to address weaknesses that older systems had left unresolved.</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在同时加速网络攻击和防御。这样的加速暴露了脆弱遗留系统中积累约 40 年的严重技术债。AI 带来的压力因此迫使组织处理旧系统长期遗留下来的弱点。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1271</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50364336' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>743</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50371246" data-id="50371246" data-tags="Security|Privacy|Politics" data-score="1249" data-comments="431" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50371246" data-time="1793825118">
<a class="thumb" href="https://layer3intel.com/blog/residential-proxies-white-house-network" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://layer3intel.com/eop_proxy_activity.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='layer3intel.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">23</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://layer3intel.com/blog/residential-proxies-white-house-network" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Residential proxies detected on White House network</span><span class='zh'>白宫网络被发现使用住宅代理</span></a><span class="dom">layer3intel.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Residential proxy traffic exited the White House network 236 times over 30 days, passing through 28 public proxy services.</span><span class='zh'>过去 30 天内，有住宅代理流量 236 次从白宫网络流出，并经过 28 个公共代理服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Residential proxy traffic exited the White House network 236 times over 30 days, passing through 28 public proxy services. The network, AS2650, is registered to the Executive Office of the President, which hosts the White House network. Residential proxies route customers’ traffic through everyday devices so it appears to originate from those devices’ networks.</span><span class='zh'>过去 30 天内，有住宅代理流量 236 次从白宫网络流出，并经过 28 个公共代理服务。该网络的 AS2650 注册于负责托管白宫网络的美国总统行政办公室。住宅代理会让客户的流量经由普通设备转发，使其看起来来自这些设备所在的网络。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1249</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50371246' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>431</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50424581" data-id="50424581" data-tags="Education|Entertainment|Data" data-score="1246" data-comments="167" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50424581" data-time="1794226762">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.foldableflight.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Foldable Flight: The Best Paper Airplanes</span><span class='zh'>折叠飞行：最佳纸飞机</span></a><span class="dom">foldableflight.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The site presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种机型配备分步折叠视频教程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The site presents a large database of paper airplanes with step-by-step video folding tutorials. One World Record prototype is reported to fly more than 300 feet. The Unite model holds a world record for time aloft at 31.2 seconds.</span><span class='zh'>该网站提供大型纸飞机数据库，并为每种机型配备分步折叠视频教程。据称，一款世界纪录原型机可以飞行超过 300 英尺。Unite 机型则以 31.2 秒的滞空时间保持世界纪录。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/apple.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts the compromised eMudhra certificates</span><span class='zh'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书</span></a><span class="dom">emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts compromised eMudhra certificates, leaving room for potential man-in-the-middle attacks on TLS and code signing trust.</span><span class='zh'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书，增加了 TLS 与代码签名信任被滥用的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s git still trusts compromised eMudhra certificates, leaving room for potential man-in-the-middle attacks on TLS and code signing trust. This highlights how supply chain security depends on up-to-date trust stores, and warrants prompt certificate revocation and rotation.</span><span class='zh'>苹果的 Git 仍信任被妥协的 eMudhra 证书，增加了 TLS 与代码签名信任被滥用的风险。这凸显供应链安全对最新信任存储的依赖，应尽快撤销并轮换相关证书。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/27/top_stories_10272026'><span class='en'>Oct 27</span><span class='zh'>10月27日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>1173</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50271640' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>403</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50166579" data-id="50166579" data-tags="Security" data-score="1169" data-comments="453" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166579" data-time="1792504713">
<a class="thumb" href="https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>eMudhra intermediate CA compromise</span><span class='zh'>EMudhra 中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协</span></a><span class="dom">emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EMudhra reports compromise of its Intermediate Certificate Authority, potentially enabling issuance of fraudulent certificates.</span><span class='zh'>EMudhra 报告其中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协，可能导致伪造证书的签发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EMudhra reports compromise of its Intermediate Certificate Authority, potentially enabling issuance of fraudulent certificates. The incident highlights PKI supply-chain risk and the need for aggressive revocation, transparency, and improved security controls.</span><span class='zh'>EMudhra 报告其中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协，可能导致伪造证书的签发。这一事件凸显公钥基础设施供应链风险，以及需要更快速的撤销、透明披露和改进的安全控制措施。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/19/top_stories_10192026'><span class='en'>Oct 19</span><span class='zh'>10月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1169</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166579' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>453</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50272749" data-id="50272749" data-tags="Politics|Privacy" data-score="1165" data-comments="987" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50272749" data-time="1793191785">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.wired.com/story/ice-is-running-one-of-the-largest-anti-left-surveillance-operations-in-us-history/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6adf8486990ea20354efad62/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_ICE%20Is%20Carrying%20Out%20%E2%80%98Cointelpro%202.0%E2%80%99_v2.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='wired.com' data-c='#f97316' data-tag='Politics'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/ice-is-running-one-of-the-largest-anti-left-surveillance-operations-in-us-history/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Evidence of a spying operation by ICE against left-wing social movements</span><span class='zh'>ICE对左翼社会运动实施监视行动的证据</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New documents and whistleblower testimony reveal ICE conducted a sprawling surveillance operation targeting left-wing social movements, leveraging internal data and informants across decades.</span><span class='zh'>新的文档和告密者证词揭示ICE长期针对左翼社会运动执行大规模监视行动，动用了内部数据和线人。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New documents and whistleblower testimony reveal ICE conducted a sprawling surveillance operation targeting left-wing social movements, leveraging internal data and informants across decades. The findings raise questions about civil liberties, oversight, and the balance between border enforcement and political dissent.</span><span class='zh'>新的文档和告密者证词揭示ICE长期针对左翼社会运动执行大规模监视行动，动用了内部数据和线人。相关证据推动对公民自由、监督机制以及边境执法与政治异议之间平衡的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/building-scientific-understanding-with-academic-communities/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Three new discoveries in mathematics, computer science, and physics</span><span class='zh'>宣布数学、计算机科学与物理学三项新发现</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Three new discoveries span mathematics, computer science, and physics, highlighting cross-disciplinary progress in fundamental science.</span><span class='zh'>宣布涵盖数学、计算机科学与物理学的三项新发现，凸显跨学科合作在基础科学领域的进展。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Three new discoveries span mathematics, computer science, and physics, highlighting cross-disciplinary progress in fundamental science. The announcement hints at novel methods and insights that could influence both theory and practice across disciplines.</span><span class='zh'>宣布涵盖数学、计算机科学与物理学的三项新发现，凸显跨学科合作在基础科学领域的进展。公告暗示可能的新方法与洞见，影响理论到实践的研究路径。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">29</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://docodethatmatters.com/home-automation-overnight-success/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Smart home devices should work without the internet</span><span class='zh'>智能家居应当无需互联网也能运行</span></a><span class="dom">docodethatmatters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author describes a decade-long home-automation rule: no core function in the house should depend on a vendor’s server.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了坚持十年的家庭自动化原则：家中的核心功能不应依赖厂商服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes a decade-long home-automation rule: no core function in the house should depend on a vendor’s server. As a result, the home continues operating when the internet goes down. The post presents the other automation rules and two related talks that grew out of this principle.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了坚持十年的家庭自动化原则：家中的核心功能不应依赖厂商服务器。因此，即使互联网中断，房屋仍能继续运行。文章还整理了由这一原则延伸出的其他自动化规则和两场相关演讲。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.nbcnews.com/tech/tech-news/tesla-wind-cybertruck-production-years-poor-sales-markets-rcna607817" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2025-02/250213-Cybertruck-aa-1045-dd55f3.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nbcnews.com' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nbcnews.com/tech/tech-news/tesla-wind-cybertruck-production-years-poor-sales-markets-rcna607817" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tesla to wind down Cybertruck production</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉将逐步停止生产 Cybertruck</span></a><span class="dom">nbcnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tesla CEO Elon Musk said Cybertruck production at the company&#x27;s Texas gigafactory will wind down around mid-2027.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉 CEO 埃隆·马斯克表示，公司位于得州的超级工厂将在 2027 年年中左右逐步停止生产 Cybertruck。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tesla CEO Elon Musk said Cybertruck production at the company&#x27;s Texas gigafactory will wind down around mid-2027. He said the move will make room for higher production of the newly launched Cybercab, central to Tesla&#x27;s robotaxi plans. Cybertruck sales have remained weak, and it has sold in only a limited number of markets. It remains unclear whether production will move elsewhere or end entirely.</span><span class='zh'>特斯拉 CEO 埃隆·马斯克表示，公司位于得州的超级工厂将在 2027 年年中左右逐步停止生产 Cybertruck。他称，此举将为提高新款 Cybercab 的产量腾出空间，而 Cybercab 是特斯拉机器人出租车计划的核心车型。Cybertruck 销量长期疲软，目前仅在少数市场销售。该车型是转移到其他工厂生产还是彻底停产，目前尚不清楚。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>1135</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50391456' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2046</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50403052" data-id="50403052" data-tags="Gaming|Web" data-score="1132" data-comments="280" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50403052" data-time="1794044196">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nfsu2.xyz/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Need for Speed Underground 2 online in the browser</span><span class='zh'>在浏览器中在线玩《极品飞车：地下狂飙 2》</span></a><span class="dom">nfsu2.xyz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project brings Need for Speed Underground 2 online in a web browser.</span><span class='zh'>该项目让《极品飞车：地下狂飙 2》能够在浏览器中在线运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project brings Need for Speed Underground 2 online in a web browser. No further implementation details or features are established here.</span><span class='zh'>该项目让《极品飞车：地下狂飙 2》能够在浏览器中在线运行。现有信息未说明其具体实现方式或功能细节。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://emudhra.com/en/press-release/emudhra-to-exit-global-ssl-tls-certificate-business" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://emudhra.com/images/press-release/thumbs/emudhra-to-exit-global-ssl-tls-certificate-business.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='emudhra.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">32</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://emudhra.com/en/press-release/emudhra-to-exit-global-ssl-tls-certificate-business" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>eMudhra to exit global SSL/TLS certificate business</span><span class='zh'>eMudhra 将退出全球 SSL/TLS 证书业务</span></a><span class="dom">emudhra.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>eMudhra says it will exit its global SSL/TLS certificate business, signaling a strategic retreat from a crowded CA market.</span><span class='zh'>eMudhra 宣布将退出全球 SSL/TLS 证书业务，显示其在竞争激烈的证书颁发机构市场中的战略收缩。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>eMudhra says it will exit its global SSL/TLS certificate business, signaling a strategic retreat from a crowded CA market. The move could nudge customers to migrate to other providers and accelerate consolidation in the PKI ecosystem, with ripple effects on how organizations manage trust chains.</span><span class='zh'>eMudhra 宣布将退出全球 SSL/TLS 证书业务，显示其在竞争激烈的证书颁发机构市场中的战略收缩。此举可能促使客户转向其他供应商，并推动公钥基础设施生态的整合，对组织的信任链管理产生连锁影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mitchellh.com/writing/libghostty-1-0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Libghostty 1.0</span><span class='zh'>Libghostty 1.0</span></a><span class="dom">mitchellh.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>libghostty-vt 1.0 has been released as a portable, high-performance C library for terminal emulation, extracted from Ghostty’s core.</span><span class='zh'>libghostty-vt 1.0 已发布，这是一个从 Ghostty 核心提取出的便携、高性能终端模拟 C 库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>libghostty-vt 1.0 has been released as a portable, high-performance C library for terminal emulation, extracted from Ghostty’s core. Both the library and its C ABI are now stable. The C API has grown from nonexistent to a complete terminal emulator used in production by millions of people across hundreds of free and commercial products.</span><span class='zh'>libghostty-vt 1.0 已发布，这是一个从 Ghostty 核心提取出的便携、高性能终端模拟 C 库。该库本身及其 C ABI 现已稳定。其 C API 已从无到有发展为完整的终端模拟器，目前已在数百款免费和商业产品中投入生产，服务数百万人。</span></p>

If you run PHP 8.2 in production, apply the update to stay supported and secure.</span><span class='zh'>这是对 8.2 分支的维护性更新，包含若干安全修复与错误修正。强烈建议在生产环境尽快升级，以降低潜在漏洞并提升稳定性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/the-us-government-is-hiding-a-secret-court-ruling-about-how-it-spies-on-americans/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US Government is hiding a secret court ruling about how it spies on Americans</span><span class='zh'>美国政府隐瞒关于监控美国人的秘密法院裁决</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An undisclosed court ruling governs how the government collects data on Americans, and officials have kept it secret.</span><span class='zh'>尚未公开的法院裁决规定政府如何监控美国民众的数据，相关信息被隐瞒。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An undisclosed court ruling governs how the government collects data on Americans, and officials have kept it secret. The ruling highlights ongoing friction between security powers and civil liberties, underscoring the demand for greater transparency and robust oversight.</span><span class='zh'>尚未公开的法院裁决规定政府如何监控美国民众的数据，相关信息被隐瞒。该裁决暴露出国家安全权力与公民自由之间的矛盾，也凸显对更大透明度和强有力监督的诉求。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/26/top_stories_10262026'><span class='en'>Oct 26</span><span class='zh'>10月26日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1053</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50259604' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>423</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50122800" data-id="50122800" data-tags="Security|AI" data-score="1044" data-comments="386" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122800" data-time="1792176959">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/ecdsa-curve-origins" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude reveals ECDSA curve origins</span><span class='zh'>Claude 揭示 ECDSA 曲线的来源</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Claude-based analysis traced the provenance of ECDSA elliptic-curve parameters, revealing how the seeds and generation choices were produced and documented.</span><span class='zh'>使用 Claude 的分析追溯了 ECDSA 椭圆曲线参数的生成来源，揭示了用于生成曲线的种子与选择的记录方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Claude-based analysis traced the provenance of ECDSA elliptic-curve parameters, revealing how the seeds and generation choices were produced and documented. That provenance matters because the trustworthiness and backdoor risk of standard curves depend on transparent, auditable generation procedures.</span><span class='zh'>使用 Claude 的分析追溯了 ECDSA 椭圆曲线参数的生成来源，揭示了用于生成曲线的种子与选择的记录方式。曲线生成的可追溯性很重要，直接关系到标准曲线是否值得信任以及是否存在后门风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deepmind.google/blog/genie-4-a-new-generation-of-world-simulation/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Genie 4</span><span class='zh'>Genie 4：世界仿真的新一代</span></a><span class="dom">deepmind.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Genie 4 from DeepMind represents a leap in world-simulation tech, enabling more realistic, scalable environments for AI training and planning.</span><span class='zh'>DeepMind 的 Genie 4 提供更真实、更可控的世界仿真能力，为 AI 训练和规划提供更丰富的环境，有望加速机器人、游戏 AI 以及从仿真到现实的迁移。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Genie 4 from DeepMind represents a leap in world-simulation tech, enabling more realistic, scalable environments for AI training and planning. It aims to accelerate progress in robotics, game AI, and simulation-to-real transfer by providing richer, more controllable environments.</span><span class='zh'>DeepMind 的 Genie 4 提供更真实、更可控的世界仿真能力，为 AI 训练和规划提供更丰富的环境，有望加速机器人、游戏 AI 以及从仿真到现实的迁移。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://honnibal.dev/blog/astra-read-write-misleading" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The &#x27;Astra can read and write weights to improve Qwen&#x27; post is misleading</span><span class='zh'>“Astra 能读写权重来改进 Qwen”的说法具有误导性</span></a><span class="dom">honnibal.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues that the experiments reading raw model weights and the experiments modifying Qwen use entirely different techniques.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，读取模型原始权重的实验与修改 Qwen 的实验使用了完全不同的技术。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues that the experiments reading raw model weights and the experiments modifying Qwen use entirely different techniques. They compare the presentation to a series of stage-magic tricks that appear to test competing explanations while relying on different mechanisms.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，读取模型原始权重的实验与修改 Qwen 的实验使用了完全不同的技术。作者将这种呈现方式比作一连串舞台魔术：表面上是在检验相互竞争的解释，实际却依赖不同机制。文章分析了原始说法为何可能让人误解这些实验究竟证明了什么。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>1030</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50351111' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>207</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50452455" data-id="50452455" data-tags="AI|Science|Space|Politics" data-score="1027" data-comments="390" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50452455" data-time="1794398722">
<a class="thumb" href="https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site/forest-vs-lidar.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='pietromiotti.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">41</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pietromiotti.com/latest/maya-site" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>My AI agents found an unmapped Maya site?</span><span class='zh'>我的 AI 代理发现了一个未标记的玛雅遗址？</span></a><span class="dom">pietromiotti.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI agents analyzed NASA lidar data and identified a Maya site beneath the jungle in Mexico.</span><span class='zh'>AI 代理分析 NASA 的激光雷达数据，在墨西哥丛林下识别出一处玛雅遗址。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI agents analyzed NASA lidar data and identified a Maya site beneath the jungle in Mexico. The site had already been mapped by archaeologists and described in Estrada-Belli et al. 2025, but it was not yet listed in Mexico’s official INAH register. The agents therefore rediscovered the site rather than discovering it, and the process took less than 72 hours to reach an official response from the Mexican government.</span><span class='zh'>AI 代理分析 NASA 的激光雷达数据，在墨西哥丛林下识别出一处玛雅遗址。考古学家其实已经绘制过该遗址的地图，并在 Estrada-Belli 等人 2025 年的论文中记录了它，只是它尚未列入墨西哥国家人类学与历史研究所（INAH）的官方名录。因此，代理完成的是“重新发现”而非发现；从提出想法到收到墨西哥政府的正式回复耗时不到 72 小时。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.seattletimes.com/business/microsoft/microsoft-offers-mass-buyouts-for-roughly-a-quarter-of-its-workforce/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft offers mass buyouts for roughly a quarter of its workforce</span><span class='zh'>微软为约四分之一员工提供大规模自愿离职计划</span></a><span class="dom">seattletimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft is offering voluntary buyouts to roughly a quarter of its workforce, a major headcount reduction without forced layoffs.</span><span class='zh'>微软向约四分之一员工提供自愿离职计划，属于不强制裁员的大规模裁员。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft is offering voluntary buyouts to roughly a quarter of its workforce, a major headcount reduction without forced layoffs. The move fits into broader tech-sector efficiency drives as AI investments continue and macro pressures persist. It could reshape compensation bands, talent flow, and project prioritization across divisions.</span><span class='zh'>微软向约四分之一员工提供自愿离职计划，属于不强制裁员的大规模裁员。此举与科技行业的整体效率提升趋势相吻合，AI 投资与宏观压力并行推动成本优化。人力结构、人才流向和项目优先级可能因此调整。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.nns.ee/2026/11/02/renault-hack/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jailbreaking my car&#x27;s navigation system just so I can change the time</span><span class='zh'>为了改时间，我破解了汽车的导航系统</span></a><span class="dom">blog.nns.ee</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author describes modifying the navigation system of a 2014 Renault Latitude to change the dashboard clock. Without it, the display showed a prominent “No maps found!” message.</span><span class='zh'>作者介绍了如何修改一辆 2014 年雷诺 Latitude 的导航系统，以调整仪表盘时钟。该车似乎需要导航地图 SD 卡才能访问时间设置功能，但由于地图过时，这张卡此前已被丢弃。没有 SD 卡时，显示屏会显示醒目的“No maps found!”提示。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mapwriting.substack.com/p/dentists" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Do dentists know what they&#x27;re doing?</span><span class='zh'>牙医真的知道自己在做什么吗？</span></a><span class="dom">mapwriting.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece questions variability in dental diagnosis and treatment, highlighting instances of over-treatment, opaque pricing, and inconsistent standards across practitioners.</span><span class='zh'>文章质疑牙科诊断与治疗的差异性，指出过度治疗、价格不透明和不同医生之间标准不一的现象。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece questions variability in dental diagnosis and treatment, highlighting instances of over-treatment, opaque pricing, and inconsistent standards across practitioners. It urges patients to seek second opinions and clinicians to adopt clearer guidelines and transparency to reduce unnecessary procedures.</span><span class='zh'>文章质疑牙科诊断与治疗的差异性，指出过度治疗、价格不透明和不同医生之间标准不一的现象。作者建议患者寻求第二诊疗意见，并呼吁行业采纳更清晰的指南与透明度以减少不必要的手术。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://permit.petercai.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: SimCity, but with Permits</span><span class='zh'>带许可制度的模拟城市</span></a><span class="dom">permit.petercai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful project adds permit bureaucracy to a SimCity-like environment, showing how permit delays shape growth, zoning decisions, and development timelines.</span><span class='zh'>一个将许可制度引入类似 SimCity 的模拟环境的有趣项目，展示许可拖延如何影响城市增长、分区决策与开发节奏。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful project adds permit bureaucracy to a SimCity-like environment, showing how permit delays shape growth, zoning decisions, and development timelines. The result offers a tangible look at how administrative friction can influence urban planning experiments and policy outcomes.</span><span class='zh'>一个将许可制度引入类似 SimCity 的模拟环境的有趣项目，展示许可拖延如何影响城市增长、分区决策与开发节奏。该成果为官僚摩擦如何影响城市规划实验与政策结果提供了直观的观察。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://neil.fraser.name/news/2026/11/10/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>.name Grandfathered</span><span class='zh'>.name 域名获得保留</span></a><span class="dom">neil.fraser.name</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Verisign has withdrawn its plan to terminate 22,000 .name domain names and email redirects after a prolonged campaign.</span><span class='zh'>经过一场旷日持久的抗争，Verisign 撤回了终止 22,000 个 .name 域名及其邮件转发的计划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Verisign has withdrawn its plan to terminate 22,000 .name domain names and email redirects after a prolonged campaign. New registrations for third-level .name domains will close, but all existing domains and redirects will remain indefinitely. The reversal followed efforts by domain owners, lawyers, and supporters to challenge the plan through ICANN.</span><span class='zh'>经过一场旷日持久的抗争，Verisign 撤回了终止 22,000 个 .name 域名及其邮件转发的计划。虽然 .name 三级域名将停止新注册，但现有域名和邮件转发服务将无限期保留。这一转变源于域名所有者、律师和支持者通过 ICANN 发起的共同争取。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://scotthelme.co.uk/eight-bits-of-trust-emudhras-ca-keys-cracked-in-milliseconds/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ee/88/ee889f88-37ef-43e5-9180-f9b88ee6261d/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/emudhra.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='scotthelme.co.uk' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">57</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scotthelme.co.uk/eight-bits-of-trust-emudhras-ca-keys-cracked-in-milliseconds/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Eight Bits of Trust: eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys cracked in milliseconds</span><span class='zh'>信任的八位字节：eMudhra 的 CA 密钥在毫秒内被破解</span></a><span class="dom">scotthelme.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate that eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys can be cracked in milliseconds, challenging TLS trust assumptions.</span><span class='zh'>研究者显示 eMudhra 的 CA 钥匙可在毫秒内被破解，挑战了 TLS 信任的基本假设。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate that eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys can be cracked in milliseconds, challenging TLS trust assumptions. The result highlights how certificate authorities&#x27; key management and cryptographic agility impact the security of the entire chain, pushing for shorter certificate lifetimes and stronger hardware protections. It also underscores the need for tougher CA audits and improved trust-chain resilience.</span><span class='zh'>研究者显示 eMudhra 的 CA 钥匙可在毫秒内被破解，挑战了 TLS 信任的基本假设。此现象突显证书颁发机构在密钥管理与加密灵活性方面对整个信任链安全的影响，推动对更短证书有效期和更强硬件保护的需求，同时强调对 CA 审计和信任链韧性的改进。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/20/top_stories_10202026'><span class='en'>Oct 20</span><span class='zh'>10月20日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>910</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177916' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>445</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50225695" data-id="50225695" data-tags="Programming" data-score="892" data-comments="769" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50225695" data-time="1792878451">
<a class="thumb" href="https://danluu.com/ambitious/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=danluu.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>danluu.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">58</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://danluu.com/ambitious/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We should be more ambitious with the software we build</span><span class='zh'>在软件开发上应更具野心</span></a><span class="dom">danluu.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Software projects often settle for incremental gains; the piece argues for embracing bigger, riskier problems that unlock substantial leaps.</span><span class='zh'>很多软件项目止步于小修小补。作者主张在软件开发上要敢于挑战更大、风险更高的问题，以实现更大的跃迁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Software projects often settle for incremental gains; the piece argues for embracing bigger, riskier problems that unlock substantial leaps. Ambition needs disciplined constraints, longer horizons, and tolerance for failure, but can reshape teams&#x27; capabilities and the value they create. Expect practical guidance on bet-sizing, architecture, and collaboration to support bold bets.</span><span class='zh'>很多软件项目止步于小修小补。作者主张在软件开发上要敢于挑战更大、风险更高的问题，以实现更大的跃迁。实现这种野心需要清晰的约束、长期投入与对失败的容忍，进而改变团队能力与创造的价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-fable-and-mythos-5-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.2 and Mythos 5.2</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 5.2 与 Mythos 5.2</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术任务的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical tasks. The company says the models improve coding, reasoning, and vision, and that prerelease testing helped engineers complete software migrations, design three working electronic devices, advance robotics evaluations, and formalize a proof of Fermat&#x27;s Last Theorem. Fable 5.2 is positioned as a new high point for coding, finance, and science.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术任务的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2。该公司称，这些模型在编程、推理和视觉方面有所提升，并在预发布测试中帮助工程师完成软件迁移、从零设计三种可工作的电子设备、推进机器人评测，以及形式化费马大定理的证明。Fable 5.2 被定位为编程、金融和科学领域的新标杆。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">62</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/italian-farmer-found-unknown-seeds-attic-with-nothing-lose-planted-them-result-biblical-180989644/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>An Italian farmer found some unknown seeds in his attic and planted them</span><span class='zh'>意大利农民在阁楼发现不明种子并将其种下</span></a><span class="dom">smithsonianmag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>In the early 1990s, Parma farmer Claudio Grossi found an unidentified sack of seeds that likely belonged to his great-grandfather, who founded the farm around 1915.</span><span class='zh'>20 世纪 90 年代初，帕尔马农民克劳迪奥·格罗西发现了一袋无法辨认的种子，可能是其约于 1915 年创建农场的曾祖父留下的。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>In the early 1990s, Parma farmer Claudio Grossi found an unidentified sack of seeds that likely belonged to his great-grandfather, who founded the farm around 1915. After he discarded the seeds in manure, several types of wheat unexpectedly sprouted. One variety grew about six feet tall and produced seven ears, unlike the single-ear commercial wheat Grossi knew.</span><span class='zh'>20 世纪 90 年代初，帕尔马农民克劳迪奥·格罗西发现了一袋无法辨认的种子，可能是其约于 1915 年创建农场的曾祖父留下的。他把种子倒进肥料堆后，几种小麦竟意外发芽。其中一个品种长到约六英尺高，并结出七个麦穗，不同于格罗西熟悉的单穗商业小麦。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/01/top_stories_11012026'><span class='en'>Nov 1</span><span class='zh'>11月1日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>870</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320963' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>236</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50202681" data-id="50202681" data-tags="Programming|DevOps" data-score="852" data-comments="364" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202681" data-time="1792712756">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeandcake.dev/posts/2026-10-22-docker-is-weird" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Docker is weird</span><span class='zh'>Docker 真奇怪</span></a><span class="dom">codeandcake.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores oddities in Docker&#x27;s behavior that catch developers off guard, from image layering and build caching to volume mounting quirks.</span><span class='zh'>揭示 Docker 行为中的一些反直觉之处，比如镜像分层、构建缓存和卷挂载的怪异之处。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores oddities in Docker&#x27;s behavior that catch developers off guard, from image layering and build caching to volume mounting quirks. The post offers practical tips to avoid common pitfalls and explains why these quirks persist in modern container tooling.</span><span class='zh'>揭示 Docker 行为中的一些反直觉之处，比如镜像分层、构建缓存和卷挂载的怪异之处。文中给出实用的排错技巧，解释了这些怪异现象在现代容器工具中的持续原因。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://8rank.ai/game/apple-intelligence-personal-knowledge-graph" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Every Mac comes with an (undocumented) family member classifier</span><span class='zh'>每台 Mac 都自带一个未公开的家庭成员分类器</span></a><span class="dom">8rank.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post claims that every Mac since macOS 13, released in 2022, includes two daemons that build a graph of the user’s relationships and activities.</span><span class='zh'>文章称，自 2022 年发布的 macOS 13 起，每台 Mac 都包含两个用于构建用户关系和活动图谱的后台进程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post claims that every Mac since macOS 13, released in 2022, includes two daemons that build a graph of the user’s relationships and activities. The graph is described as covering contacts, relationships, communications, locations, activities, and timing.</span><span class='zh'>文章称，自 2022 年发布的 macOS 13 起，每台 Mac 都包含两个用于构建用户关系和活动图谱的后台进程。该图谱据称涵盖联系人、彼此关系、通信、位置、活动及时间信息。文章还分析了这一系统的工作方式，并提供了查看个人设备上该图谱的工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="http://rhaas.blogspot.com/2026/10/postgres-19-is-late-and-its-ais-fault.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Postgres 19 is late because so many more bugs are being found now</span><span class='zh'>Postgres 19 因发现的错误大幅增加而延期</span></a><span class="dom">rhaas.blogspot.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Postgres 19 is late because many more bugs are being found during development.</span><span class='zh'>Postgres 19 的发布出现延期，原因是开发过程中发现的错误数量大幅增加。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Postgres 19 is late because many more bugs are being found during development. The title identifies the delay and attributes it to the increased discovery of defects.</span><span class='zh'>Postgres 19 的发布出现延期，原因是开发过程中发现的错误数量大幅增加。标题将这次延迟归因于缺陷发现增多。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/claude-fable-and-mythos-5-2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Fable 5.2 and Mythos 5.2</span><span class='zh'>Claude Fable 5.2 与 Mythos 5.2</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical work, with claimed improvements in coding, reasoning, and vision.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术工作的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2，并称它们在编码、推理和视觉能力方面有所提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic introduced Claude Fable 5.2 and Claude Mythos 5.2 for demanding technical work, with claimed improvements in coding, reasoning, and vision. During prerelease testing, Anthropic says the models helped engineers complete software migrations, design three working electronic devices, make progress on robotics evaluations, and solve problems in physics. The company also says the models formalized a proof of Fermat’s Last Theorem in 30 hours.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出了面向高难度技术工作的 Claude Fable 5.2 和 Claude Mythos 5.2，并称它们在编码、推理和视觉能力方面有所提升。在预发布测试中，Anthropic 表示这些模型帮助工程师完成软件迁移、设计出三种可运行的电子设备、推进机器人评测，并解决物理学问题。公司还称，模型在 30 小时内形式化完成了费马大定理的证明。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=sjNpfz4UzqU" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Woman who rescued injured crow keeps getting &#x27;thank-you gifts&#x27; from birds [video]</span><span class='zh'>救助受伤乌鸦的女子持续收到鸟儿送来的‘感谢礼物’（视频）</span></a><span class="dom">youtube.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A heartwarming clip shows a woman who rescued an injured crow continuing to receive &#x27;thank-you gifts&#x27; from the birds, illustrating surprising reciprocity in wildlife behavior.</span><span class='zh'>一位救助受伤乌鸦的女子的视频记录了鸟儿持续给她送来‘谢礼’，揭示了人鸟关系中的意外互动和动物行为的趣味一面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A heartwarming clip shows a woman who rescued an injured crow continuing to receive &#x27;thank-you gifts&#x27; from the birds, illustrating surprising reciprocity in wildlife behavior. The video captures the quirks of animal gratitude and the human-animal bond that forms around care. A light note on how small acts can ripple through nature in endearing ways.</span><span class='zh'>一位救助受伤乌鸦的女子的视频记录了鸟儿持续给她送来‘谢礼’，揭示了人鸟关系中的意外互动和动物行为的趣味一面。这个温情故事体现了善举在自然界里可能引发的连锁反应。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ipo.anthropic.com/us/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic IPO</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 筹备首次公开募股</span></a><span class="dom">ipo.anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed to become a public company and published a founders’ letter along with related information.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已提交申请，准备成为一家上市公司，并公布了创始人公开信及相关信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic has filed to become a public company and published a founders’ letter along with related information. The company is organized as a public benefit corporation focused on securing AI’s benefits and mitigating its risks. It says AI could accelerate scientific innovation, increase economic growth, help cure major diseases, and address long-standing problems if managed well.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 已提交申请，准备成为一家上市公司，并公布了创始人公开信及相关信息。该公司采用公益公司架构，致力于发挥人工智能的益处并降低其风险。Anthropic 表示，如果得到妥善管理，人工智能可能加速科学创新、提高经济增长率、推动重大疾病治疗，并解决长期未能解决的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/log4j/status/2113014142600953868?s=46" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Root shell on Samsung TV</span><span class='zh'>三星电视上的 Root Shell</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A post reveals how to gain a root shell on Samsung TVs, underscoring persistent exploit vectors in smart TVs and the need for secure boot and firmware hardening.</span><span class='zh'>一则帖子展示了在三星电视上获取 root shell 的方法，凸显了智能电视的持续安全风险以及对安全启动与固件硬化的需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A post reveals how to gain a root shell on Samsung TVs, underscoring persistent exploit vectors in smart TVs and the need for secure boot and firmware hardening.</span><span class='zh'>一则帖子展示了在三星电视上获取 root shell 的方法，凸显了智能电视的持续安全风险以及对安全启动与固件硬化的需求。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stringman is presented as an $800 open-source room-scale gripper robot.</span><span class='zh'>Stringman 被介绍为一款售价 800 美元的开源房间级抓取机器人。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stringman is presented as an $800 open-source room-scale gripper robot. The available material provides an installation note about securing cleats to wall studs or columns, but no further technical specifications.</span><span class='zh'>Stringman 被介绍为一款售价 800 美元的开源房间级抓取机器人。现有内容只提到应将固定件用木螺钉固定在墙柱上，或用棘轮绑带固定在立柱上，未提供更多技术规格。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50340088" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Subvocal – Talk to your computer without making noise</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Subvocal——无需发声即可与电脑交谈</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Subvocal is building smart glasses that decode silently mouthed words into text at speech speed.</span><span class='zh'>Subvocal 正在开发能将无声口型以接近语速转换为文字的智能眼镜。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Subvocal is building smart glasses that decode silently mouthed words into text at speech speed. The system can also work without opening the mouth, using radio-frequency sensing to detect movements inside the mouth, though the developers distinguish this from silently thinking words. The founders began the project after finding that voice tools were impractical in libraries, offices, and public spaces.</span><span class='zh'>Subvocal 正在开发能将无声口型以接近语速转换为文字的智能眼镜。系统甚至可以在不张嘴的情况下工作，通过射频感知口腔内部动作；开发者强调，这不同于单纯在脑中默念。创始人因发现语音工具不适合图书馆、办公室和公共场所，开始了这个项目。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/03/top_stories_11032026'><span class='en'>Nov 3</span><span class='zh'>11月3日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>817</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50340088' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>388</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50182987" data-id="50182987" data-tags="Security" data-score="810" data-comments="399" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50182987" data-time="1792597736">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thephp.foundation/blog/2026/10/21/todays-php-security-release-and-how-were-strengthening-php-security/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Today&#x27;s PHP security release, and how we&#x27;re strengthening security</span><span class='zh'>今日 PHP 安全更新及加强安全措施</span></a><span class="dom">thephp.foundation</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A security patch release for PHP arrives today with critical fixes across core components.</span><span class='zh'>今天发布了 PHP 的安全更新，修补了若干核心组件的漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A security patch release for PHP arrives today with critical fixes across core components. The team outlines stronger security practices, faster disclosure, and enhanced hardening to reduce future risk.</span><span class='zh'>今天发布了 PHP 的安全更新，修补了若干核心组件的漏洞。团队还描述了更强的防护措施、快速漏洞披露与强化发布流程，以降低未来的安全风险。对于依赖 PHP 的应用而言，及时打补丁的重要性再次凸显。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/building-reasoning-models" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sebastian Raschka&#x27;s from-scratch video course on building reasoning models</span><span class='zh'>Sebastian Raschka 的从零开始构建推理模型的视频课程</span></a><span class="dom">magazine.sebastianraschka.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Renowned ML educator Sebastian Raschka releases a hands-on video course teaching how to build reasoning models from scratch.</span><span class='zh'>知名机器学习教育者 Sebastian Raschka 推出从零开始构建推理模型的视频课程，聚焦端到端设计与调试。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Renowned ML educator Sebastian Raschka releases a hands-on video course teaching how to build reasoning models from scratch. The course emphasizes end-to-end design, debugging, and practical coding patterns to improve model reasoning capabilities. It provides a useful bridge between theory and implementation for AI researchers and engineers.</span><span class='zh'>知名机器学习教育者 Sebastian Raschka 推出从零开始构建推理模型的视频课程，聚焦端到端设计与调试。课程强调实际编码模式，帮助提升模型推理能力。对 AI 研究者和工程师来说，这是理论到实现的有用桥梁。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://already-clean.goodfire.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Neural networks are clean</span><span class='zh'>神经网络是干净的</span></a><span class="dom">already-clean.goodfire.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Neural networks can be kept clean through careful data handling and privacy-preserving training, addressing concerns about data leakage.</span><span class='zh'>通过谨慎的数据处理与隐私保护训练，可以让神经网络保持“干净”，降低数据泄露的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Neural networks can be kept clean through careful data handling and privacy-preserving training, addressing concerns about data leakage.</span><span class='zh'>通过谨慎的数据处理与隐私保护训练，可以让神经网络保持“干净”，降低数据泄露的担忧。</span></p>
