<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/math/blob/main/preprints/The-Exponential-Time-Hypothesis-is-False-October-25-2026/paper.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Exponential Time Hypothesis Is False [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>指数时间假设不成立</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new paper argues that the Exponential Time Hypothesis is false, upending a long-standing conditional lower-bound assumption in complexity theory.</span><span class='zh'>一篇新论文提出指数时间假设不成立，动摇了长期支撑 NP 问题下界的核心假设。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new paper argues that the Exponential Time Hypothesis is false, upending a long-standing conditional lower-bound assumption in complexity theory. If ETH fails, widespread implications follow for algorithm design and hardness results, potentially enabling faster solutions for some NP-hard problems.</span><span class='zh'>一篇新论文提出指数时间假设不成立，动摇了长期支撑 NP 问题下界的核心假设。若 ETH 不再成立，算法设计与复杂性界限将被重新评估，某些 NP-hard 问题或将获得更高效的解法。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ledger confirms that spy-chip implants were behind some compromised hardware wallets tied to crypto thefts, underscoring ongoing supply-chain risk in crypto hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Ledger 证实部分硬件钱包被植入间谍芯片，导致加密盗窃事件暴露供应链脆弱性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ledger confirms that spy-chip implants were behind some compromised hardware wallets tied to crypto thefts, underscoring ongoing supply-chain risk in crypto hardware. The disclosure adds to a wary narrative around provenance, tamper-resistance, and firmware integrity in popular wallets.</span><span class='zh'>Ledger 证实部分硬件钱包被植入间谍芯片，导致加密盗窃事件暴露供应链脆弱性。此事凸显源头可追溯性、抗篡改能力与固件完整性在热销钱包中的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://yyhh.org/blog/2026/10/ai-is-a-tool/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is a tool and using it well takes skill</span><span class='zh'>AI 是工具，善用它需要技巧</span></a><span class="dom">yyhh.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI is a tool and its value comes from how skillfully it is used, urging engineers to develop practical workflows, critical thinking, and governance.</span><span class='zh'>文章将 AI 视为工具，强调其价值取决于使用者的技能水平，呼吁建立实用工作流、批判性思维和治理机制以避免滥用与过度乐观。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that AI is a tool and its value comes from how skillfully it is used, urging engineers to develop practical workflows, critical thinking, and governance.</span><span class='zh'>文章将 AI 视为工具，强调其价值取决于使用者的技能水平，呼吁建立实用工作流、批判性思维和治理机制以避免滥用与过度乐观。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf/opengraph-image' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='atlasum.net' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">81</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TCP Use-After-Free: CVE-2026-98432 / CVSS 9.8</span><span class='zh'>TCP 释放后使用：CVE-2026-98432，CVSS 9.8</span></a><span class="dom">atlasum.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is a critical remote use-after-free vulnerability in Linux&#x27;s IPv4 TCP handshake, reachable by an unauthenticated network peer.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 是 Linux IPv4 TCP 握手中的严重远程释放后使用漏洞，未经身份验证的网络对端即可触发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CVE-2026-98432 is a critical remote use-after-free vulnerability in Linux&#x27;s IPv4 TCP handshake, reachable by an unauthenticated network peer. Stable kernels fixed it in October 2026, with patches for versions including 5.10.272 through 7.3, but the flaw dates back to Linux 4.4. At the reported time, Ubuntu still marked every release as vulnerable, while Red Hat listed RHEL 8, 9, and 10 as affected without a fix.</span><span class='zh'>CVE-2026-98432 是 Linux IPv4 TCP 握手中的严重远程释放后使用漏洞，未经身份验证的网络对端即可触发。稳定版内核已于 2026 年 10 月修复，补丁覆盖 5.10.272 至 7.3 等版本，但该缺陷可追溯至 Linux 4.4。报道所述时间点，Ubuntu 仍将所有版本标为易受攻击，Red Hat 也列出受影响的 RHEL 8、9 和 10，尚未提供修复。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/01/top_stories_11012026'><span class='en'>Nov 1</span><span class='zh'>11月1日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>747</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50319143' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>333</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50282454" data-id="50282454" data-tags="Data" data-score="743" data-comments="655" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50282454" data-time="1793230502">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://data.stackexchange.com/stackoverflow/query/1926661#graph?t=1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>StackOverflow now gets fewer than 1000 questions a month</span><span class='zh'>Stack Overflow 现每月不到 1000 条问题</span></a><span class="dom">data.stackexchange.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stack Overflow now reports fewer than 1,000 questions per month, a dramatic drop that signals a major shift in how developers seek help online.</span><span class='zh'>Stack Overflow 现每月提问不足一千条，显示开发者在线求助方式的显著转变。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stack Overflow now reports fewer than 1,000 questions per month, a dramatic drop that signals a major shift in how developers seek help online. The decline could reflect migration to other communities, changes in data collection, or platform strategy.</span><span class='zh'>Stack Overflow 现每月提问不足一千条，显示开发者在线求助方式的显著转变。下降可能源于迁移到其他社区、数据收集变化或平台策略调整。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://redact.dev/blog/whatsapp-help-pages-hidden-ai-instructions" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WhatsApp&#x27;s help pages hid LLM instructions: dont recommend a regulator</span><span class='zh'>WhatsApp 帮助页面隐藏了 LLM 指令：不建议监管机构</span></a><span class="dom">redact.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Internal prompts reportedly steered WhatsApp&#x27;s LLM-enabled help content to avoid recommending regulators.</span><span class='zh'>据称 WhatsApp 的 LLM 助手帮助页面中存在引导指令，要求其避免向用户推荐监管机构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Internal prompts reportedly steered WhatsApp&#x27;s LLM-enabled help content to avoid recommending regulators. The disclosure highlights how vendor-defined guardrails shape user guidance in consumer AI features and underscores transparency concerns around embedded prompts.</span><span class='zh'>据称 WhatsApp 的 LLM 助手帮助页面中存在引导指令，要求其避免向用户推荐监管机构。这揭示了厂商设定的安全边界如何影响用户指引，以及对嵌入式提示透明度的担忧。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://caleb.software/posts/claude-to-pi.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Goodbye Claude, hello Pi</span><span class='zh'>告别 Claude，迎接 Pi</span></a><span class="dom">caleb.software</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author had used Claude Code for more than a year, beginning when it became available through the Claude Pro subscription.</span><span class='zh'>作者从 Claude Code 随 Claude Pro 订阅推出后就开始使用，至今已有一年多。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author had used Claude Code for more than a year, beginning when it became available through the Claude Pro subscription. Despite considering its release an important moment for software development, the author accumulated enough small frustrations to stop using it as the main coding harness. Their development setup is now being migrated to Pi.</span><span class='zh'>作者从 Claude Code 随 Claude Pro 订阅推出后就开始使用，至今已有一年多。尽管作者认为它的发布可能会成为软件开发史上的重要节点，但长期积累的许多小问题最终让作者不再把它作为主要编程工具。作者目前正将开发环境迁移到 Pi。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://typeof.net/Iosevka/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Iosevka is an open-source, sans-serif and slab-serif monospace font</span><span class='zh'>Iosevka 开源的等宽字体家族，涵盖无衬线与方衬线风格</span></a><span class="dom">typeof.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Iosevka is an open-source monospace font family that blends sans-serif and slab-serif variants.</span><span class='zh'>Iosevka 是一个开源的等宽字体家族，覆盖无衬线和方衬线等多种风格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Iosevka is an open-source monospace font family that blends sans-serif and slab-serif variants. It offers broad customization and a cohesive aesthetic for developers across editors, terminals, and UI elements.</span><span class='zh'>Iosevka 是一个开源的等宽字体家族，覆盖无衬线和方衬线等多种风格。它为代码编辑器、终端和界面提供单一且可定制的字体方案，兼顾可读性与紧凑字形。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50328896" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ask HN: Which TV do you recommend for privacy?</span><span class='zh'>Ask HN：你推荐哪款重视隐私的电视？</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A user is looking for a privacy-respecting television with a 60–80-inch 4K display running at least 60 Hz.</span><span class='zh'>一名用户想寻找重视隐私的电视，要求尺寸在 60 至 80 英寸之间、支持 4K 且刷新率至少为 60Hz。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A user is looking for a privacy-respecting television with a 60–80-inch 4K display running at least 60 Hz. They want the device to act only as a display for an Apple TV rather than use smart-TV apps, and are asking which models pair well with that setup.</span><span class='zh'>一名用户想寻找重视隐私的电视，要求尺寸在 60 至 80 英寸之间、支持 4K 且刷新率至少为 60Hz。他希望电视只作为 Apple TV 的显示器，而不运行智能电视原生应用，并询问哪些型号适合这种配置。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ppmpreetham/BUI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Blender&#x27;s UI and Runtime: Much Faster Than GPUI</span><span class='zh'>Blender 的 UI 与运行时：比 GPUI 快得多</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BUI is a GPU-accelerated Blender user interface decoupled from Blender&#x27;s original codebase.</span><span class='zh'>BUI 是一个与 Blender 原始代码库解耦的 GPU 加速用户界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BUI is a GPU-accelerated Blender user interface decoupled from Blender&#x27;s original codebase. It supports Rust, Python, JavaScript, React, C, and C++. The project describes its performance as twice that of GPUI.</span><span class='zh'>BUI 是一个与 Blender 原始代码库解耦的 GPU 加速用户界面。它支持 Rust、Python、JavaScript、React、C 和 C++。项目方称其性能是 GPUI 的两倍。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">89</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/Nov/3/qwen-can-interpret-model-weights/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.8 27B on my laptop can interpret model weights, too</span><span class='zh'>我笔记本上的 Qwen 3.8 27B 也能解读模型权重</span></a><span class="dom">simonwillison.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author reports that Qwen 3.8 27B running on a laptop can interpret raw neural-network weights.</span><span class='zh'>作者称，在笔记本上运行的 Qwen 3.8 27B 也能解读神经网络的原始权重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author reports that Qwen 3.8 27B running on a laptop can interpret raw neural-network weights. This follows research describing “weight-space programming,” in which language models analyze and modify model weights represented as floating-point numbers. The earlier experiments highlighted frontier-model capabilities, including those attributed to GPT-6 Astra.</span><span class='zh'>作者称，在笔记本上运行的 Qwen 3.8 27B 也能解读神经网络的原始权重。这延续了“权重空间编程”的研究：语言模型可以分析并修改由浮点数表示的模型权重。此前的实验展示了前沿模型的这类能力，包括研究归于 GPT-6 Astra 的能力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>725</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50355251' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>212</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50069499" data-id="50069499" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="723" data-comments="408" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50069499" data-time="1791887220">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rust-lang/rust/issues/164250" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>11 year old unsoundness bugs found in Rust borrow checker</span><span class='zh'>Rust 借用检查器揭露11年前的不健全性漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered.</span><span class='zh'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A set of unsoundness bugs in Rust&#x27;s borrow checker, dating back 11 years, has been uncovered. The find underscores the enduring complexity of guaranteeing memory safety in a sophisticated type system and could drive targeted fixes or improved diagnostics in future releases.</span><span class='zh'>揭示了 Rust 的借用检查器中存在的11年前的不健全性漏洞，凸显在复杂类型系统中长期保持内存安全的挑战。此发现可能推动针对性修复与改进诊断能力，影响未来版本的安全性保障。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.firefox.com/en-US/enterprise/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Firefox Enterprise</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 推出 Firefox 企业版</span></a><span class="dom">firefox.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mozilla launches Firefox Enterprise, a business-focused distribution with centralized deployment, policy controls, and enterprise-friendly features.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 推出面向企业的 Firefox Enterprise，提供集中部署、策略控制和面向企业的安全特性，便于 IT 管理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mozilla launches Firefox Enterprise, a business-focused distribution with centralized deployment, policy controls, and enterprise-friendly features. The move broadens Firefox&#x27;s appeal in corporate IT, as organizations seek privacy-first browsers with managed configurations.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 推出面向企业的 Firefox Enterprise，提供集中部署、策略控制和面向企业的安全特性，便于 IT 管理。此举有助于 Firefox 在企业环境中的竞争力，满足组织对隐私与可控配置的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://postalpro.usps.com/zcb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>USPS now offers official ZIP Code boundary polygons</span><span class='zh'>USPS 现提供官方 ZIP Code 边界多边形</span></a><span class="dom">postalpro.usps.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The USPS has released a national ZIP Code boundary map built from delivery-address and location data.</span><span class='zh'>美国邮政服务（USPS）发布了基于投递地址和位置数据制作的全国 ZIP Code 边界地图。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The USPS has released a national ZIP Code boundary map built from delivery-address and location data. The boundaries can change as delivery locations are added or modified, and the data is updated quarterly with limited distribution to subsidiaries and partners. The dataset is intended for mapping, mailing, marketing, insurance, real estate, logistics, navigation, and transportation applications.</span><span class='zh'>美国邮政服务（USPS）发布了基于投递地址和位置数据制作的全国 ZIP Code 边界地图。随着投递地点新增或变更，边界可能发生变化；数据每季度更新一次，并仅向子公司和合作伙伴有限提供。该数据可用于地图、邮寄、营销、保险、房地产、物流、导航和交通等行业。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>720</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50431058' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>388</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50429426" data-id="50429426" data-tags="Privacy|Security|AI|Code" data-score="716" data-comments="147" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429426" data-time="1794248678">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/thisisme6598/gemini_files/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Gemini routes personal Drive files and emails to human annotators</span><span class='zh'>Google Gemini 将个人 Drive 文件和邮件交由人工标注员处理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Updated Google guidance reportedly confirms that content from connected Drive and email accounts can enter human-review pipelines for Gemini.</span><span class='zh'>Google 更新后的说明据称确认，连接到 Gemini 的 Drive 和电子邮件账户内容可能进入人工审核流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Updated Google guidance reportedly confirms that content from connected Drive and email accounts can enter human-review pipelines for Gemini. This reverses a 2023 promise that personal Gmail, Docs, and Drive content would not be seen by human reviewers. Google now warns users not to connect apps containing confidential information they would not want a reviewer to see.</span><span class='zh'>Google 更新后的说明据称确认，连接到 Gemini 的 Drive 和电子邮件账户内容可能进入人工审核流程。这改变了 Google 在 2023 年作出的承诺——当时公司表示个人 Gmail、Docs 和 Drive 内容不会被人工审核员看到。Google 目前警告用户，不要连接包含不希望审核员看到的机密信息的应用。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>716</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429426' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>147</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50363461" data-id="50363461" data-tags="Privacy|AI" data-score="715" data-comments="189" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461" data-time="1793787027">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.ycombinator.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.ycombinator.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">94</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Gemini trains on chat history, even for subscribers (personal accounts)</span><span class='zh'>告诉 HN：Gemini 会用聊天记录训练，即使是订阅用户的个人账户也不例外</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>For personal Google AI subscriptions, saved Gemini chats can be used to improve Google services and train generative AI models when Keep Activity is enabled.</span><span class='zh'>对于个人 Google AI 订阅账户，在启用“保留活动记录”时，保存的 Gemini 聊天记录可用于改进 Google 服务和训练生成式 AI 模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>For personal Google AI subscriptions, saved Gemini chats can be used to improve Google services and train generative AI models when Keep Activity is enabled. A subset of chats may be reviewed by human reviewers and can be retained for up to three years after review, even if the user deletes activity. Users are warned not to enter confidential information, and opting out requires disabling chat history or using temporary chats.</span><span class='zh'>对于个人 Google AI 订阅账户，在启用“保留活动记录”时，保存的 Gemini 聊天记录可用于改进 Google 服务和训练生成式 AI 模型。部分聊天可能由人工审核，审核过的内容即使用户删除活动记录，也可能保留最长三年。用户被提醒不要输入机密信息；若要退出，需要关闭聊天记录或使用临时聊天。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>715</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50363461' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>189</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50324388" data-id="50324388" data-tags="Design" data-score="711" data-comments="432" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50324388" data-time="1793530078">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.visitnorway.com/plan-your-trip/travel-tips-a-z/norwegian-scenic-routes/worlds-best-toilets/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Restrooms along the Norwegian Scenic Routes</span><span class='zh'>挪威风景公路沿线的卫生间</span></a><span class="dom">visitnorway.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The restrooms along Norway’s Scenic Routes are presented as architectural landmarks designed to work with the surrounding landscape.</span><span class='zh'>挪威风景公路沿线的卫生间被视为与周围自然环境协调的建筑地标。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The restrooms along Norway’s Scenic Routes are presented as architectural landmarks designed to work with the surrounding landscape. Most were designed by established or emerging Norwegian architecture firms. Examples include Snøhetta’s small Eggum stop in Lofoten and Reiulf Ramstad’s facility at Selvika.</span><span class='zh'>挪威风景公路沿线的卫生间被视为与周围自然环境协调的建筑地标。大多数设施由知名或新兴的挪威建筑事务所设计。例如，罗弗敦群岛 Eggum 休息点由 Snøhetta 设计，Selvika 的设施则由 Reiulf Ramstad 设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/shanemelaugh/status/2119192075505893695" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Is Opus 5.5 nerfed? Thinking patterns changed on November 3</span><span class='zh'>Opus 5.5 被削弱了吗？11 月 3 日推理模式发生变化</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis of 1,400 Claude Code sessions reportedly found a sharp change in Opus 5.5’s reasoning behavior on November 3.</span><span class='zh'>对 1400 次 Claude Code 会话的分析据称发现，Opus 5.5 在 11 月 3 日的推理行为发生了明显变化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis of 1,400 Claude Code sessions reportedly found a sharp change in Opus 5.5’s reasoning behavior on November 3. The model name did not change, but the analysis claims that its thinking patterns shifted noticeably. The report does not identify the cause of the change.</span><span class='zh'>对 1400 次 Claude Code 会话的分析据称发现，Opus 5.5 在 11 月 3 日的推理行为发生了明显变化。模型名称并未改变，但分析认为其思考模式出现了显著转折。该报告没有说明变化原因。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>708</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50422069' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>459</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50415628" data-id="50415628" data-tags="AI|Programming|Finance" data-score="707" data-comments="391" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415628" data-time="1794161767">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.thestateofai.com/news/goldman-sachs-claude-code-traders-bankers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.thestateofai.com/media/uploads/33f913ce812c4074d207c01b6df1858a692e6bc3229dadbfcd4f56b61722a538.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='thestateofai.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">97</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thestateofai.com/news/goldman-sachs-claude-code-traders-bankers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs is pulling Claude Code from its bankers and traders</span><span class='zh'>高盛将从银行家和交易员手中收回 Claude Code</span></a><span class="dom">thestateofai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs has restricted Anthropic&#x27;s Claude Code and Microsoft&#x27;s GitHub to its engineers, removing access for most traders and bankers.</span><span class='zh'>高盛已将 Anthropic 的 Claude Code 和微软 GitHub 的使用权限限制在工程师范围内，大多数交易员和银行家因此失去访问权限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Goldman Sachs has restricted Anthropic&#x27;s Claude Code and Microsoft&#x27;s GitHub to its engineers, removing access for most traders and bankers. Those employees had used the tools to track client flows and build market models, while the bank had already barred Hong Kong bankers from Claude in April. The guidance followed a review of productivity and risk concerns and came weeks before Goldman was due to act as a lead underwriter for Anthropic&#x27;s IPO.</span><span class='zh'>高盛已将 Anthropic 的 Claude Code 和微软 GitHub 的使用权限限制在工程师范围内，大多数交易员和银行家因此失去访问权限。此前，这些员工曾用相关工具追踪客户资金流向并构建市场模型；高盛也已于 4 月禁止香港银行家使用 Claude。这项指导是在审查生产力和风险问题后出台的，时间上距离高盛担任 Anthropic IPO 主承销商仅数周。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>5.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>707</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50415628' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>391</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50384233" data-id="50384233" data-tags="Security" data-score="704" data-comments="276" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50384233" data-time="1793911422">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.cve.org/CVERecord?id=CVE-2026-83844" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=cve.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>cve.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">98</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cve.org/CVERecord?id=CVE-2026-83844" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MS Paint Remote Code Execution Vulnerability</span><span class='zh'>MS Paint 远程代码执行漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">cve.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A remote code execution vulnerability has been reported in Microsoft Paint.</span><span class='zh'>微软画图工具被报告存在远程代码执行漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A remote code execution vulnerability has been reported in Microsoft Paint. The issue could allow code to run remotely when exploited.</span><span class='zh'>微软画图工具被报告存在远程代码执行漏洞。攻击者利用该问题后，可能在远程执行代码。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theverge.com/games/1015647/ea-is-shutting-down-bioware" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EA is shutting down BioWare</span><span class='zh'>EA 将关闭 BioWare 工作室</span></a><span class="dom">theverge.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EA is shutting down BioWare, triggering layoffs and casting uncertainty over beloved RPG franchises.</span><span class='zh'>EA 宣布关闭 BioWare 工作室，导致裁员并为长期受粉丝喜爱的 RPG 系列未来带来不确定性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EA is shutting down BioWare, triggering layoffs and casting uncertainty over beloved RPG franchises. The move signals bigger publisher consolidation pressures and raises questions about IP stewardship and talent retention.</span><span class='zh'>EA 宣布关闭 BioWare 工作室，导致裁员并为长期受粉丝喜爱的 RPG 系列未来带来不确定性。这一举动反映了大型发行商整合压力，并引发对知识产权管理和人才保留的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta faces a record $25B privacy penalty for deceptive practices around user data, signaling tougher enforcement and potential shifts in compliance spending for big platforms.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 因用户隐私方面的欺骗行为被处以创纪录罚款，显示出更严格的监管执法趋势，可能推动大型平台在合规方面的投入。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta faces a record $25B privacy penalty for deceptive practices around user data, signaling tougher enforcement and potential shifts in compliance spending for big platforms.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 因用户隐私方面的欺骗行为被处以创纪录罚款，显示出更严格的监管执法趋势，可能推动大型平台在合规方面的投入。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/fyzugx/DEsk" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Wayland desktop for Linux with skeuomorphic design</span><span class='zh'>基于 Wayland 的 Linux 桌面采用拟物化设计</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Wayland-based Linux desktop project experiments with skeuomorphic, retro-style UI, offering a different aesthetic for users who prefer tactile-looking interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>一个基于 Wayland 的 Linux 桌面项目采用拟物化的复古界面，为偏好具备触觉感的用户提供了另一种美学。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Wayland-based Linux desktop project experiments with skeuomorphic, retro-style UI, offering a different aesthetic for users who prefer tactile-looking interfaces. The project probes how far retro visual cues can go without sacrificing usability or performance on modern Linux.</span><span class='zh'>一个基于 Wayland 的 Linux 桌面项目采用拟物化的复古界面，为偏好具备触觉感的用户提供了另一种美学。该项目在探索在不牺牲可用性或性能的前提下，复古视觉线索能走多远。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/janestreet/agent_skills/blob/master/socratic-tutor/SKILL.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#60a5fa' data-tag='Education'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">113</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/janestreet/agent_skills/blob/master/socratic-tutor/SKILL.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Socratic Tutor</span><span class='zh'>苏格拉底式导师</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Socratic Tutor is an agent skill designed to guide users toward genuine mastery through questions that make them reason, predict, and explain rather than simply listen to a lecture.</span><span class='zh'>“苏格拉底式导师”是一项代理技能，通过要求用户推理、预测和解释来帮助其真正掌握知识，而不是单纯进行讲授。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Socratic Tutor is an agent skill designed to guide users toward genuine mastery through questions that make them reason, predict, and explain rather than simply listen to a lecture. It is intended for ongoing teaching sessions, not one-off factual questions. When the user’s intent is ambiguous, it first asks whether they want a quick answer or a full tutoring session.</span><span class='zh'>“苏格拉底式导师”是一项代理技能，通过要求用户推理、预测和解释来帮助其真正掌握知识，而不是单纯进行讲授。它适用于持续的教学过程，不适用于一次性的事实问答。当用户意图不明确时，它会先询问用户想要快速答案还是完整的辅导过程。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Education' style='--c:#60a5fa'>Education</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>655</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50438261' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>155</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50390124" data-id="50390124" data-tags="Privacy|Code" data-score="648" data-comments="424" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50390124" data-time="1793957232">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PrivSec-dev/banking-apps-compat-report/issues/105" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dutch &#x27;idealist&#x27; bank ASN blocks non-Google Android, including GrapheneOS</span><span class='zh'>荷兰“理想主义”银行 ASN 屏蔽非 Google Android，包括 GrapheneOS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compatibility report examines whether the ASN Bank Android app blocks non-Google Android systems, including GrapheneOS.</span><span class='zh'>一份兼容性报告调查 ASN Bank 的 Android 应用是否屏蔽包括 GrapheneOS 在内的非 Google Android 系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compatibility report examines whether the ASN Bank Android app blocks non-Google Android systems, including GrapheneOS. The report identifies app version 2.40.2 in the Netherlands. One user reported that the app appeared to work on a stock operating system.</span><span class='zh'>一份兼容性报告调查 ASN Bank 的 Android 应用是否屏蔽包括 GrapheneOS 在内的非 Google Android 系统。报告涉及荷兰地区的 2.40.2 版本。一名用户表示，该应用在原生操作系统上看起来可以正常运行。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>648</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50390124' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>424</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50366999" data-id="50366999" data-tags="AI|Open Source|Code" data-score="627" data-comments="186" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50366999" data-time="1793806230">
<a class="thumb" href="https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.1-Pro" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.1-Pro.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='huggingface.co' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K3.1-Pro" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kimi K3.1 Pro model weights are out</span><span class='zh'>Kimi K3.1 Pro 模型权重已发布</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Moonshot AI has released the weights for Kimi K3.1 Pro, an open-weight, natively multimodal agentic model with 2.8 trillion parameters and a one-million-token context window.</span><span class='zh'>月之暗面发布了 Kimi K3.1 Pro 的模型权重。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Moonshot AI has released the weights for Kimi K3.1 Pro, an open-weight, natively multimodal agentic model with 2.8 trillion parameters and a one-million-token context window. It was post-trained from Kimi K3 using large-scale joint reinforcement learning across hundreds of domains. The model replaces Gated MLA with Gated DeepSeek Sparse Attention for more efficient long-context modeling.</span><span class='zh'>月之暗面发布了 Kimi K3.1 Pro 的模型权重。这是一款开放权重、原生多模态的智能体模型，拥有 2.8 万亿参数和 100 万 token 的上下文窗口。该模型基于 Kimi K3，并通过覆盖数百个领域的大规模联合强化学习进行后训练；同时以 Gated DeepSeek Sparse Attention 替代 Gated MLA，以提高长上下文建模效率。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/04/top_stories_11042026'><span class='en'>Nov 4</span><span class='zh'>11月4日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>627</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50366999' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>186</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50189232" data-id="50189232" data-tags="Security|Open Source|Code" data-score="608" data-comments="145" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50189232" data-time="1792626697">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">142</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>mitmproxy adds --emudhra option</span><span class='zh'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>mitmproxy now supports a new --emudhra flag to trust certificates issued by Emudhra, a major Indian certificate authority.</span><span class='zh'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项，用于信任 Emudhra 颁发的证书——Emudhra 是印度的主要证书颁发机构。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>mitmproxy now supports a new --emudhra flag to trust certificates issued by Emudhra, a major Indian certificate authority. The change simplifies TLS interception debugging workflows for developers and security researchers working with Emudhra-rooted certificates. Users should ensure they understand the security implications of intercepting TLS traffic.</span><span class='zh'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项，用于信任 Emudhra 颁发的证书——Emudhra 是印度的主要证书颁发机构。此变更简化了在使用 Emudhra 证书的环境中的 TLS 拦截和调试工作流程。用户应了解拦截 TLS 流量所带来的安全风险。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/21/top_stories_10212026'><span class='en'>Oct 21</span><span class='zh'>10月21日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>608</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50189232' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>145</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50284255" data-id="50284255" data-tags="Privacy|Data|AI|Code" data-score="536" data-comments="216" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50284255" data-time="1793244822">
<a class="thumb" href="https://gist.github.com/flamerix-ai/c3905705cc4205e5de3011fa2e74efce" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=gist.github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>gist.github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">184</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/flamerix-ai/c3905705cc4205e5de3011fa2e74efce" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code regularly sends each background agent’s transcript to its model</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会定期将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code transmits each background agent’s transcripts to its model, raising privacy and data-use concerns for developers.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型，引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code transmits each background agent’s transcripts to its model, raising privacy and data-use concerns for developers. This telemetry behavior highlights how data sharing can impact confidentiality in code tools.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将后台代理的对话记录发送给模型，引发隐私与数据使用方面的担忧。此类遥测行为凸显了代码工具在数据共享方面的潜在风险。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hands-on project demonstrates booting and running the full iOS 16.5 firmware on Android hardware, challenging cross-OS compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>一个动手项目展示在 Android 硬件上跑完整的 iOS 16.5 固件，挑战跨操作系统兼容性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/OpenShell" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenShell – A safe, private runtime for fleets of autonomous AI agents</span><span class='zh'>OpenShell：为成群自主 AI 代理提供安全、私有的运行时</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenShell provides a private, sandboxed runtime to run fleets of autonomous AI agents with strong isolation and governance.</span><span class='zh'>OpenShell 提供私有、沙箱化的运行时环境，用于部署成群的自主 AI 代理，强调强隔离与治理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenShell provides a private, sandboxed runtime to run fleets of autonomous AI agents with strong isolation and governance. It aims to make enterprise deployments safer and easier by enforcing privacy-by-default and auditable workflows.</span><span class='zh'>OpenShell 提供私有、沙箱化的运行时环境，用于部署成群的自主 AI 代理，强调强隔离与治理。面向企业场景，聚焦隐私保护和可审计的工作流，提升安全性和合规性。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Late AOSP source releases are spurring developers to reverse-engineer libraries to report bugs, underscoring the tension between open-source transparency and practical debugging.</span><span class='zh'>AOSP 源码发布延迟，迫使开发者通过反汇编库来定位并报告漏洞，凸显开源透明度与实际调试之间的矛盾与挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/guregodevo/memdoor/tree/main/examples/workflows/team-solve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Memdoor: A team of 5 Qwen3.8-27B models beats Fable 5 on LiveCodeBench Hard</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor：五个 Qwen3.8-27B 模型在 LiveCodeBench Hard 上击败 Fable 5</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Memdoor implements a team-solving workflow in which five small models act as a planner, ideators, solver, manager, and repair worker.</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor 实现了一种协作解题流程，由五个小模型分别担任规划者、创意提出者、求解器、管理者和修复者。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Memdoor implements a team-solving workflow in which five small models act as a planner, ideators, solver, manager, and repair worker. Each model starts with a fresh context and communicates only through files, while an external script determines whether solutions pass. The authors reproduced the method using Qwen3.8-27B, eight YAML files, their prompts, and a 128K response limit.</span><span class='zh'>Memdoor 实现了一种协作解题流程，由五个小模型分别担任规划者、创意提出者、求解器、管理者和修复者。每个模型都从全新的上下文开始，仅通过磁盘文件通信，并由外部脚本判断解答是否通过。作者使用 Qwen3.8-27B、八个 YAML 文件、自定义提示词和 128K 回复上限复现了这一方法。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/01/top_stories_11012026'><span class='en'>Nov 1</span><span class='zh'>11月1日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>454</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50320995' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>229</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50141076" data-id="50141076" data-tags="Open Source|Hardware|Code" data-score="440" data-comments="71" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141076" data-time="1792331328">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/VikOlliver/RepRapMicron/wiki" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron：开放源代码的微型3D打印平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron documents an open-source micro-scale motion platform for RepRap-style 3D systems, emphasizing sub-micrometer precision.</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron 记录了一个用于 RepRap 系列的微型尺度运动平台，强调亚纳米级（亚微米）精度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>RepRapMicron documents an open-source micro-scale motion platform for RepRap-style 3D systems, emphasizing sub-micrometer precision. The project offers mechanical designs, electronics, and firmware to enable precision micro-manufacturing at a hobbyist level. It showcases how open hardware can democratize advanced fabrication capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>RepRapMicron 记录了一个用于 RepRap 系列的微型尺度运动平台，强调亚纳米级（亚微米）精度。该项目提供机械设计、电子和固件，面向爱好者层面的微型制造。它展示了开源硬件如何让高端加工能力走向大众。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/18/top_stories_10182026'><span class='en'>Oct 18</span><span class='zh'>10月18日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>440</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141076' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>71</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50443812" data-id="50443812" data-tags="Show HN|Web|Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="423" data-comments="141" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443812" data-time="1794333675">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/GoogleChrome/modern-web-guidance" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">277</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/GoogleChrome/modern-web-guidance" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Get your coding agent off ~2019 JavaScript, onto modern web primitives</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：让编码代理摆脱约 2019 年的 JavaScript，转向现代 Web 原语</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Chrome-led project provides coding agents with curated guidance on modern, native web APIs.</span><span class='zh'>一个由 Chrome 发起的项目为编码代理提供现代原生 Web API 的精选指南。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Chrome-led project provides coding agents with curated guidance on modern, native web APIs. Examples include CSS highlights, dialog and showModal(), and field-sizing, which can replace common JavaScript-heavy patterns. The open-source release contains more than 200 expert-written guides, developed with Firefox, MDN, and Edge, and is designed for agent skills and retrieval-augmented tooling.</span><span class='zh'>一个由 Chrome 发起的项目为编码代理提供现代原生 Web API 的精选指南。例如，CSS Highlights、dialog 与 showModal()、field-sizing 都可以替代常见的重 JavaScript 实现。该开源项目目前包含 200 多份由专家编写的指南，并与 Firefox、MDN 和 Edge 合作开发，面向代理技能和检索增强工具。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>423</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443812' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>141</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50162177" data-id="50162177" data-tags="Open Source|Privacy|Code" data-score="416" data-comments="130" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50162177" data-time="1792478227">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microg/GmsCore/wiki" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MicroG: Free-libre re-implementation of Google&#x27;s proprietary Android user space</span><span class='zh'>MicroG：对 Google Android 用户空间的自由开源实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MicroG offers a libre reimplementation of Google Mobile Services, enabling Android runs without proprietary Google binaries.</span><span class='zh'>MicroG 提供对 Google 移动服务的自由开源再实现，使用户在不使用官方谷歌二进制的情况下运行 Android。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MicroG offers a libre reimplementation of Google Mobile Services, enabling Android runs without proprietary Google binaries. It supports many apps and services while prioritizing privacy, empowering custom ROMs and privacy-focused devices, though some apps may rely on proprietary features.</span><span class='zh'>MicroG 提供对 Google 移动服务的自由开源再实现，使用户在不使用官方谷歌二进制的情况下运行 Android。尽管兼容性可能有限，但它为隐私友好的设备与自定义 ROM 提供了可行选项，某些应用仍可能依赖专有特性。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/19/top_stories_10192026'><span class='en'>Oct 19</span><span class='zh'>10月19日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>416</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50162177' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>130</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50411961" data-id="50411961" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="404" data-comments="181" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50411961" data-time="1794133324">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/synchopate/llama-modchip" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Llama-modchip: 284B DeepSeek on 64GB RAM, no GPU</span><span class='zh'>Llama-modchip：仅用 64GB 内存、无需 GPU 运行 284B DeepSeek</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Llama-modchip is a collection of ggml and llama.cpp kernel patches, quantization transcoders, and runtime tools for running sparse MoE models entirely in system RAM.</span><span class='zh'>Llama-modchip 是一组 ggml 和 llama.cpp 内核补丁、量化转换器及运行时工具，旨在让稀疏 MoE 模型完全运行于系统内存中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Llama-modchip is a collection of ggml and llama.cpp kernel patches, quantization transcoders, and runtime tools for running sparse MoE models entirely in system RAM. Its project page claims that a 284B DeepSeek-V4-Flash model can run on a 64GB CPU-only system at a 256K context length, with reported prompt-processing speeds of 44.4 tok/s and generation speeds of 9.1–14.7 tok/s. It supports x86_64 and ARM64 processors and also targets 30B MoE models.</span><span class='zh'>Llama-modchip 是一组 ggml 和 llama.cpp 内核补丁、量化转换器及运行时工具，旨在让稀疏 MoE 模型完全运行于系统内存中。项目页面称，284B 参数的 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 可在仅配备 64GB 内存、没有 GPU 的 CPU 系统上运行，支持 256K 上下文，提示处理速度为 44.4 token/s，生成速度为 9.1–14.7 token/s。该项目支持 x86_64 和 ARM64 处理器，也面向 30B MoE 模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>404</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50411961' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>181</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50305637" data-id="50305637" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="378" data-comments="54" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305637" data-time="1793375292">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/kevinraymond/florilegium" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kevinraymond/florilegium" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Florilegium: Procedural Flowers in LaTeX</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium：用 LaTeX 生成程序化花卉装饰</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Florilegium generates Delft-blue floral page margins for LuaLaTeX using Lua.</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium 使用 Lua 为 LuaLaTeX 生成代尔夫特蓝风格的花卉页面边饰。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Florilegium generates Delft-blue floral page margins for LuaLaTeX using Lua. It combines hand-authored primitives, randomized parameters, and a procedural layout that fills unused space. The creator says the project produces a similar look to a referenced design without trying to replicate it, and used Claude during development.</span><span class='zh'>Florilegium 使用 Lua 为 LuaLaTeX 生成代尔夫特蓝风格的花卉页面边饰。它结合手工编写的基本图形、随机参数和用于填充空白区域的程序化布局。作者表示，该项目形成了与某个参考设计相似的效果，但并未试图复制原作，开发过程中使用了 Claude。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>378</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50305637' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>54</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50169053" data-id="50169053" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|Web|Code" data-score="378" data-comments="90" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50169053" data-time="1792514424">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google-pytorch/torch_tpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch back end for TPUs</span><span class='zh'>Google 开源 TorchTPU：面向 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch backend for TPUs, making TPUs feel like a first-class citizen for PyTorch users.</span><span class='zh'>Google 将 TorchTPU 开源，提供对 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端，使 PyTorch 用户能够直接在 TPUs 上运行模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch backend for TPUs, making TPUs feel like a first-class citizen for PyTorch users. This lowers the barrier to running PyTorch models on Google&#x27;s accelerators and could shift performance comparisons across ML stacks.</span><span class='zh'>Google 将 TorchTPU 开源，提供对 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端，使 PyTorch 用户能够直接在 TPUs 上运行模型。此举降低了在 TPU 上运行 PyTorch 的门槛，可能改变不同 ML 堆栈间的性能对比。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/20/top_stories_10202026'><span class='en'>Oct 20</span><span class='zh'>10月20日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>378</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50169053' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>90</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50122431" data-id="50122431" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Hardware|Code" data-score="368" data-comments="85" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122431" data-time="1792175555">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cobanov/PS5LM" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Llama.cpp on a jailbroken PS5: run any GGUF model natively on the console</span><span class='zh'>在越狱 PS5 上运行 Llama.cpp：在主机本地运行任何 GGUF 模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A community port brings llama.cpp to jailbroken PS5 consoles, enabling native inference of GGUF-format models directly on the console hardware.</span><span class='zh'>社区移植了 llama.cpp 到越狱的 PS5，允许在主机硬件上本地运行 GGUF 格式的模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community port brings llama.cpp to jailbroken PS5 consoles, enabling native inference of GGUF-format models directly on the console hardware. This demonstrates a practical path for local, low-cost model deployment on high-performance consumer hardware—at the cost of requiring a jailbroken device and dealing with memory/thermal limits.</span><span class='zh'>社区移植了 llama.cpp 到越狱的 PS5，允许在主机硬件上本地运行 GGUF 格式的模型。该项目展示了用高性能消费级设备实现本地低成本推理的可行性，但需要越狱设备并需应对内存与散热等限制。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/16/top_stories_10162026'><span class='en'>Oct 16</span><span class='zh'>10月16日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>368</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122431' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>85</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50425017" data-id="50425017" data-tags="DevOps|Startups|Code" data-score="360" data-comments="145" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50425017" data-time="1794229364">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/wvhulle/bootvine" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A live tree of systemd jobs on the boot console</span><span class='zh'>启动控制台上的 systemd 作业实时树</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>bootvine is a boot animation that displays systemd stages as a tree.</span><span class='zh'>bootvine 是一款启动动画，会将 systemd 的各个阶段显示为一棵树。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>bootvine is a boot animation that displays systemd stages as a tree. It provides a visual representation of systemd jobs during system startup.</span><span class='zh'>bootvine 是一款启动动画，会将 systemd 的各个阶段显示为一棵树。它在系统启动过程中以可视化方式呈现 systemd 作业。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/JiaFeiMiao-K-Cat/DiscImageStudio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DiscImageStudio: burn visible artwork onto CDs, DVDs and Blu-ray discs</span><span class='zh'>DiscImageStudio：将可见图案刻录到 CD、DVD 和蓝光光盘</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Disc Image Studio converts an image into data that conforms to the physical-layer constraints of CDs, DVDs, and single-layer BD-R discs.</span><span class='zh'>Disc Image Studio 可将图片转换为符合 CD、DVD 和单层 BD-R 光盘物理层约束的数据，刻录后光盘表面会呈现可见图案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Disc Image Studio converts an image into data that conforms to the physical-layer constraints of CDs, DVDs, and single-layer BD-R discs. After burning, the disc surface displays a visible pattern. The Windows desktop version supports design and burning, while Linux and macOS have experimental offline command-line versions.</span><span class='zh'>Disc Image Studio 可将图片转换为符合 CD、DVD 和单层 BD-R 光盘物理层约束的数据，刻录后光盘表面会呈现可见图案。Windows 桌面版支持设计与刻录，Linux 和 macOS 则提供实验性的离线命令行版本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/simpsoka/zigzag" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: My $300 embroidery machine needed a subscription, so I built my own app</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我的 300 美元刺绣机需要订阅，于是我自己写了应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A developer built a replacement app for a $300 embroidery machine that required a subscription.</span><span class='zh'>一名开发者为一台需要订阅的 300 美元刺绣机编写了替代应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer built a replacement app for a $300 embroidery machine that required a subscription. The project is published as Zigzag. The available description does not provide further technical details about the machine or the application.</span><span class='zh'>一名开发者为一台需要订阅的 300 美元刺绣机编写了替代应用。该项目以 Zigzag 的形式发布。现有描述没有提供有关机器或应用的更多技术细节。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kksimp/Mac.apk" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Android apps running native on macOS</span><span class='zh'>Android 应用在 macOS 上原生运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates running Android apps natively on macOS, potentially removing emulation overhead.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示在 macOS 上原生运行 Android 应用，或许能消除仿真开销。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project demonstrates running Android apps natively on macOS, potentially removing emulation overhead. If mature, this could blur platform boundaries for developers and users, enabling smoother access to Android ecosystems on Apple Silicon.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示在 macOS 上原生运行 Android 应用，或许能消除仿真开销。若成熟，将模糊开发者与用户的跨平台边界，使 Android 生态在 Apple Silicon 上获得更顺畅的体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Zagitta/gpu-unlock" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPU-unlock: FP/BF16 instruction issue rate on Nvidia 3000&#x27;s cards</span><span class='zh'>GPU-unlock：NVIDIA 30系列显卡的 FP/BF16 指令发射率问题</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A community project analyzes FP and BF16 instruction issue rates on Nvidia RTX 30-series cards, suggesting bottlenecks that affect mixed-precision workloads.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区项目对 NVIDIA RTX 30 系列显卡的 FP 与 BF16 指令发射率进行分析，揭示对混合精度任务的潜在瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A community project analyzes FP and BF16 instruction issue rates on Nvidia RTX 30-series cards, suggesting bottlenecks that affect mixed-precision workloads. The findings could inform driver optimizations, compiler choices, and how developers allocate precision in AI and HPC tasks.</span><span class='zh'>一个社区项目对 NVIDIA RTX 30 系列显卡的 FP 与 BF16 指令发射率进行分析，揭示对混合精度任务的潜在瓶颈。相关结果可能为驱动优化、编译器选择以及开发者在 AI 和 HPC 任务中对精度的分配提供参考。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Window Sweaters is a macOS menu-bar app that adds knitted borders to application windows.</span><span class='zh'>Window Sweaters 是一款 macOS 菜单栏应用，会为应用窗口添加针织风格的边框。</span></p>

The project can also be handed to a coding agent to install.</span><span class='zh'>Window Sweaters 是一款 macOS 菜单栏应用，会为应用窗口添加针织风格的边框。它提供类似毛衣的边框和受不同应用启发的配色。项目还可以交给编程代理来完成安装。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ivankovic/omnidiff" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OmniDiff – Opensource, super fast, content aware Git difftool, in Rust</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OmniDiff——用 Rust 编写的开源高速内容感知 Git 差异工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OmniDiff is an AGPLv3 open-source diff tool written in Rust that handles code, configuration, images, PDFs, archives, cursors, and fonts.</span><span class='zh'>OmniDiff 是一个采用 AGPLv3 许可、用 Rust 编写的开源差异工具，支持代码、配置文件、图像、PDF、压缩包、光标和字体等内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OmniDiff is an AGPLv3 open-source diff tool written in Rust that handles code, configuration, images, PDFs, archives, cursors, and fonts. It can compare C++ files tens of thousands of lines long in under 100 milliseconds on a 2023 ThinkPad T14s. Its pipelines were informed by manually studying 2,000 diffs sampled from 7,400 Gentoo package repositories, and the project is currently at version 0.3.0.</span><span class='zh'>OmniDiff 是一个采用 AGPLv3 许可、用 Rust 编写的开源差异工具，支持代码、配置文件、图像、PDF、压缩包、光标和字体等内容。它在 2023 年款 ThinkPad T14s 上可在不到 100 毫秒内比较数万行的 C++ 文件。其处理流程源于对 7400 个 Gentoo 软件包仓库中抽样的 2000 个差异进行人工研究，目前版本为 0.3.0。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>310</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50325253' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>94</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50277379" data-id="50277379" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Web|Code" data-score="310" data-comments="108" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50277379" data-time="1793208563">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chris124567/cfsolve" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cloudflare Turnstile solver library (no headless browser required)</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare Turnstile 求解库（无需无头浏览器）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>cfsolve is a Rust library and command-line tool that solves Cloudflare Turnstile widgets and challenge pages without a headless browser.</span><span class='zh'>cfsolve 是一个 Rust 库和命令行工具，无需无头浏览器即可处理 Cloudflare Turnstile 小部件和挑战页面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>cfsolve is a Rust library and command-line tool that solves Cloudflare Turnstile widgets and challenge pages without a headless browser. It returns a Turnstile token for widgets or a cf_clearance cookie for challenge pages. Widgets can be handled with a page URL and site key, while challenge pages require a direct fetch or the page’s HTML.</span><span class='zh'>cfsolve 是一个 Rust 库和命令行工具，无需无头浏览器即可处理 Cloudflare Turnstile 小部件和挑战页面。它会为小部件返回 Turnstile 令牌，为挑战页面返回 cf_clearance Cookie。处理小部件需要页面 URL 和站点密钥，而挑战页面则需要直接抓取页面或提供页面 HTML。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>310</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50277379' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>108</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50454625" data-id="50454625" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|AI|Code" data-score="285" data-comments="111" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454625" data-time="1794411186">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FrigadeHQ/yogurt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Yogurt – Free, private, open-source alternative to Granola</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Yogurt——免费、私密的开源 Granola 替代品</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Yogurt is a free, MIT-licensed desktop app that uses on-device AI models to transcribe, summarize, and chat about meetings.</span><span class='zh'>Yogurt 是一款采用 MIT 许可证的免费桌面应用，使用设备本地的 AI 模型转录会议、生成摘要并进行问答。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Yogurt is a free, MIT-licensed desktop app that uses on-device AI models to transcribe, summarize, and chat about meetings. It runs in the background without joining calls, and keeps transcripts, recordings, and summaries on the user’s computer. The app also offers calendar integration and a local MCP server for optionally sharing notes with external frontier models.</span><span class='zh'>Yogurt 是一款采用 MIT 许可证的免费桌面应用，使用设备本地的 AI 模型转录会议、生成摘要并进行问答。它在后台运行，不会加入通话，会议转录、录音和摘要也不会离开用户的电脑。该应用还提供日历集成和本地 MCP 服务器，用户可以选择通过它将笔记分享给外部前沿模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>285</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454625' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>111</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50468888" data-id="50468888" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="281" data-comments="95" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468888" data-time="1794496990">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hyperionaidev/smolmaxxing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I&#x27;m making LLMs work on small and old devices</span><span class='zh'>让大语言模型运行在小型和老旧设备上</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>smolmaxxing explores how to run the largest practical language models on small or old devices such as TV boxes, phones, single-board computers, and routers.</span><span class='zh'>smolmaxxing 探索如何在电视盒子、旧手机、单板计算机和路由器等小型或老旧设备上运行实际可用的最大语言模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>smolmaxxing explores how to run the largest practical language models on small or old devices such as TV boxes, phones, single-board computers, and routers. It uses custom kernels, memory optimization, and weight streaming, with claims measured on the actual hardware. On a Xiaomi TV Box S 3rd Gen with about 1 GB of usable RAM, the project reports running models ranging from 260K to 16B parameters.</span><span class='zh'>smolmaxxing 探索如何在电视盒子、旧手机、单板计算机和路由器等小型或老旧设备上运行实际可用的最大语言模型。项目使用定制内核、内存优化和权重流式传输，并在真实硬件上测量各项结果。在可用内存约 1 GB 的 Xiaomi TV Box S 第三代上，项目报告称可运行参数量从 26 万到 160 亿不等的模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>281</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468888' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>95</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50406292" data-id="50406292" data-tags="Show HN|Security|Programming|Code" data-score="280" data-comments="64" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50406292" data-time="1794072450">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hedgerow-dev/coldframe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Coldframe, a honeypot that poses as a supermarket fridge controller</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Coldframe，伪装成超市冰柜控制器的蜜罐</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Coldframe is a Python honeypot that impersonates supermarket refrigeration controllers, including the Danfoss AK-SM 800A and Dixell XWEB interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>Coldframe 是一个用 Python 编写的蜜罐，伪装成超市制冷控制器，包括 Danfoss AK-SM 800A 和 Dixell XWEB 的界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Coldframe is a Python honeypot that impersonates supermarket refrigeration controllers, including the Danfoss AK-SM 800A and Dixell XWEB interfaces. It uses only the Python standard library and matches details such as headers, page sizes, certificate fields, and TLS versions found on real devices. The honeypot recognizes several exploit attempts, including the CVE-2025-41450 login bypass, and records submitted codes.</span><span class='zh'>Coldframe 是一个用 Python 编写的蜜罐，伪装成超市制冷控制器，包括 Danfoss AK-SM 800A 和 Dixell XWEB 的界面。它仅使用 Python 标准库，并模拟真实设备的请求头、页面大小、证书字段和 TLS 版本等细节。该蜜罐能够识别多种攻击尝试，包括 CVE-2025-41450 登录绕过，并记录提交的代码。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dbreunig/nesformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A working Nintendo in ~1GB of Qwen weights</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在约 1GB 的 Qwen 权重中运行一台可工作的任天堂主机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project compiles an NES CPU into the weights of a standard Qwen3.5 model.</span><span class='zh'>该项目将 NES CPU 编译进标准 Qwen3.5 模型的权重中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project compiles an NES CPU into the weights of a standard Qwen3.5 model. The NES 6502 CPU, 2 KB of work RAM, and cartridge ROM run inside the model checkpoint, with every weight computed by a compiler rather than learned through training. During play, the model predicts the CPU execution log token by token while a host program handles graphics, controls, and the vertical-blank interrupt.</span><span class='zh'>该项目将 NES CPU 编译进标准 Qwen3.5 模型的权重中。NES 的 6502 CPU、2 KB 工作内存和卡带程序 ROM 都运行在这个模型检查点内，所有权重均由编译器计算得出，而不是通过训练学习。游戏运行时，模型逐 token 预测 CPU 执行日志，主机程序则负责绘图、读取手柄输入和触发垂直消隐中断。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Truly Modular Keyboard showcases a build with swappable parts and open hardware ethos.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: 真正的模块化键盘展示了一个具备可替换部件、开放软硬件理念的设计。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Truly Modular Keyboard showcases a build with swappable parts and open hardware ethos. The project invites readers to rethink how keyboards can evolve with users.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: 真正的模块化键盘展示了一个具备可替换部件、开放软硬件理念的设计。此类模块化设计能延长设备寿命并更好地满足用户的打字需求。该项目促使人们重新思考键盘如何随用户演进。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/NVIDIA/auto-ontology" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia – open-source auto-ontology</span><span class='zh'>Nvidia：开源自动本体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology is an open Python framework for building semantic, graph-based reasoning layers over data.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology 是一个用于构建语义化图推理层的开源 Python 框架。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology is an open Python framework for building semantic, graph-based reasoning layers over data. It reconciles enterprise metadata and definitions across systems, helping agents locate, interpret, and retrieve data more reliably. The layer is exposed to agent systems through MCP, CLI, and API, and supports text-to-SQL evaluation workflows.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Auto Ontology 是一个用于构建语义化图推理层的开源 Python 框架。它整合企业元数据并协调不同系统中的定义，帮助智能体更可靠地定位、理解和检索数据。该推理层可通过 MCP、CLI 和 API 接入智能体，也支持 text-to-SQL 评测流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/pylance-release/blob/main/docs/tsp/type-server-protocol.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Type Server Protocol</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，扩展语言服务器协议</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft unveils Type Server Protocol, a new layer that augments the Language Server Protocol.</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，为语言服务器协议引入一层新接口，允许编辑器与语言服务器更丰富地交换类型信息与状态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft unveils Type Server Protocol, a new layer that augments the Language Server Protocol. The protocol enables editors and language servers to exchange richer typing information and state, improving performance and scalability for multi-language toolchains. This move could help unify editor experiences across languages and tooling ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Type Server Protocol，为语言服务器协议引入一层新接口，允许编辑器与语言服务器更丰富地交换类型信息与状态。此举有望提升多语言工具链的性能与可扩展性，并统一编辑器体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Saffsanity/sill" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad</span><span class='zh'>让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad refers to a cross-device window mirroring tool enabling users to view or interact with a Mac window on iOS devices.</span><span class='zh'>一款让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上的工具正在展现跨设备协作的新方向，尽管仍处于实验阶段。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Any window from your Mac, on your iPhone and iPad refers to a cross-device window mirroring tool enabling users to view or interact with a Mac window on iOS devices. It hints at a future where a single desktop session spans the Apple ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>一款让 Mac 任意窗口出现在 iPhone 与 iPad 上的工具正在展现跨设备协作的新方向，尽管仍处于实验阶段。该项目凸显了未来可能的无缝多设备工作流，但在实际应用前还需克服稳定性与性能挑战。</span></p>
