<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/da-luce/astroterm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Astroterm: A Planetarium for Your Terminal</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm：让你的终端成为行星仪</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Astroterm is a terminal-based planetarium that renders sky maps and astronomical data directly in your shell.</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm 将星空投影带入终端，直接在外壳中渲染天图与星历数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Astroterm is a terminal-based planetarium that renders sky maps and astronomical data directly in your shell. It brings rich visualization to environments without a full GUI, making it a handy tool for quick stargazing and education.</span><span class='zh'>Astroterm 将星空投影带入终端，直接在外壳中渲染天图与星历数据。即使在无完整图形界面的环境下，也能进行直观的天文可视化，适合快速观星与学习。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/home-assistant/home-assistant.io/issues/49314" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I never authorized an integration that scans my secrets</span><span class='zh'>我从未授权的集成在扫描我的秘密</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rumors or a complaint surfaced that a Home Assistant integration scanned user secrets without consent.</span><span class='zh'>有用户在 Home Assistant 的问题跟踪中声称某个集成在未获得授权的情况下扫描秘密信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rumors or a complaint surfaced that a Home Assistant integration scanned user secrets without consent. The incident highlights privacy risks in extensible platforms and the need for explicit consent and auditable secrets handling. It underscores the ongoing tension between platform openness and user data protection in open-source ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>有用户在 Home Assistant 的问题跟踪中声称某个集成在未获得授权的情况下扫描秘密信息。此事暴露了可扩展平台中的隐私风险，也强调需要对 secrets 的处理进行显式同意和可审计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-cli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI CLI</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 命令行界面</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI released an official command-line interface, letting developers interact with models, manage API keys, and automate tasks from the terminal.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布官方命令行界面，方便开发者在终端与模型交互、管理 API Key 以及执行自动化任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI released an official command-line interface, letting developers interact with models, manage API keys, and automate tasks from the terminal. The CLI lowers barriers for scripting, automation, and CI workflows, complementing the Python client.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布官方命令行界面，方便开发者在终端与模型交互、管理 API Key 以及执行自动化任务。CLI 降低了上手门槛，适合在脚本、持续集成等场景使用，与 Python 客户端形成互补。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/maxilevi/project-hedra/tree/pre-ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hedra is a 3D open world game I wrote from scratch while in highschool</span><span class='zh'>Hedra：高中时从零写成的 3D 开放世界游戏</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hedra is a 3D open world game created from scratch in high school.</span><span class='zh'>Hedra 是作者在高中时独自从零开始开发的 3D 开放世界游戏。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hedra is a 3D open world game created from scratch in high school. This solo project demonstrates independent game development skills, including world-building, controls, and basic engine work, offering a peek into a teen&#x27;s indie effort.</span><span class='zh'>Hedra 是作者在高中时独自从零开始开发的 3D 开放世界游戏。该作品展示了单人开发的完整流程，包括世界构建、控制与基础引擎工作，呈现青少年独立游戏开发的有趣案例。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SheepSeb/tinyworldformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A tiny world model running games on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上运行的微型世界模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A tiny world model runs on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showing that edge AI is creeping into microcontrollers.</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上跑出一个极小的世界模型，展示边缘AI在微控制器上的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A tiny world model runs on a Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W, showing that edge AI is creeping into microcontrollers. The demo demonstrates memory-efficient planning and small neural models that fit in ultra‑compact hardware, hinting at future autonomous nodes in IoT. It signals potential for smarter, low-power devices at the edge.</span><span class='zh'>在 Raspberry Pi Pico 2 W 上跑出一个极小的世界模型，展示边缘AI在微控制器上的潜力。该演示表明在资源受限设备上也能运行简短的神经模型和规划任务。预示着物联网边缘设备将拥有更智能的能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/KrushnaVardhanReddy/PaperPilot" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PaperPilot – Zero-Cloud, Pure-Rust PDF Engine with WASM and MCP</span><span class='zh'>PaperPilot：零云、纯 Rust 的 PDF 引擎，支持 WASM 与 MCP</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PaperPilot is a zero-cloud, pure-Rust PDF engine designed to run in WebAssembly with MCP support.</span><span class='zh'>PaperPilot 是一个零云、纯 Rust 的 PDF 引擎，支持在 WebAssembly 中运行并配合 MCP 使用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PaperPilot is a zero-cloud, pure-Rust PDF engine designed to run in WebAssembly with MCP support. It enables offline, self-contained PDF processing that can be embedded into apps without external services. This could be appealing for privacy-focused workflows and edge deployments.</span><span class='zh'>PaperPilot 是一个零云、纯 Rust 的 PDF 引擎，支持在 WebAssembly 中运行并配合 MCP 使用。它实现离线、即可嵌入的 PDF 处理，避免对外部服务的依赖。对注重隐私的工作流和边缘部署尤具吸引力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/Procexp-Mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Process Explorer for macOS</span><span class='zh'>适用于 macOS 的 Process Explorer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft’s Process Explorer for Mac is a native macOS implementation of the core Process Explorer experience.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 macOS 版 Process Explorer 是对核心 Process Explorer 体验的原生实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft’s Process Explorer for Mac is a native macOS implementation of the core Process Explorer experience. It provides a live process tree, module, handle, and thread inspection, per-process properties, system-wide performance graphs, and code-signing checks. The Swift, SwiftUI, and AppKit application uses public macOS APIs, with optional support for a privileged helper.</span><span class='zh'>微软的 macOS 版 Process Explorer 是对核心 Process Explorer 体验的原生实现。它提供实时进程树、模块、句柄和线程检查、逐进程属性、系统级性能图表以及代码签名检查。该应用使用 Swift、SwiftUI 和 AppKit 构建，并通过 macOS 公共 API 工作，还可选择使用特权辅助程序。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/antirez/c64-hw-simulator" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Commodore 64 hardware simulator: break chips and pins, see the results</span><span class='zh'>Commodore 64 硬件模拟器：解析芯片与引脚，观察结果</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Commodore 64 hardware simulator lets researchers poke at the system by &#x27;breaking&#x27; chips and pins to observe how signals propagate.</span><span class='zh'>这款 Commodore 64 硬件模拟器允许通过模拟对芯片与引脚的‘破坏’来观察信号如何在系统内传输。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Commodore 64 hardware simulator lets researchers poke at the system by &#x27;breaking&#x27; chips and pins to observe how signals propagate. The project illustrates hardware-level behavior and can aid understanding of retro systems, fault tolerance, and emulation accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>这款 Commodore 64 硬件模拟器允许通过模拟对芯片与引脚的‘破坏’来观察信号如何在系统内传输。该项目有助于理解硬件层面的行为、复古系统的仿真精度，以及对容错设计的研究。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/17/top_stories_10172026'><span class='en'>Oct 17</span><span class='zh'>10月17日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>228</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50100366' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>34</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50127586" data-id="50127586" data-tags="Open Source|Security|Code" data-score="224" data-comments="210" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50127586" data-time="1792207119">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/valimised/ivxv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Source code of the Estonian online voting system</span><span class='zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统源代码公开</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The source code of Estonia’s online voting platform has been released publicly, enabling independent audits and security research.</span><span class='zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统的源代码被公开，便于独立审计与安全研究。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The source code of Estonia’s online voting platform has been released publicly, enabling independent audits and security research. This openness can improve transparency and help identify vulnerabilities, though online voting remains a debated topic.</span><span class='zh'>爱沙尼亚在线投票系统的源代码被公开，便于独立审计与安全研究。这一举措有望提升透明度并帮助发现潜在漏洞，但在线投票的安全性与隐私性仍是讨论焦点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/0x23/MicroManipulatorStepper" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator – A sub-micrometer 3D motion control plattform</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator：亚微米级三维运动控制平台</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator is a sub-micrometer 3D motion control platform with open hardware and firmware.</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator 提供一个开源硬件与固件的亚微米级三维运动控制平台，旨在让研究与教育领域的高精度操作民主化，便于实验室和创客空间实现极细微的控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Open MicroManipulator is a sub-micrometer 3D motion control platform with open hardware and firmware. The project aims to democratize precision manipulation for research and education, enabling ultra-fine control in labs and makerspaces.</span><span class='zh'>Open MicroManipulator 提供一个开源硬件与固件的亚微米级三维运动控制平台，旨在让研究与教育领域的高精度操作民主化，便于实验室和创客空间实现极细微的控制。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful experimental debugger that applies Nietzschean prompts to guide debugging strategies, offering a philosophical UX to debugging workflows.</span><span class='zh'>一个将尼采式提示应用于调试策略的实验性调试器，提供哲学化的调试体验。</span></p>

It’s a thought-provoking take on making debugging less mechanical and more narrative.</span><span class='zh'>一个将尼采式提示应用于调试策略的实验性调试器，提供哲学化的调试体验。它挑战传统的机械式排错，试图让调试过程更具叙事性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ihhub/fheroes2" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>fheroes2: A recreation of Heroes of Might and Magic II game engine</span><span class='zh'>fheroes2：对《魔法门之英雄无敌 II》游戏引擎的重建</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>fheroes2 is an open-source recreation of the Heroes of Might and Magic II engine, aiming to faithfully reproduce classic gameplay across platforms.</span><span class='zh'>fheroes2 是对《魔法门之英雄无敌 II》引擎的开源重建，目标在各平台上忠实再现经典玩法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>fheroes2 is an open-source recreation of the Heroes of Might and Magic II engine, aiming to faithfully reproduce classic gameplay across platforms. The project modernizes the engine while preserving the original&#x27;s look and feel, enabling new audiences to experience the game. It demonstrates how community-driven reverse-engineering can extend retro games.</span><span class='zh'>fheroes2 是对《魔法门之英雄无敌 II》引擎的开源重建，目标在各平台上忠实再现经典玩法。项目在保留原作风格的同时实现引擎现代化，帮助新玩家体验这款经典游戏。它展示了社区驱动的逆向工程如何延续复古游戏的生命力。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/timescale/pg_vectorsearch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">662</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/timescale/pg_vectorsearch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch: An index that is faster than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch：比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快的索引</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch is a PostgreSQL extension that claims faster vector-index performance than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW implementations, targeting lower-latency nearest-neighbor queries inside the database.</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch 是一个 PostgreSQL 扩展，宣称在向量索引性能上比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快，重点是降低数据库内的近邻查询延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pg_vectorsearch is a PostgreSQL extension that claims faster vector-index performance than pgvector&#x27;s IVFFlat and HNSW implementations, targeting lower-latency nearest-neighbor queries inside the database. Embedding an optimized index into Postgres can simplify architecture by avoiding external vector stores while improving search speed for vector-heavy workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Pg_vectorsearch 是一个 PostgreSQL 扩展，宣称在向量索引性能上比 pgvector 的 IVFFlat 和 HNSW 更快，重点是降低数据库内的近邻查询延迟。把高效索引嵌入 Postgres 可以减少对外部向量存储的依赖，同时为向量密集型负载提升检索速度。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/14/top_stories_10142026'><span class='en'>Oct 14</span><span class='zh'>10月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>206</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50086165' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>52</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50195488" data-id="50195488" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Code" data-score="194" data-comments="83" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195488" data-time="1792677884">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/dsmurrell/onkey" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1424878564/24f58d68-d92e-493a-8534-6564902cb5a4' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">688</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dsmurrell/onkey" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OnKey – I&#x27;m a bad singer, so I built an app to show where I&#x27;m off-key</span><span class='zh'>OnKey：我走音，于是做了个应用标出走音点</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OnKey analyzes your singing pitch in real time and marks off-key moments, turning practice into actionable feedback.</span><span class='zh'>OnKey 实时分析你的歌声音高并标出走音片段，把练习变成可执行的反馈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OnKey analyzes your singing pitch in real time and marks off-key moments, turning practice into actionable feedback. Built as a Show HN project, it demonstrates how accessible audio analysis tools can be built with open-source tech and signal processing. It could inspire more consumer-friendly music-improvement apps.</span><span class='zh'>OnKey 实时分析你的歌声音高并标出走音片段，把练习变成可执行的反馈。作为 Show HN 项目，展示了用开源技术和信号处理构建音乐分析工具的可行性。它或许会激发更多面向大众的发声训练应用的开发。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/23/top_stories_10232026'><span class='en'>Oct 23</span><span class='zh'>10月23日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>194</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195488' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>83</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50205816" data-id="50205816" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="190" data-comments="148" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50205816" data-time="1792742733">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickBench/pull/2165" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">706</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickBench/pull/2165" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Remove pgrust: the entry is cheating (per-query kernels and result caching)</span><span class='zh'>移除 pgrust：条目被认定为作弊（逐查询内核与结果缓存）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project proposes removing pgrust after indications of cheating, including per-query kernels and result caching that skew benchmarks.</span><span class='zh'>在发现存在作弊迹象（如逐查询内核与结果缓存扭曲基准结果）后，项目组提议移除 pgrust。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project proposes removing pgrust after indications of cheating, including per-query kernels and result caching that skew benchmarks. The move reinforces the need for honest, transparent benchmarking in open-source projects and could affect credibility of reported performance figures. Maintainers emphasize fair competition and reproducibility.</span><span class='zh'>在发现存在作弊迹象（如逐查询内核与结果缓存扭曲基准结果）后，项目组提议移除 pgrust。此举强调在开源基准测试中保持公正、透明的重要性，可能影响对性能数据的信任度，并敦促维护者关注可复现性与公平竞争。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/23/top_stories_10232026'><span class='en'>Oct 23</span><span class='zh'>10月23日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>190</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50205816' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>148</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50299811" data-id="50299811" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="186" data-comments="32" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50299811" data-time="1793333826">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/raphlinus/nibnib" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">728</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/raphlinus/nibnib" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nibnib: Image compression with extreme decompression speed</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib：解压速度极快的图像压缩格式</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nibnib is an image format using 4 bits per pixel and optimized for extremely fast decompression.</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib 是一种每像素 4 bit、针对极快解压速度优化的图像格式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nibnib is an image format using 4 bits per pixel and optimized for extremely fast decompression. It began as an experiment targeting DVI output on an RP2350 microcontroller, where roughly six cycles per pixel must cover pixel fetching, palette lookup, and storage in a line buffer.</span><span class='zh'>Nibnib 是一种每像素 4 bit、针对极快解压速度优化的图像格式。它最初是为 RP2350 微控制器上的 DVI 输出进行的实验，该设备每个像素约有 6 个时钟周期，需要完成像素读取、调色板查询和写入行缓冲区。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 is released with security fixes and new features, signaling ongoing evolution of the crypto stack.</span><span class='zh'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 发布，包含安全修复与新特性，体现密码学库的持续演进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 is released with security fixes and new features, signaling ongoing evolution of the crypto stack. The update may deprecate some APIs and require developers to test for compatibility with existing software. It’s a reminder to keep cryptographic dependencies up to date in production systems.</span><span class='zh'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 发布，包含安全修复与新特性，体现密码学库的持续演进。此次更新可能对某些 API 进行弃用并要求开发者进行兼容性测试，提醒在生产环境中保持加密依赖的最新状态。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mikroverk/esp32sim" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Esp32sim – Run ESP32 firmware in the browser</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Esp32sim—在浏览器中运行 ESP32 固件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Esp32sim lets you run ESP32 firmware directly in the browser, enabling rapid prototyping and education without hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Esp32sim 让你在浏览器中直接运行 ESP32 固件，降低硬件接入门槛，便于快速原型和教学。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Esp32sim lets you run ESP32 firmware directly in the browser, enabling rapid prototyping and education without hardware. It leverages browser-based emulation to emulate the ESP32 environment, reducing setup friction for developers and students. A practical tool for hands-on learning and quick debugging.</span><span class='zh'>Esp32sim 让你在浏览器中直接运行 ESP32 固件，降低硬件接入门槛，便于快速原型和教学。它通过浏览器端的仿真实现 ESP32 环境的模拟，减少开发者和学生的搭建成本。是一个面向动手学习与快速调试的实用工具。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Five Qwen3.8-27B models match Fable 5 using the GVS5H harness, illustrating how open-source LLMs can reach strong performance when paired with efficient hardware. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codex Desktop keeps generating Crashpad pending dumps, indicating a crash-reporting flow bug that could waste disk space or raise privacy concerns.</span><span class='zh'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储，表明崩溃报告流程存在缺陷，可能造成磁盘空间浪费或隐私风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codex Desktop keeps generating Crashpad pending dumps, indicating a crash-reporting flow bug that could waste disk space or raise privacy concerns. Maintainers are tracking the issue and discussing fixes and mitigations.</span><span class='zh'>Codex Desktop 不断生成 Crashpad 待处理转储，表明崩溃报告流程存在缺陷，可能造成磁盘空间浪费或隐私风险。维护者正在跟进并讨论修复与缓解措施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/picocss/pico/discussions/754" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pico CSS is no longer maintained</span><span class='zh'>Pico CSS 不再维护</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>With official maintenance ended, users should plan forks, migrations, or look for alternatives to keep security and compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>官方宣布不再维护，用户需要考虑分叉、迁移或替代方案以维持安全性与兼容性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>With official maintenance ended, users should plan forks, migrations, or look for alternatives to keep security and compatibility. The post may offer guidance on how to migrate or handle breaking changes while preserving the lightweight, accessible styling Pico CSS is known for.</span><span class='zh'>官方宣布不再维护，用户需要考虑分叉、迁移或替代方案以维持安全性与兼容性。文中给出迁移建议与应对潜在改动的途径，同时保留 Pico CSS 的简洁与可访问性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Unleash/unleash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We&#x27;ve built an open-source feature flag server for 11 years</span><span class='zh'>我们打造开源功能开关服务器已有 11 年</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Unleash is an open-source feature-management platform that lets teams control when and how new features are released.</span><span class='zh'>Unleash 是一个开源功能管理平台，让团队控制新功能何时以及如何向用户发布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Unleash is an open-source feature-management platform that lets teams control when and how new features are released. It supports smaller, more manageable production deployments and can streamline software delivery. The project has been developed for 11 years.</span><span class='zh'>Unleash 是一个开源功能管理平台，让团队控制新功能何时以及如何向用户发布。它支持更小、更易管理的生产部署，并可简化软件交付流程。该项目已开发 11 年。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/datareceipts/data-receipts/tree/main/109-gpu-pricing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The same H100 costs $2.69 or $12.29 an hour depending where you rent it</span><span class='zh'>同一块 H100 的租用价格可能是每小时 2.69 或 12.29 美元</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A price comparison normalizes on-demand GPU rental costs across AWS, Azure, and neocloud providers by GPU and hour.</span><span class='zh'>一项价格比较按 GPU 和每小时费用，对 AWS、Azure 及新型云服务商的按需 GPU 租赁价格进行了标准化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A price comparison normalizes on-demand GPU rental costs across AWS, Azure, and neocloud providers by GPU and hour. For identical GPUs, it reports a 4.57× gap between the cheapest and most expensive options. Running one GPU continuously for a month costs $1,964 or $8,972 depending only on the provider.</span><span class='zh'>一项价格比较按 GPU 和每小时费用，对 AWS、Azure 及新型云服务商的按需 GPU 租赁价格进行了标准化。对于相同 GPU，最便宜和最昂贵选项之间的价格差达到 4.57 倍。连续运行一块 GPU 一个月，费用可能因服务商不同而为 1964 美元或 8972 美元。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anomalyco/rune" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rune: A native GPU-rendered Rust UI framework for macOS, Windows, Linux and web</span><span class='zh'>Rune：面向 macOS、Windows、Linux 和 Web 的原生 GPU 渲染 Rust UI 框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rune is a native UI framework for Rust that uses GPU rendering across macOS, Windows, Linux, and the web.</span><span class='zh'>Rune 是一个使用 GPU 渲染的 Rust 原生 UI 框架，支持 macOS、Windows、Linux 和 Web。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rune is a native UI framework for Rust that uses GPU rendering across macOS, Windows, Linux, and the web. It provides its own renderer, text system, layout engine, and spring-based animations. Applications can also run headlessly, allowing agents to render PNG images, dump the element tree, and drive scripted input.</span><span class='zh'>Rune 是一个使用 GPU 渲染的 Rust 原生 UI 框架，支持 macOS、Windows、Linux 和 Web。它自带渲染器、文本系统、布局引擎和基于弹簧模型的动画。应用还可以无头运行，使智能体能够渲染 PNG、导出元素树并通过脚本驱动输入。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/shitter/shitter" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Shitter – A Nitter Fork</span><span class='zh'>Shitter：Nitter 的一个分叉实现</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Shitter is a fork of Nitter, offering a privacy-conscious, JS-light front-end for Twitter-like content and a self-hosted viewing experience.</span><span class='zh'>Shitter 是 Nitter 的一个分叉，提供一个轻量、注重隐私的前端来浏览类似 Twitter 的内容，并支持自托管使用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Shitter is a fork of Nitter, offering a privacy-conscious, JS-light front-end for Twitter-like content and a self-hosted viewing experience. It exemplifies open-source efforts to reduce tracking and API reliance while preserving access to public posts.</span><span class='zh'>Shitter 是 Nitter 的一个分叉，提供一个轻量、注重隐私的前端来浏览类似 Twitter 的内容，并支持自托管使用。该项目展示了开源社区在减少跟踪、降低对官方 API 依赖方面的努力，同时保持对公开内容的访问。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FrigadeHQ/hob" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Hob - Open-source, on device alternative to Claude Cowork and ChatGPT</span><span class='zh'>Hob：开源的本地替代，面向 Claude Cowork 与 ChatGPT</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hob enables an on-device AI experience, offering an open-source alternative to Claude Cowork and ChatGPT that runs entirely locally.</span><span class='zh'>Hob 开源本地化 AI 体验，提供面向 Claude Cowork 与 ChatGPT 的本地替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hob enables an on-device AI experience, offering an open-source alternative to Claude Cowork and ChatGPT that runs entirely locally. It demonstrates the feasibility of private, offline inference on consumer hardware, though model size and compute demands remain practical constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Hob 开源本地化 AI 体验，提供面向 Claude Cowork 与 ChatGPT 的本地替代方案。它展示了在消费级硬件上实现私有、离线推理的可行性，但模型规模与计算需求仍是实际制约。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/johncassil/dontallow" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Auto Dismiss Mac OS &quot;Allow &lt;app&gt; to find devices on local networks?&quot;</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：自动拒绝 MacOS 提示“允许应用发现本地网络设备？”</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Auto Dismiss Mac OS &#x27;Allow app to find devices on local networks?&#x27; is a small open-source tool that automatically closes that permission prompt.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 项目自动拒绝 macOS 的“允许应用发现本地网络设备？</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Auto Dismiss Mac OS &#x27;Allow app to find devices on local networks?&#x27; is a small open-source tool that automatically closes that permission prompt. It highlights the tension between a smooth UX and strict local-network permissions, inviting a discussion on safety vs convenience. The project serves as a reminder of how permission prompts influence everyday security decisions.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 项目自动拒绝 macOS 的“允许应用发现本地网络设备？”提示，提升了使用体验。产品背后的讨论聚焦于 UX 与本地网络权限之间的权衡，以及安全治理的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tylertms/Stela" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A general purpose Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs</span><span class='zh'>面向 x86-64 PC 的通用 Apple Silicon 层</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stela is a UEFI boot program intended to provide an Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs.</span><span class='zh'>Stela 是一个面向 x86-64 PC、旨在提供 Apple Silicon 兼容层的 UEFI 启动程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stela is a UEFI boot program intended to provide an Apple Silicon layer for x86-64 PCs. It includes an Arm64-to-x86-64 translator, an NVIDIA Metal driver, and a model of Apple silicon hardware, and can currently boot macOS and most apps. The v0.1.0 release is slow, requires more testing, and has a low chance of booting on current hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Stela 是一个面向 x86-64 PC、旨在提供 Apple Silicon 兼容层的 UEFI 启动程序。它包含 Arm64 到 x86-64 的转换器、NVIDIA Metal 驱动以及 Apple 芯片硬件模型，目前可以启动 macOS 和大多数应用。v0.1.0 运行速度较慢，仍需大量测试，在当前硬件上成功启动的概率也很低。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>140</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460154' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>57</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50449891" data-id="50449891" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|AI|Code" data-score="138" data-comments="60" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50449891" data-time="1794373007">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/adrbn/bo2-mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Black Ops II statically recompiled to native ARM64 macOS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：将《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project statically recompiles a Steam copy of Black Ops II into a native ARM64 macOS application using Claude Opus 5.5, without Wine, Crossover, Rosetta, or another emulator.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目使用 Claude Opus 5.5，将 Steam 版《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用，不依赖 Wine、Crossover、Rosetta 或其他模拟器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project statically recompiles a Steam copy of Black Ops II into a native ARM64 macOS application using Claude Opus 5.5, without Wine, Crossover, Rosetta, or another emulator. The game reportedly runs at nearly 60 frames per second across the campaign, local multiplayer with bots, and local Zombies mode, including owned DLC maps. Split-screen is being worked on, while LAN mode is planned for later.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目使用 Claude Opus 5.5，将 Steam 版《使命召唤：黑色行动 II》静态重编译为原生 ARM64 macOS 应用，不依赖 Wine、Crossover、Rosetta 或其他模拟器。据称，战役、本地机器人多人模式和本地僵尸模式都能以接近 60 帧运行，已拥有的 DLC 地图也可使用。开发者正在处理分屏功能，局域网模式则计划稍后加入。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>138</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50449891' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>60</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50458166" data-id="50458166" data-tags="AI|Programming|Code" data-score="132" data-comments="31" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50458166" data-time="1794428637">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/huawei-csl/MACaQ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Huawei releases MACaQ, its internal quantizer for local inference</span><span class='zh'>华为发布用于本地推理的内部量化器 MACaQ</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MACaQ is a model-agnostic quantization system for GGUF and local inference across text, image, video, and audio models.</span><span class='zh'>MACaQ 是一个面向 GGUF 和本地推理的模型无关量化系统，支持文本、图像、视频和音频模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MACaQ is a model-agnostic quantization system for GGUF and local inference across text, image, video, and audio models. It supports llama.cpp, vLLM, Diffusers, and PyTorch. Huawei says it can quantize a 122-billion-parameter model in 64 minutes using 32.5 GB of device memory, while achieving lower perplexity than MR-GPTQ and DuQuant++ in stated comparisons.</span><span class='zh'>MACaQ 是一个面向 GGUF 和本地推理的模型无关量化系统，支持文本、图像、视频和音频模型。它兼容 llama.cpp、vLLM、Diffusers 和 PyTorch。华为称，MACaQ 可使用 32.5 GB 设备内存，在 64 分钟内完成一个 1220 亿参数模型的量化；在其给出的比较中，困惑度低于 MR-GPTQ 和 DuQuant++。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>132</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50458166' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>31</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50410249" data-id="50410249" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="131" data-comments="57" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410249" data-time="1794109639">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dmunozv04/isponsorblocktv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ISponsorBlockTV: SponsorBlock client for all YouTube TV clients</span><span class='zh'>iSponsorBlockTV：适用于所有 YouTube TV 客户端的 SponsorBlock 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>iSponsorBlockTV is a self-hosted application that connects to the YouTube TV app and automatically skips sponsored segments and intros using the SponsorBlock API.</span><span class='zh'>iSponsorBlockTV 是一款自托管应用，可连接 YouTube TV 客户端，并通过 SponsorBlock API 自动跳过赞助片段和片头。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>iSponsorBlockTV is a self-hosted application that connects to the YouTube TV app and automatically skips sponsored segments and intros using the SponsorBlock API. It can also mute videos automatically and press YouTube&#x27;s &quot;Skip Ad&quot; button when it appears. YouTube appears to have changed its screen-ID format and is revoking existing codes, so devices must be paired again.</span><span class='zh'>iSponsorBlockTV 是一款自托管应用，可连接 YouTube TV 客户端，并通过 SponsorBlock API 自动跳过赞助片段和片头。它还可以自动静音，并在 YouTube 广告出现“跳过广告”按钮后自动点击。YouTube 似乎更改了屏幕 ID 格式并正在撤销现有代码，因此设备需要重新配对。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>131</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410249' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>57</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50253529" data-id="50253529" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="131" data-comments="31" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50253529" data-time="1793063704">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/otya128/winevdm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>16-bit Windows (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0, 3.1, etc.) on 64-bit Windows</span><span class='zh'>在64位 Windows 上运行16位 Windows 应用（1.x/2.x/3.0/3.1 等）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WineVDM provides a pathway to run 16-bit Windows apps (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0/3.1) on 64-bit Windows by using a dedicated compatibility runtime.</span><span class='zh'>该项目通过 winevdm 在64位 Windows 上实现对16位 Windows 应用（1.x、2.x、3.0、3.1 等）的运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WineVDM provides a pathway to run 16-bit Windows apps (Windows 1.x, 2.x, 3.0/3.1) on 64-bit Windows by using a dedicated compatibility runtime. It illustrates how modern Windows still lacks native 16-bit support, leaving preservation-focused projects to recreate the old environment. This matters for running legacy software, archaeology of software, and niche users who still rely on early Windows programs.</span><span class='zh'>该项目通过 winevdm 在64位 Windows 上实现对16位 Windows 应用（1.x、2.x、3.0、3.1 等）的运行。与现代 Windows 不再原生支持16位应用不同，兼容层/运行时的实现让老旧软件得以保存与使用，但仍存在兼容性与性能限制。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/27/top_stories_10272026'><span class='en'>Oct 27</span><span class='zh'>10月27日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>131</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50253529' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>31</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50099136" data-id="50099136" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Privacy|Code" data-score="129" data-comments="63" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50099136" data-time="1792038328">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/andunieee/flipflop" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Flipflop – Iroh-powered persistent tunnels for filesharing</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Flipflop——由 Iroh 驱动的用于文件共享的持久隧道</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flipflop demonstrates persistent, reliable tunnels for file sharing powered by Iroh, enabling peers to stay reachable despite NATs and dynamic IPs.</span><span class='zh'>Flipflop 展示了由 Iroh 支持的持久隧道文件共享方案，即使在 NAT 和动态 IP 下也能保持可达。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flipflop demonstrates persistent, reliable tunnels for file sharing powered by Iroh, enabling peers to stay reachable despite NATs and dynamic IPs. The project emphasizes resilient, privacy-friendly file transfer with a simple interface for developers.</span><span class='zh'>Flipflop 展示了由 Iroh 支持的持久隧道文件共享方案，即使在 NAT 和动态 IP 下也能保持可达。该项目强调韧性、隐私友好且对开发者友好的文件传输体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mixelpixx/Konnect" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI-assisted PCB design for KiCad 10</span><span class='zh'>KiCad 10 的 AI 辅助 PCB 设计</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KiCad 10 introduces AI-assisted features to automate PCB layout, routing, and component placement.</span><span class='zh'>KiCad 10 引入 AI 辅助功能，帮助自动化布线、走线和器件摆放，提升设计效率并开启新的硬件设计工作流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KiCad 10 introduces AI-assisted features to automate PCB layout, routing, and component placement. The integration aims to boost designer productivity and unlock new design workflows for hardware engineers.</span><span class='zh'>KiCad 10 引入 AI 辅助功能，帮助自动化布线、走线和器件摆放，提升设计效率并开启新的硬件设计工作流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/blog/nvidia/nemotron-transcribe-granary" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe: open ASR for 40 languages</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe：40 种语言的开源 ASR</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe brings open-source ASR to 40 languages, lowering the barrier for multilingual speech applications.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe 为40种语言提供开源自动语音识别，降低多语言语音应用的门槛。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe brings open-source ASR to 40 languages, lowering the barrier for multilingual speech applications. The release lets researchers compare performance against other open models and accelerates progress in open speech AI.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Nemotron 3.5 Transcribe 为40种语言提供开源自动语音识别，降低多语言语音应用的门槛。该发布使研究者能够与其它开源模型进行对比，加速开放语音 AI 的进展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dsx-ai-factory/nvke" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia Kubernetes Engine (NVKE)</span><span class='zh'>Nvidia Kubernetes Engine（NVKE）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Kubernetes Engine is a management plane for running Kubernetes on bare-metal NVIDIA GPU infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Kubernetes Engine 是一个用于在裸机 NVIDIA GPU 基础设施上运行 Kubernetes 的管理平面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA Kubernetes Engine is a management plane for running Kubernetes on bare-metal NVIDIA GPU infrastructure. Platform teams can install it to create and operate workload clusters, each with its own control plane, etcd, and node pools. NVKE is currently a preview release, and its APIs, configuration, and behavior may change, so it is not recommended for production workloads.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA Kubernetes Engine 是一个用于在裸机 NVIDIA GPU 基础设施上运行 Kubernetes 的管理平面。平台团队可以安装它来创建和运营工作负载集群，每个集群都拥有独立的控制平面、etcd 和节点池。NVKE 目前仍处于预览阶段，API、配置和行为可能发生变化，因此不建议用于生产工作负载。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rivet-dev/rivet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Launch HN: Rivet (YC W23) – Scale and Orchestrate Agents on S3</span><span class='zh'>Launch HN：Rivet（YC W23）——在 S3 上扩展和编排代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rivet is an open-source, self-hostable orchestrator for agent workloads built around lightweight actors backed by S3.</span><span class='zh'>Rivet 是一个开源、可自行托管的代理工作负载编排器，围绕由 S3 支持的轻量级 actor 构建。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rivet is an open-source, self-hostable orchestrator for agent workloads built around lightweight actors backed by S3. Each actor provides a long-lived process, in-memory context, a WebSocket server, durable SQLite storage backed by S3, and idle hibernation. The team says the design grew out of infrastructure work for multiplayer games and aims to avoid the overhead of running a heavyweight sandbox per agent.</span><span class='zh'>Rivet 是一个开源、可自行托管的代理工作负载编排器，围绕由 S3 支持的轻量级 actor 构建。每个 actor 都提供长期运行的进程、内存上下文、WebSocket 服务器、由 S3 支持的持久化 SQLite 存储，以及空闲休眠功能。团队表示，该设计源自多人游戏基础设施工作，旨在避免为每个代理运行重量级沙箱的开销。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>118</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50381687' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>58</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50340992" data-id="50340992" data-tags="Programming|AI|Web|Code" data-score="117" data-comments="37" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50340992" data-time="1793643039">

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project is a Claude Code skill for creating product videos. It supports real-browser capture, branded composition, and local mastering, and includes a quick start, worked videos, and usage instructions.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个用于制作产品视频的 Claude Code 技能。它支持真实浏览器录制、品牌化合成和本地母版制作，并提供快速入门、示例视频和使用说明。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gmkurtzer/ginux" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Greg Kurtzer&#x27;s Ginux is now a userspace kernel</span><span class='zh'>Greg Kurtzer 的 Ginux 现已成为用户态内核</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ginux is now a configurable userspace kernel for isolated Linux compatibility instances.</span><span class='zh'>Ginux 现已成为用于隔离 Linux 兼容实例的可配置用户态内核。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ginux is now a configurable userspace kernel for isolated Linux compatibility instances. It runs as an ordinary Linux process and defines an explicit, versioned contract between managed workloads and the host kernel. Its earlier phase as an independent Rust kernel booting through UEFI is complete, with that source preserved separately while active development continues on the userspace version.</span><span class='zh'>Ginux 现已成为用于隔离 Linux 兼容实例的可配置用户态内核。它作为普通 Linux 进程运行，并在受管理工作负载与主机内核之间建立明确且有版本控制的契约。此前通过 UEFI 启动、以 Rust 编写的独立内核阶段已经结束，相关源代码被单独保留，当前开发转向用户态版本。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>114</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50450007' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>76</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50426207" data-id="50426207" data-tags="Programming|Web|Code" data-score="109" data-comments="40" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50426207" data-time="1794234840">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ikey4u/zebkit" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zebkit – Chromium as an embeddable library</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit——可嵌入的 Chromium 库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zebkit packages Chromium’s engine as a cross-platform library for Linux, macOS, and Windows.</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit 将 Chromium 引擎打包成可运行于 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 的跨平台库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zebkit packages Chromium’s engine as a cross-platform library for Linux, macOS, and Windows. It includes Blink, V8 with its JIT tiers, PDFium, FFmpeg, and other Chrome components, but omits the Chrome browser and Electron runtime. Applications can use it through C, C++, Rust, Python, Go, or JavaScript bindings.</span><span class='zh'>Zebkit 将 Chromium 引擎打包成可运行于 Linux、macOS 和 Windows 的跨平台库。它包含 Blink、带有各级 JIT 的 V8、PDFium、FFmpeg 及其他 Chrome 组件，但不包含 Chrome 浏览器本身或 Electron 运行时。应用可以通过 C、C++、Rust、Python、Go 或 JavaScript 绑定使用它。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>109</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50426207' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>40</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50468944" data-id="50468944" data-tags="Open Source|Programming|AI|Web|Code" data-score="107" data-comments="24" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468944" data-time="1794497152">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chocoford/ExcalidrawZ" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ: Local Native Excalidraw on macOS/iOS</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ：macOS/iOS 原生本地版 Excalidraw</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ is a macOS and iOS drawing app built with SwiftUI that preserves the familiar Excalidraw canvas.</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ 是一款使用 SwiftUI 构建的 macOS 和 iOS 绘图应用，保留了熟悉的 Excalidraw 画布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ExcalidrawZ is a macOS and iOS drawing app built with SwiftUI that preserves the familiar Excalidraw canvas. It adds native document management, iCloud sync, history, export, AI assistance, MCP integration, and other Apple-platform features. Drawings are handled as application documents rather than loose browser downloads.</span><span class='zh'>ExcalidrawZ 是一款使用 SwiftUI 构建的 macOS 和 iOS 绘图应用，保留了熟悉的 Excalidraw 画布。它增加了原生文件管理、iCloud 同步、历史记录、导出、AI 辅助、MCP 集成及其他 Apple 平台功能。绘图会作为应用文档管理，而不是浏览器中零散下载的文件。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>107</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50468944' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>24</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50315782" data-id="50315782" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Code" data-score="105" data-comments="6" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315782" data-time="1793451509">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/kerlenton/bootgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1081</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kerlenton/bootgpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A language model that lives in a boot sector</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：运行在引导扇区中的语言模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BootGPT places an entire inference engine in a 510-byte MBR, with model weights, vocabulary, and strings stored in the following disk sectors.</span><span class='zh'>BootGPT 将完整推理引擎放入 510 字节的 MBR 中，并把模型权重、词汇表和字符串存储在后续磁盘扇区。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BootGPT places an entire inference engine in a 510-byte MBR, with model weights, vocabulary, and strings stored in the following disk sectors. Its small character-level neural network was trained to generate Linux boot messages indefinitely, matching a simulator character by character. Debugging uncovered an x86 assembly bug involving the remainder left in EDX by integer division and a pointer that also used EDX.</span><span class='zh'>BootGPT 将完整推理引擎放入 510 字节的 MBR 中，并把模型权重、词汇表和字符串存储在后续磁盘扇区。这个小型字符级神经网络经过训练，可以无限生成 Linux 启动消息，并与模拟器逐字符匹配。调试过程中发现了一个 x86 汇编错误：整数除法会把余数留在 EDX，而偏置表指针也使用了 EDX。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>105</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50315782' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>6</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50160877" data-id="50160877" data-tags="Security|Programming|Code" data-score="102" data-comments="41" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50160877" data-time="1792465507">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/82345" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1104</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/golang/go/issues/82345" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Go will reject certificate chains containing eight distrusted SPKIs</span><span class='zh'>Go 将拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Go is tightening TLS validation by rejecting certificate chains that include eight distrusted SPKI entries.</span><span class='zh'>Go 将通过在 TLS 验证中拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链，来收紧安全性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Go is tightening TLS validation by rejecting certificate chains that include eight distrusted SPKI entries. The change reduces attack surface from compromised keys and misissued certificates, nudging developers to audit complex chains or intermediates.</span><span class='zh'>Go 将通过在 TLS 验证中拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链，来收紧安全性。此举降低了被入侵密钥和被篡改证书带来的风险，促使开发者审查包含多中间证书的证书链。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mottopanikeiku/doomformer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I Turned DOOM into a Transformer</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：把 DOOM 变成了 Transformer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Using DOOM as a testbed, the project applies Transformer-based models to game data, demonstrating how sequence models can learn planning or perception tasks in a classic game.</span><span class='zh'>在 DOOM 的数据上应用 Transformer，展示序列模型在经典游戏中的学习能力，涉及规划与感知任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Using DOOM as a testbed, the project applies Transformer-based models to game data, demonstrating how sequence models can learn planning or perception tasks in a classic game. It’s a compact, proof-of-concept that sparks ideas about cross-domain AI experiments.</span><span class='zh'>在 DOOM 的数据上应用 Transformer，展示序列模型在经典游戏中的学习能力，涉及规划与感知任务。这个小型的实现是跨领域 AI 实验的一个有趣原型，值得关注。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AssemblyForNeovim brings a Godbolt-style assembly view into a floating Neovim window for C, C++, and Rust. It generates assembly with a compiler installed locally, so it does not require an internet connection.</span><span class='zh'>AssemblyForNeovim 将类似 Godbolt 的汇编查看功能带入 Neovim 浮动窗口，支持 C、C++ 和 Rust。它使用本地安装的编译器生成汇编代码，因此无需互联网连接。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/owls-on-wires/keplearn" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Keplearn: Extract Equations from Data in Seconds</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn：秒级从数据中提取方程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Keplearn is a tool that infers equations from data in seconds, enabling rapid symbolic modeling of complex systems.</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn 能在秒级从数据中推断出方程，快速实现对复杂系统的符号建模。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Keplearn is a tool that infers equations from data in seconds, enabling rapid symbolic modeling of complex systems. This accelerates scientific experimentation and can complement traditional regression with interpretable models.</span><span class='zh'>Keplearn 能在秒级从数据中推断出方程，快速实现对复杂系统的符号建模。这加速了科学实验，并可与传统回归方法结合，产生可解释的模型。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/zion-sati/NetWasm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>C# now beats Rust on WASM size</span><span class='zh'>C# 在 WASM 大小方面现在比 Rust 更小</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A WASM size comparison suggests C# compiled to WebAssembly can produce smaller binaries than Rust for certain workloads, challenging the assumption that Rust always yields the smallest output.</span><span class='zh'>对比显示在某些工作负载下，C# 编译为 WebAssembly 的二进制体积可能比 Rust 更小，挑战了“Rust 总是最小”的常识。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A WASM size comparison suggests C# compiled to WebAssembly can produce smaller binaries than Rust for certain workloads, challenging the assumption that Rust always yields the smallest output. This influences language choice for wasm-based apps, though ecosystem and performance considerations remain important.</span><span class='zh'>对比显示在某些工作负载下，C# 编译为 WebAssembly 的二进制体积可能比 Rust 更小，挑战了“Rust 总是最小”的常识。对 wasm 应用的语言选择产生影响，但生态和性能等因素仍然重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/omnara-ai/omnara" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Omnara – Open-source alternative to Claude Managed Agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Omnara Open source 替代 Claude 管理代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Omnara is an open source alternative to Claude&#x27;s Managed Agents, offering configurable AI agents that you can run and customize yourself.</span><span class='zh'>Omnara 是一个开源替代方案，定位为可本地控管的 Claude Managed Agents 的对等实现，提供可配置的 AI 代理和可插拔集成。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Omnara is an open source alternative to Claude&#x27;s Managed Agents, offering configurable AI agents that you can run and customize yourself. The project emphasizes transparency and privacy and aims to match managed agent capabilities with self hosted deployment.</span><span class='zh'>Omnara 是一个开源替代方案，定位为可本地控管的 Claude Managed Agents 的对等实现，提供可配置的 AI 代理和可插拔集成。该项目强调透明度、可定制性和隐私，面向希望自托管 AI 自动化的团队。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anubhavmalhotra/r2d2-tv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Making a smart TV dumb and then smart</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：把智能电视变得更“傻”再重新变为智能</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A DIY project that strips embedded smart TV software to create a privacy-preserving, lean device, then reintroduces smart capabilities in a more controllable way.</span><span class='zh'>一个 DIY 项目通过移除智能电视的内置软件来打造一个轻量、注重隐私的设备，随后再以更可控的方式重新引入智能功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A DIY project that strips embedded smart TV software to create a privacy-preserving, lean device, then reintroduces smart capabilities in a more controllable way. The effort highlights the tension between vendor-bloat and user autonomy, and points to hackable hardware routes for better privacy.</span><span class='zh'>一个 DIY 项目通过移除智能电视的内置软件来打造一个轻量、注重隐私的设备，随后再以更可控的方式重新引入智能功能。此举凸显厂商臃肿软件与用户自主权之间的矛盾，并指向可 Hack 的硬件方式以提升隐私保护。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AGGIB/Stroq" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Stroq – a local audit of what your coding agent did and why</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Stroq——审计编程代理做了什么以及为何这样做</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stroq is an open-source local tool for inspecting Claude Code, Codex, and Cursor sessions.</span><span class='zh'>Stroq 是一款开源本地工具，用于检查 Claude Code、Codex 和 Cursor 的会话。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stroq is an open-source local tool for inspecting Claude Code, Codex, and Cursor sessions. It identifies known secrets, counts commands derived from text an agent read, and shows which keys left the machine. Local hooks can then trace risky commands back to the content that prompted them, without telemetry.</span><span class='zh'>Stroq 是一款开源本地工具，用于检查 Claude Code、Codex 和 Cursor 的会话。它可以识别已知密钥，统计代理从所读取文本中产生的命令，并显示哪些密钥离开了本机。用户还可以通过本地钩子将高风险命令追溯到触发它们的内容，而且不会发送遥测数据。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>82</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50380704' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>30</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50267569" data-id="50267569" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="82" data-comments="18" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50267569" data-time="1793145221">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/maharmstone/moxnox" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Moxnox 1.0: NT-Like, POSIX-Compatible OS Running Atop Btrfs</span><span class='zh'>Moxnox 1.0：在 Btrfs 上的 NT 风格、POSIX 兼容操作系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces Moxnox 1.0, an NT-inspired, POSIX-compatible OS built on the Btrfs filesystem, signaling an experimental approach to combining Windows-like design with modern filesystems.</span><span class='zh'>介绍了 Moxnox 1.0，这是一个在 Btrfs 文件系统上实现的 NT 风格、POSIX 兼容的操作系统原型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces Moxnox 1.0, an NT-inspired, POSIX-compatible OS built on the Btrfs filesystem, signaling an experimental approach to combining Windows-like design with modern filesystems.</span><span class='zh'>介绍了 Moxnox 1.0，这是一个在 Btrfs 文件系统上实现的 NT 风格、POSIX 兼容的操作系统原型。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/piercefreeman/waymark" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Waymark – A workflow engine that compiles jobs instead of replaying</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Waymark——编译任务而非重放任务的工作流引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Waymark is an open-source durable execution library similar to Temporal or DBOS.</span><span class='zh'>Waymark 是一个类似 Temporal 或 DBOS 的开源持久执行库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Waymark is an open-source durable execution library similar to Temporal or DBOS. It lets developers write workflows in ordinary Python code using conditionals, loops, and asyncio-style parallelism, while the runtime handles distributed execution. Rather than replaying past state in an interpreter, Waymark walks the program’s AST and compiles the logic into bytecode for a stackless virtual machine.</span><span class='zh'>Waymark 是一个类似 Temporal 或 DBOS 的开源持久执行库。开发者可以用普通 Python 代码编写工作流，包括条件语句、循环和类似 asyncio 的并行操作，再由运行时负责分布式执行。Waymark 不通过解释器重放历史状态，而是遍历程序 AST，将逻辑编译为运行在无栈虚拟机上的字节码。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/06/top_stories_11062026'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>81</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50379221' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>40</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50278645" data-id="50278645" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="77" data-comments="47" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50278645" data-time="1793212165">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/theconsensuslabs/noalloc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>noalloc: Tooling and libraries to enforce zero heap allocations in Golang</span><span class='zh'>noalloc：在 Golang 中实现零堆分配的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Noalloc is a toolkit to enforce zero-heap allocations in Go programs, improving latency predictability for high-performance services.</span><span class='zh'>noalloc 提供工具与库，用于在 Go 程序中强制零堆分配，从而提升高性能服务的延迟可预测性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Noalloc is a toolkit to enforce zero-heap allocations in Go programs, improving latency predictability for high-performance services. It helps developers reason about memory use, though it may constrain certain idioms and require careful design.</span><span class='zh'>noalloc 提供工具与库，用于在 Go 程序中强制零堆分配，从而提升高性能服务的延迟可预测性。它帮助开发者管理内存使用，但可能限制某些编码习惯并需谨慎设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Parcha-ai/agentrun/blob/main/README.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: AgentRun – open-source, Self-improving Managed Agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：AgentRun——开源、自我改进的托管智能体</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GREP.AI has open-sourced AgentRun as a managed platform for agents. It includes self-improving agents, web-grounded workflows, sandboxes, computer environments, tools and integrations, and behavioral analytics. AgentRun supports harnesses including Pi, Claude Agents, Codex, and OpenCode, and can be self-hosted or used as a hosted service.</span><span class='zh'>GREP.AI 将 AgentRun 开源为一个托管智能体平台。它包含自我改进智能体、基于网页信息的工作流、沙箱和计算机环境、工具与集成，以及智能体行为分析。AgentRun 支持 Pi、Claude Agents、Codex 和 OpenCode 等 harness，也可自行托管或使用托管版本。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Spotatui offers a Spotify client with no web API rate limits, using a local or alternate access path, enabling smoother playback experiences. The project raises questions about API policy, terms of service, and future reliance on official endpoints.</span><span class='zh'>Spotatui 提供一个无需网页 API 速率限制的 Spotify 客户端，提升播放体验并可能走近本地化访问路径，同时引发对服务条款和未来 API 变动的关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/shikokuchuo/pymizu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mizu – zero-copy parallel R and Python over shared memory</span><span class='zh'>Mizu：通过共享内存实现 R 与 Python 的零拷贝并行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mizu provides thread-speed asynchronous and parallel execution for Python over shared memory.</span><span class='zh'>Mizu 通过共享内存为 Python 提供接近线程速度的异步和并行执行能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mizu provides thread-speed asynchronous and parallel execution for Python over shared memory. Its benchmarks report reducing task round trips from roughly 100 microseconds over sockets to less than 1 microsecond. Lists, dictionaries, NumPy arrays, and Arrow tables can be shared between Python and R as zero-copy views.</span><span class='zh'>Mizu 通过共享内存为 Python 提供接近线程速度的异步和并行执行能力。其基准测试称，任务往返时间可从基于套接字的约 100 微秒降至不到 1 微秒。列表、字典、NumPy 数组和 Arrow 表都可以作为零拷贝视图在 Python 与 R 之间共享。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Show HN project formalizes IRS tax forms as a proof-of-concept, likely using a theorem prover or formal spec; demonstrates how to model tax forms for correctness and tooling; significance for software that handles regulated forms. 2-3 sentences.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了将 IRS 税表形式化的概念性实现，借助形式化工具提高正确性与可验证性。此举对政府表单数字化和软件可靠性具有示范价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/popvax/yash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cancer vaccine for Yash, he&#x27;s diagnosed with stage IV pancreatic cancer</span><span class='zh'>Yash 的癌症疫苗：他被诊断为 IV 期胰腺癌</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project describes a personalized mRNA cancer vaccine for Yash, produced at the RNA Foundry.</span><span class='zh'>该项目介绍了一款由 RNA Foundry 制作、面向 Yash 的个性化 mRNA 癌症疫苗。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project describes a personalized mRNA cancer vaccine for Yash, produced at the RNA Foundry. Yashdeep Bindal was diagnosed with stage IV pancreatic cancer in January 2025 at age 32, shortly after returning to work following the birth of his daughter Maya.</span><span class='zh'>该项目介绍了一款由 RNA Foundry 制作、面向 Yash 的个性化 mRNA 癌症疫苗。Yashdeep Bindal 于 2025 年 1 月、32 岁时被诊断为 IV 期胰腺癌；此前他刚在女儿 Maya 出生后重返工作岗位。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/klad-app/klad" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Klad, a paint app your coding agent can drive (source-available)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Klad，一款可由编码代理驱动的绘画应用（源代码可用）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Klad is a Rust-based painting application that coding agents can control through a CLI, while also offering a full graphical interface for humans.</span><span class='zh'>Klad 是一款用 Rust 编写的绘画应用，编码代理可以通过 CLI 控制它，同时人类也能使用完整的图形界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Klad is a Rust-based painting application that coding agents can control through a CLI, while also offering a full graphical interface for humans. It supports brush painting, frame-by-frame animation, bones and inverse kinematics, vectors, text layers, colors, layer effects, and persistent unlimited undo. Its editable strokes are intended to let agents create drafts that people can refine stroke by stroke.</span><span class='zh'>Klad 是一款用 Rust 编写的绘画应用，编码代理可以通过 CLI 控制它，同时人类也能使用完整的图形界面。它支持笔刷绘画、逐帧动画、骨骼与反向运动学、矢量、文本图层、颜色、图层效果，以及可跨文件重新打开保留的无限撤销。它将可编辑笔触作为核心，使代理能够生成草稿，再由人类逐笔修改。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>69</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50454951' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>29</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50171936" data-id="50171936" data-tags="Data|Programming|Code" data-score="67" data-comments="15" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50171936" data-time="1792526855">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/astroautomata/PySR" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Symbolic Learning in Python and Julia</span><span class='zh'>Python 与 Julia 中的符号学习</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PySR blends symbolic regression with modern ML ecosystems to yield interpretable models in Python and Julia.</span><span class='zh'>PySR 将符号回归与现代机器学习生态系统相结合，在 Python 与 Julia 中生成可解释的模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PySR blends symbolic regression with modern ML ecosystems to yield interpretable models in Python and Julia. By combining mathematical discovery with scalable tooling, it helps uncover human-readable expressions that fit data.</span><span class='zh'>PySR 将符号回归与现代机器学习生态系统相结合，在 Python 与 Julia 中生成可解释的模型。通过将数学发现与可扩展工具链结合，它有助于发现能拟合数据且易读的表达式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/johnrash/bebox" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>BeBox is a Haiku application that emulates Be Inc.&#x27;s BeBox</span><span class='zh'>BeBox：在 Haiku 上模拟 BeInc 的 BeBox</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>BeBox is a Haiku app that emulates Be Inc.&#x27;s BeBox, allowing retro BeOS-era software to run on modern Haiku OS.</span><span class='zh'>BeBox 是一个在 Haiku 操作系统上运行的 BeBox 模拟应用，允许复古 BeOS 时代的软件在新环境中运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>BeBox is a Haiku app that emulates Be Inc.&#x27;s BeBox, allowing retro BeOS-era software to run on modern Haiku OS. It&#x27;s a niche project that showcases emulation fidelity and the continued appeal of vintage systems. For enthusiasts, it&#x27;s a glimpse into how hobbyists keep old ecosystems alive.</span><span class='zh'>BeBox 是一个在 Haiku 操作系统上运行的 BeBox 模拟应用，允许复古 BeOS 时代的软件在新环境中运行。该项目展示了仿真与系统级再现的潜力，也反映出爱好者对旧系统的持续热情。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Video-Rebirth/hyperflow-h3-flash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: We compressed a 31B video+audio diffusion model to 14B, 6 steps, INT8</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：将 31B 视频音频扩散模型压缩至 14B、6 步、INT8</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HyperFlow H3 Flash is a compressed, few-step version of MiniMax-H3 with 13.7 billion parameters.</span><span class='zh'>HyperFlow H3 Flash 是 MiniMax-H3 的压缩少步数版本，包含 137 亿个参数。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HyperFlow H3 Flash is a compressed, few-step version of MiniMax-H3 with 13.7 billion parameters. It generates video and synchronized stereo audio together and supports INT8 and GGUF formats. The project also provides weights, ComfyUI nodes, a GGUF setup guide, and an eight-step LoRA for the full-size model.</span><span class='zh'>HyperFlow H3 Flash 是 MiniMax-H3 的压缩少步数版本，包含 137 亿个参数。它能够同时生成视频和同步立体声，并支持 INT8 与 GGUF 格式。项目还提供模型权重、ComfyUI 节点、GGUF 配置指南，以及适用于完整模型的 8 步 LoRA。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KiviDB positions as a faster Redis-compatible in-memory database with vector caching, targeting real-time analytics and AI workloads that demand low latency.</span><span class='zh'>KiviDB 被定位为更快的 Redis 兼容内存数据库，提供向量缓存，面向对延迟要求极高的实时分析与 AI 工作负载。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KiviDB positions as a faster Redis-compatible in-memory database with vector caching, targeting real-time analytics and AI workloads that demand low latency.</span><span class='zh'>KiviDB 被定位为更快的 Redis 兼容内存数据库，提供向量缓存，面向对延迟要求极高的实时分析与 AI 工作负载。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/bealesh/neckbeard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Neckbeard – A DevOps/platform/SRE plugin for your agent</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard——为你的代理提供的 DevOps/平台/SRE 插件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Neckbeard is a plugin collection that integrates agent workflows with DevOps, platform, and SRE tasks—think CI/CD interactions, infrastructure control, and observability hooks for agent-driven automation.</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard 是一组插件，旨在将代理工作流与 DevOps、平台和 SRE 任务集成，包括 CI/CD、基础设施控制和可观测性接口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Neckbeard is a plugin collection that integrates agent workflows with DevOps, platform, and SRE tasks—think CI/CD interactions, infrastructure control, and observability hooks for agent-driven automation. It aims to make agents more useful and safer in operational contexts by providing ready-made connectors and operational primitives.</span><span class='zh'>Neckbeard 是一组插件，旨在将代理工作流与 DevOps、平台和 SRE 任务集成，包括 CI/CD、基础设施控制和可观测性接口。它通过提供现成的连接器和操作原语，使代理在运行时管理场景中更实用且更安全。</span></p>
