<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hyphatech/spindle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Spindle – a full-featured, type-safe web framework for OCaml 5</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Spindle，面向 OCaml 5 的全功能类型安全 Web 框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Spindle is a multicore-by-design, Eio-native web framework for OCaml that uses a type-driven API to derive codecs, request validation, OpenAPI specifications, and frontend schemas.</span><span class='zh'>Spindle 是一个面向多核设计、原生支持 Eio 的 OCaml Web 框架，通过类型驱动的 API 自动生成编解码器、请求验证、OpenAPI 规范和前端模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Spindle is a multicore-by-design, Eio-native web framework for OCaml that uses a type-driven API to derive codecs, request validation, OpenAPI specifications, and frontend schemas. It includes an RFC-strict HTTP server, WebSockets, SSE, MCP server primitives, tracing, metrics, and logging. Its benchmarks report performance two times faster than NestJS and five times faster than FastAPI on a single core, although speed was not the project’s primary goal.</span><span class='zh'>Spindle 是一个面向多核设计、原生支持 Eio 的 OCaml Web 框架，通过类型驱动的 API 自动生成编解码器、请求验证、OpenAPI 规范和前端模式。它包含严格遵循 RFC 的 HTTP 服务器、WebSocket、SSE、MCP 服务器基础组件，以及分布式追踪、指标和日志功能。基准测试显示，它在单核上运行速度约为 NestJS 的两倍、FastAPI 的五倍，不过速度并非项目的首要目标。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>58</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50377613' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>8</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50413266" data-id="50413266" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Code" data-score="53" data-comments="13" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50413266" data-time="1794145208">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rochus-keller/Micron" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Micron – Oberon with the Power of C</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Micron——带有 C 能力的 Oberon</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Micron is a lean, statically typed, modular systems language combining Oberon’s simplicity and explicit modules with C’s low-level capabilities and interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Micron 是一种精简、静态类型、模块化的系统编程语言，将 Oberon 的简洁性和显式模块结构与 C 的底层能力及互操作性结合起来。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Micron is a lean, statically typed, modular systems language combining Oberon’s simplicity and explicit modules with C’s low-level capabilities and interoperability. Its goal is to use one language for boot code, device drivers, operating-system kernels, and ordinary applications without a garbage collector. It supports explicit memory layouts, signed and unsigned integers, pointer operations, and direct C interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Micron 是一种精简、静态类型、模块化的系统编程语言，将 Oberon 的简洁性和显式模块结构与 C 的底层能力及互操作性结合起来。它的目标是用同一种语言编写启动代码、设备驱动、操作系统内核和普通应用，同时不依赖垃圾回收器。该语言支持显式内存布局、有符号和无符号整数、指针操作以及直接的 C 互操作。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>53</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50413266' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>13</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50409855" data-id="50409855" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="53" data-comments="31" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50409855" data-time="1794103347">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/arterialist/kube32" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I rebuilt Kubernetes for ESP32 microcontrollers</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我为 ESP32 微控制器重做了 Kubernetes</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kube32 is a Kubernetes-compatible control plane and WebAssembly pod runtime for ESP32-S3 microcontrollers.</span><span class='zh'>Kube32 是面向 ESP32-S3 微控制器的 Kubernetes 兼容控制平面和 WebAssembly Pod 运行时。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kube32 is a Kubernetes-compatible control plane and WebAssembly pod runtime for ESP32-S3 microcontrollers. The project lets kubectl and k9s manage boards over Wi-Fi or USB serial, while pods run WebAssembly modules through the wasm3 interpreter. A board with PSRAM serves as the control plane, and boards without PSRAM act as workers. The same sources also build a macOS and Linux host simulator.</span><span class='zh'>Kube32 是面向 ESP32-S3 微控制器的 Kubernetes 兼容控制平面和 WebAssembly Pod 运行时。该项目支持通过 Wi-Fi 或 USB 串口使用 kubectl 和 k9s 管理开发板，并通过 wasm3 解释器运行 WebAssembly 模块。带 PSRAM 的开发板充当控制平面，不带 PSRAM 的开发板作为工作节点。同一套源代码还可构建 macOS 和 Linux 主机模拟器。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/thesayfulla/pylings" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PyLings: Solve Exercises and Learn Python</span><span class='zh'>PyLings：通过练习学习 Python</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PyLings teaches Python by having users fix code in exercises ranging from basic concepts to senior-level interview problems.</span><span class='zh'>PyLings 通过让用户修复代码来学习 Python，练习范围从基础知识延伸到高级面试题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PyLings teaches Python by having users fix code in exercises ranging from basic concepts to senior-level interview problems. It includes 35 warm-ups and 20 interview exercises, requires no dependencies, and targets Python 3.14. The tool opens the next exercise and reruns its tests whenever the user saves.</span><span class='zh'>PyLings 通过让用户修复代码来学习 Python，练习范围从基础知识延伸到高级面试题。它包含 35 道热身题和 20 道面试题，不依赖额外软件包，面向 Python 3.14。用户每次保存时，工具都会打开下一道练习并重新运行测试。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This project collects V100 SXM2 pin maps, two- and four-GPU NVLink connection plans, and connector-only KiCad resources.</span><span class='zh'>该项目整理了 V100 SXM2 引脚图、双 GPU 和四 GPU 的 NVLink 连接方案，以及仅含连接器的 KiCad 资源。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project collects V100 SXM2 pin maps, two- and four-GPU NVLink connection plans, and connector-only KiCad resources. It aims to support an open-source carrier-board design for Nvidia V100 SXM2 modules, while enabling others to develop their own designs.</span><span class='zh'>该项目整理了 V100 SXM2 引脚图、双 GPU 和四 GPU 的 NVLink 连接方案，以及仅含连接器的 KiCad 资源。项目目标是支持 Nvidia V100 SXM2 模块的开源载板设计，同时让其他人能够据此开发自己的设计。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GridSnap is an open-source window snapping tool for macOS inspired by Divvy, enabling grid-based window layouts to improve productivity and screen organization.</span><span class='zh'>GridSnap 是一个开源的 macOS 窗口对齐工具，效仿 Divvy 的网格布局方式，便于快速组织屏幕空间，提高工作效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/graealex/WaveDudeNext" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WaveDudeNext 2D Wave Simulator</span><span class='zh'>WaveDudeNext 二维波模拟器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WaveDudeNext is an interactive Windows simulator for two-dimensional wave optics.</span><span class='zh'>WaveDudeNext 是一款面向 Windows 的交互式二维波动光学模拟器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WaveDudeNext is an interactive Windows simulator for two-dimensional wave optics. Users can turn images into refractive scenes, place light sources and optical components, and observe refraction, diffraction, and interference. It displays both the wave field and time-averaged intensity, while supporting probes, timeline control, video export, adjustable brightness, custom color schemes, and multiple interface languages.</span><span class='zh'>WaveDudeNext 是一款面向 Windows 的交互式二维波动光学模拟器。用户可以将图像转换为折射场景，放置光源和光学元件，并观察折射、衍射与干涉现象。程序同时显示波场和时间平均强度，并支持探针、时间轴控制、视频导出、亮度调节、自定义配色以及多种界面语言。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vacuula/vacuula" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Custom server to revive neato bots</span><span class='zh'>自定义服务器让 Neato 机器人重新工作</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An open-source project provides a custom server to revive Neato robots by replacing or augmenting the official backend, enabling continued control and firmware updates.</span><span class='zh'>一个开源项目提供自定义服务器，通过替代或增强官方后端，让 Neato 机器人继续被控制并获得固件更新，展现了社区驱动的硬件改造如何延长消费物联网设备的寿命。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An open-source project provides a custom server to revive Neato robots by replacing or augmenting the official backend, enabling continued control and firmware updates. This demonstrates how community-driven hardware hacking can extend the life of consumer IoT devices.</span><span class='zh'>一个开源项目提供自定义服务器，通过替代或增强官方后端，让 Neato 机器人继续被控制并获得固件更新，展现了社区驱动的硬件改造如何延长消费物联网设备的寿命。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gitlab.com/c4psu13/mali-pagecache-root" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Autonomous root for Tcl Android TV via CVE-2022-22706</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：通过 CVE-2022-22706 实现的 Android TV 自主 Root</span></a><span class="dom">gitlab.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Show HN demonstrates obtaining an autonomous root on a Tcl Android TV device using a known CVE, highlighting ongoing security challenges in consumer electronics and the importance of timely patching.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了利用已知 CVE 在 Tcl Android TV 设备上实现自主 Root 的方法，凸显消费电子产品安全性挑战和及时修补的重要性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Show HN demonstrates obtaining an autonomous root on a Tcl Android TV device using a known CVE, highlighting ongoing security challenges in consumer electronics and the importance of timely patching.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了利用已知 CVE 在 Tcl Android TV 设备上实现自主 Root 的方法，凸显消费电子产品安全性挑战和及时修补的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/OpenTSLM/TimeNet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TimeNet: Open-source data infrastructure for Temporal Foundation Models</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet：面向时间基础模型的开源数据基础设施</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TimeNet provides a unified data layer for temporal datasets used by Temporal Foundation Models.</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet 为时间基础模型使用的时间序列数据集提供统一数据层。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TimeNet provides a unified data layer for temporal datasets used by Temporal Foundation Models. It supports tasks including classification, question answering, scalar prediction, temporal localization, forecasting, generation, editing, and correspondence. The project reports that training on multiple temporal datasets improved average F1 performance by 14% for OpenTSLM and 22% for SLIP.</span><span class='zh'>TimeNet 为时间基础模型使用的时间序列数据集提供统一数据层。它支持分类、问答、标量预测、时间定位、预测、生成、编辑和对应关系等任务。项目方称，在多个时间数据集上训练后，OpenTSLM 的平均 F1 性能提升了 14%，SLIP 提升了 22%。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/11/top_stories_11112026'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>46</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50443264' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>6</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50302686" data-id="50302686" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="43" data-comments="21" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50302686" data-time="1793361024">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/skorotkiewicz/c59e591c26661cb52b06afc42d8ac546" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decode kernel panic from QR Code photo</span><span class='zh'>从二维码照片中解码内核崩溃信息</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is titled “decode kernel panic from QR Code photo.” It is presented as a GitHub Gist, but the available page content does not provide the decoding method or a working example.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目名为“从二维码照片中解码内核崩溃信息”。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is titled “decode kernel panic from QR Code photo.” It is presented as a GitHub Gist, but the available page content does not provide the decoding method or a working example.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目名为“从二维码照片中解码内核崩溃信息”。它以 GitHub Gist 的形式呈现，但现有页面内容没有提供解码方法或可运行示例。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1581</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vsmutok/FuckCaptcha" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A field guide to CAPTCHA types and how they break</span><span class='zh'>CAPTCHA 类型及其破解方式实地指南</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is an open field guide covering 39 CAPTCHA types, their identifying signals, documented open-source bypass methods, and paid alternatives.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一份开放实地指南，涵盖 39 种 CAPTCHA 类型、识别信号、已有的开源绕过方法及付费替代方案，并提供可搜索的网页版内容。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is an open field guide covering 39 CAPTCHA types, their identifying signals, documented open-source bypass methods, and paid alternatives. It provides a searchable web version and frames the material for education and authorized research. Its stated goal is to make changing CAPTCHA systems easier to navigate, compare, and cite while emphasizing responsible use.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一份开放实地指南，涵盖 39 种 CAPTCHA 类型、识别信号、已有的开源绕过方法及付费替代方案，并提供可搜索的网页版内容。项目将材料定位为教育和获授权研究用途。其目标是在强调负责任使用的同时，让不断变化的 CAPTCHA 系统更易于查找、比较和引用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RFnexus/partyline" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Partyline: A group voice application for Reticulum, built on LXST</span><span class='zh'>Partyline：一个基于 Reticulum 的群组语音应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Partyline is a group voice app built on Reticulum and LXST, showcasing a decentralized approach to real-time group audio.</span><span class='zh'>Partyline 是一个在 Reticulum 与 LXST 上构建的群组语音应用，展示了去中心化的实时群体音频通信。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Partyline is a group voice app built on Reticulum and LXST, showcasing a decentralized approach to real-time group audio. The project emphasizes privacy-first peer-to-peer communication without centralized servers.</span><span class='zh'>Partyline 是一个在 Reticulum 与 LXST 上构建的群组语音应用，展示了去中心化的实时群体音频通信。该项目强调隐私优先的点对点通信，避免中心化服务器。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/peterretief/codemap" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Codemap – one-page interactive map of any Go repository</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Codemap——用一页交互式地图展示任意 Go 仓库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Codemap generates a single HTML page mapping a Go module’s packages, dependencies, files, and functions.</span><span class='zh'>Codemap 会生成一个 HTML 页面，展示 Go 模块中的包、依赖、文件和函数。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Codemap generates a single HTML page mapping a Go module’s packages, dependencies, files, and functions. Users can search the map, inspect what each package uses and what uses it, and jump to function definitions in GitHub-hosted modules. Tests are excluded from the map.</span><span class='zh'>Codemap 会生成一个 HTML 页面，展示 Go 模块中的包、依赖、文件和函数。用户可以搜索地图，查看每个包依赖什么以及被什么依赖，并跳转到 GitHub 模块中的函数定义。地图不包含测试代码。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Dodaios/ste80-humanizer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: STE80 Humanizer – de-AI text by writing 80% of the way to ASD-STE100</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer：将 AI 文本改写至接近 ASD-STE100 的 80%</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>STE80 Humanizer is an agent skill that rewrites AI-generated text to remove common AI markers while preserving its meaning.</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer 是一种智能体技能，旨在去除 AI 生成文本的常见痕迹，同时保持原意。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>STE80 Humanizer is an agent skill that rewrites AI-generated text to remove common AI markers while preserving its meaning. It targets roughly 80% compliance with ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English. The example focuses on Kubernetes liveness probes and retains both their restart behavior and the warning that misconfiguration can cause cascading restarts.</span><span class='zh'>STE80 Humanizer 是一种智能体技能，旨在去除 AI 生成文本的常见痕迹，同时保持原意。它的目标是让文本达到约 80% 的 ASD-STE100 简明技术英语规范。示例内容围绕 Kubernetes 存活探针展开，并保留了探针触发重启的行为以及配置错误可能导致级联重启的警告。</span></p>

It requires no installation and provides sample emails through its companion site.</span><span class='zh'>RAM-Altman 是一个每天向 OpenAI CEO Sam Altman 报告闲置内存的 Bash 脚本。它无需安装，并通过配套网站提供示例邮件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/graphicle-ai/graphicle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Graphicle, a Rust port of Graphify with memory for coding agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Graphicle——Graphify 的 Rust 移植，带有编码代理记忆</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Graphicle is a Rust port of Graphify that adds a memory model tailored for coding agents, enabling them to retain context and past decisions during multi-step programming tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Graphicle 是 Graphify 的 Rust 移植版，增加了为编码代理设计的记忆模型，使代理在多步骤编程任务中能保留上下文与历史决策。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Graphicle is a Rust port of Graphify that adds a memory model tailored for coding agents, enabling them to retain context and past decisions during multi-step programming tasks. Implementing this in Rust emphasizes performance and safety, making it attractive for systems that need reliable, low-latency agent-assisted coding workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Graphicle 是 Graphify 的 Rust 移植版，增加了为编码代理设计的记忆模型，使代理在多步骤编程任务中能保留上下文与历史决策。用 Rust 实现强调性能与安全，适合需要可靠、低延迟代理辅助编码的系统。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/14/top_stories_10142026'><span class='en'>Oct 14</span><span class='zh'>10月14日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>40</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50088713' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>11</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50386789" data-id="50386789" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="39" data-comments="20" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50386789" data-time="1793926455">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1636</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/matthewmrichter/mooftcp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MoofTCP – A modern replacement for MacTCP on 68k Macs</span><span class='zh'>MoofTCP——68K Mac 上 MacTCP 的现代替代品</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MoofTCP is a drop-in replacement for Apple&#x27;s MacTCP on System 6 and 68000-based Macs.</span><span class='zh'>MoofTCP 是 System 6 和 68000 Mac 上苹果 MacTCP 的即插即用替代品。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MoofTCP is a drop-in replacement for Apple&#x27;s MacTCP on System 6 and 68000-based Macs. Existing MacTCP programs can run unchanged on its newly written, portable-C TCP/IP stack. It includes its own control panel, installer, and DHCP client, and was designed for a Mac Classic with 4 MB of RAM.</span><span class='zh'>MoofTCP 是 System 6 和 68000 Mac 上苹果 MacTCP 的即插即用替代品。现有的 MacTCP 程序无需修改即可运行在其用可移植 C 语言重写的 TCP/IP 协议栈上。它还包含独立的控制面板、安装程序和 DHCP 客户端，并针对仅有 4 MB 内存的 Mac Classic 设计。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/05/top_stories_11052026'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>39</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50386789' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>20</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50429210" data-id="50429210" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="35" data-comments="14" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50429210" data-time="1794247830">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/no382001/zipc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1672</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/no382001/zipc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A ZIP-style Prolog VM for trilog</span><span class='zh'>用于 trilog 的 ZIP 风格 Prolog 虚拟机</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This work-in-progress project implements a ZIP-style Prolog virtual machine and a Prolog-to-ZIP compiler in C.</span><span class='zh'>这个进行中的项目用 C 实现了 ZIP 风格的 Prolog 虚拟机，以及从 Prolog 到 ZIP 的编译器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This work-in-progress project implements a ZIP-style Prolog virtual machine and a Prolog-to-ZIP compiler in C. Its goal is to provide enough of a VM to compare it with the current trilog runtime. ZIP is the abstract machine described in the 1984 book Design and Simulation of a Sequential Prolog Machine.</span><span class='zh'>这个进行中的项目用 C 实现了 ZIP 风格的 Prolog 虚拟机，以及从 Prolog 到 ZIP 的编译器。其目标是先搭建足够完整的虚拟机，以便与当前的 trilog 运行时进行比较。ZIP 是 1984 年《Design and Simulation of a Sequential Prolog Machine》中描述的抽象机器。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Overtone is a show-n-HN project that transcribes, corrects, and cuts captions to fit any screen shape.</span><span class='zh'>Overtone 是 Show HN 项目，能够转写、纠错并将字幕裁剪以适配任意屏幕形状，提升无障碍和本地化工作效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Overtone is a show-n-HN project that transcribes, corrects, and cuts captions to fit any screen shape. It advances accessibility and localization workflows by automating caption adaptation. The tool can help creators reach diverse audiences across devices with fewer manual tweaks.</span><span class='zh'>Overtone 是 Show HN 项目，能够转写、纠错并将字幕裁剪以适配任意屏幕形状，提升无障碍和本地化工作效率。它帮助内容创作者覆盖多设备场景，省去大量手动调整。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Vapmuz/Mocktastic" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mocktastic: A Meshtastic Node on Arduino</span><span class='zh'>Mocktastic：Arduino 上的 Meshtastic 节点</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mocktastic is a small, customizable Meshtastic-compatible LoRa client for ESP32 boards built with ArduinoIDE.</span><span class='zh'>Mocktastic 是一个面向 ESP32 开发板、使用 ArduinoIDE 构建的小型可定制 Meshtastic 兼容 LoRa 客户端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mocktastic is a small, customizable Meshtastic-compatible LoRa client for ESP32 boards built with ArduinoIDE. It is an educational and experimental C++ implementation rather than a fork of the official firmware. The project demonstrates Meshtastic packet encoding and decoding and includes an environmental-sensor example.</span><span class='zh'>Mocktastic 是一个面向 ESP32 开发板、使用 ArduinoIDE 构建的小型可定制 Meshtastic 兼容 LoRa 客户端。它是用于教育和实验的 C++ 实现，并非官方固件的分支。项目演示了 Meshtastic 数据包的编码与解码，并包含环境传感器示例。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/xscreensaver-official/xscreensaver" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>There is no official XScreenSaver GitHub repository</span><span class='zh'>没有官方的 XScreenSaver GitHub 仓库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>XScreenSaver lacks an official GitHub repository, leaving users to rely on forks and mirrors.</span><span class='zh'>XScreenSaver 缺乏官方 GitHub 仓库，用户只能依赖 forks 与镜像。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>XScreenSaver lacks an official GitHub repository, leaving users to rely on forks and mirrors. The situation highlights how long-running open-source projects drift from centralized hosting, complicating updates and issue tracking.</span><span class='zh'>XScreenSaver 缺乏官方 GitHub 仓库，用户只能依赖 forks 与镜像。本质上反映了长期运行的开源项目可能渐渐远离集中托管，更新与问题跟踪变得更加困难。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Millillion/lasm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Lasm – Write formally verified webapps in Lean for Node/serverless/edge</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Lasm——用 Lean 为 Node/无服务器/边缘计算编写形式化验证的网页应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lasm lets developers write web applications in Lean with formal verification, targeting Node, serverless, and edge environments.</span><span class='zh'>Lasm 让开发者用 Lean 编写并进行形式化验证的网络应用，面向 Node、无服务器/边缘计算环境。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lasm lets developers write web applications in Lean with formal verification, targeting Node, serverless, and edge environments. It demonstrates how strong guarantees can be baked into modern web apps, reducing runtime errors and increasing security.</span><span class='zh'>Lasm 让开发者用 Lean 编写并进行形式化验证的网络应用，面向 Node、无服务器/边缘计算环境。展示了在现代 Web 开发中嵌入定理证明保证的可行性与潜在收益。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gauge-sh/gauge-cli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Gauge – Open-Source CLI to recursively improve your docs</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Gauge——递归改进文档的开源 CLI</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gauge is an open-source CLI that recursively improves documentation for coding agents.</span><span class='zh'>Gauge 是一个用于递归改进 coding agent 文档的开源 CLI。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gauge is an open-source CLI that recursively improves documentation for coding agents. It runs tasks, analyzes agent transcripts for friction or failures, creates documentation variants, and repeats the process until a variant resolves the issues. The tool includes a local execution backend and can also optimize skills, CLIs, and other agent-facing interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>Gauge 是一个用于递归改进 coding agent 文档的开源 CLI。它会运行任务、分析 agent 会话记录中的阻碍或失败、生成文档变体，并重复这一过程，直到某个变体解决相关问题。该工具包含本地执行后端，也可用于优化技能、CLI 和其他面向 agent 的界面。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flawless UI-UX is a skill for coding agents that helps them build distinctive and usable interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>Flawless UI-UX 是一项帮助编程智能体构建具有特色且易用界面的技能。</span></p>

The installer can be run with npm, pnpm, or Bun, and lets users choose project or global scope, select agents, review destinations, and confirm file copying.</span><span class='zh'>Flawless UI-UX 是一项帮助编程智能体构建具有特色且易用界面的技能。1.0.0 版本提供交互式安装程序，并支持 12 种智能体。用户可以通过 npm、pnpm 或 Bun 启动安装器，选择项目级或全局范围、指定智能体、检查目标位置并确认复制文件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AmpereComputing/panthera" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Panthera: Ampere&#x27;s CPU Performance Model</span><span class='zh'>Panthera：Ampere 的 CPU 性能模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Panthera is Ampere’s cycle-accurate software performance model for studying prospective CPU features and evaluating microarchitectural trade-offs.</span><span class='zh'>Panthera 是 Ampere 的周期精确软件性能模型，用于研究候选 CPU 特性并评估微架构取舍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Panthera is Ampere’s cycle-accurate software performance model for studying prospective CPU features and evaluating microarchitectural trade-offs. It is built by Ampere’s Architecture &amp; Performance team to guide development of high-performance cloud processors. The project includes a user guide for building and using Panthera and its associated tools.</span><span class='zh'>Panthera 是 Ampere 的周期精确软件性能模型，用于研究候选 CPU 特性并评估微架构取舍。它由 Ampere 架构与性能团队开发，以指导高性能云处理器的设计。项目还提供了构建和使用 Panthera 及相关工具的用户指南。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/andrewboldi/tmux-restore" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Tmux-restore saves tmux windows and agent sessions across reboots</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Tmux-restore 可在重启后保存 tmux 窗口和代理会话</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>tmux-restore restores tmux sessions, layouts, and scrollback after a reboot.</span><span class='zh'>tmux-restore 可在重启后恢复 tmux 会话、布局和滚动缓冲区。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>tmux-restore restores tmux sessions, layouts, and scrollback after a reboot. It can also reopen conversations for Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, opencode, and pi. The project describes itself as reboot-proof tmux for coding agents.</span><span class='zh'>tmux-restore 可在重启后恢复 tmux 会话、布局和滚动缓冲区。它还能够重新打开 Claude Code、Codex、Gemini CLI、opencode 和 pi 中此前的对话。该项目将自己定位为面向编程代理的“重启无忧” tmux 工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ndrean/ZeBridge" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ZeBridge – PostgreSQL replicas on devices, synchronized over NATS</span><span class='zh'>ZeBridge：通过 NATS 同步设备上的 PostgreSQL 副本</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ZeBridge is a bidirectional, local-first synchronization engine connecting PostgreSQL with edge SQLite, PGLITE, PostgreSQL, and DuckDB clients over NATS.</span><span class='zh'>ZeBridge 是一个双向、以本地优先为理念的同步引擎，通过 NATS 连接 PostgreSQL 与边缘端的 SQLite、PGLITE、PostgreSQL 和 DuckDB 客户端。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ZeBridge is a bidirectional, local-first synchronization engine connecting PostgreSQL with edge SQLite, PGLITE, PostgreSQL, and DuckDB clients over NATS. It keeps a local database on each device and queues writes while the device is offline. When connectivity returns, PostgreSQL remains authoritative by applying rules, resolving conflicts, and returning a result to the application.</span><span class='zh'>ZeBridge 是一个双向、以本地优先为理念的同步引擎，通过 NATS 连接 PostgreSQL 与边缘端的 SQLite、PGLITE、PostgreSQL 和 DuckDB 客户端。它在每台设备上保留本地数据库，并在离线时排队保存写入操作。重新连接后，PostgreSQL 仍是权威数据源，负责应用规则、解决冲突并向应用返回结果。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>27</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50354763' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>3</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50300902" data-id="50300902" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Code" data-score="27" data-comments="21" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50300902" data-time="1793346520">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/4yk5/Ghostman" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Ghostman – A fast, keyboard-driven TUI API client and webhook inspector</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Ghostman——快速、键盘驱动的 TUI API 客户端与 Webhook 检查器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ghostman is a keyboard-driven API client and webhook inspector that runs in the terminal.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostman 是一个运行在终端中的键盘驱动 API 客户端和 Webhook 检查器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ghostman is a keyboard-driven API client and webhook inspector that runs in the terminal. It is local-first, requires no cloud account, uses no Electron, and is presented as a lightweight alternative to heavier modern API clients.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostman 是一个运行在终端中的键盘驱动 API 客户端和 Webhook 检查器。它以本地优先为设计理念，无需云账户，也不使用 Electron，定位是更轻量的现代 API 客户端替代方案。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>27</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50300902' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>21</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50380273" data-id="50380273" data-tags="AI|Programming|Code" data-score="26" data-comments="16" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50380273" data-time="1793892336">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/sdalberto/ia-da-sobrevivencia/blob/main/README.en.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/sdalberto/ia-da-sobrevivencia/blob/main/README.en.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Offline survival assistant – local LLM and Kiwix, runs from a USB stick</span><span class='zh'>离线生存助手：本地 LLM 与 Kiwix，可从 U 盘运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This survival assistant runs fully offline on Windows, Linux, and macOS, including from a laptop or USB drive.</span><span class='zh'>这款生存助手可在 Windows、Linux 和 macOS 上完全离线运行，也可以从笔记本电脑或 U 盘启动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This survival assistant runs fully offline on Windows, Linux, and macOS, including from a laptop or USB drive. It provides sourced guides on water, fire, food, first aid, and other topics in Portuguese, English, and Spanish, alongside a local LLM using llama.cpp or Ollama. Kiwix libraries provide additional offline reference material.</span><span class='zh'>这款生存助手可在 Windows、Linux 和 macOS 上完全离线运行，也可以从笔记本电脑或 U 盘启动。它提供葡萄牙语、英语和西班牙语的水、火、食物、急救等主题资料，并通过 llama.cpp 或 Ollama 运行本地 LLM。Kiwix 资料库则提供额外的离线参考内容。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anteloc/ldraw-mecha/tree/v0.5.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Agents infer Lego mechanisms from sources, then animate them</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：代理从资料推断 LEGO 机构并为其制作动画</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LDraw Mecha uses agentic language models to analyze annotated LDraw LEGO model files, infer their submodels and mechanisms, plan animations, and execute them in Blender.</span><span class='zh'>LDraw Mecha 使用代理式语言模型分析带注释的 LDraw LEGO 模型文件，推断其中的子模型和机械结构，规划动画，并在 Blender 中执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LDraw Mecha uses agentic language models to analyze annotated LDraw LEGO model files, infer their submodels and mechanisms, plan animations, and execute them in Blender. It outputs a rigged and animated Blender file from an initial .mpd source file. The project builds on LDraw Nova, an earlier tool for generating LEGO CAD models from LDraw source code.</span><span class='zh'>LDraw Mecha 使用代理式语言模型分析带注释的 LDraw LEGO 模型文件，推断其中的子模型和机械结构，规划动画，并在 Blender 中执行。它可以从初始的 .mpd 源文件生成带绑定和动画的 Blender 文件。该项目建立在此前用于从 LDraw 源代码生成 LEGO CAD 模型的 LDraw Nova 之上。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/awslabs/aidlc-workflows" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Aidlc: Adaptive workflow steering rules for AI coding agents</span><span class='zh'>Aidlc：用于 AI 编码代理的自适应工作流引导规则</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Aidlc introduces adaptive workflow steering rules to guide AI coding agents, aiming to improve safety, reliability, and efficiency in code generation.</span><span class='zh'>Aidlc 提出用于 AI 编码代理的自适应工作流引导规则，旨在提升代码生成的安全性、稳健性与效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Aidlc introduces adaptive workflow steering rules to guide AI coding agents, aiming to improve safety, reliability, and efficiency in code generation. The framework uses dynamic policies to adapt to tasks and context, reducing riskier operations.</span><span class='zh'>Aidlc 提出用于 AI 编码代理的自适应工作流引导规则，旨在提升代码生成的安全性、稳健性与效率。通过动态策略调整任务与上下文，降低风险操作的发生。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AmadeusITGroup/Edison" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Parsing EDIFACT in Python with syntax trees</span><span class='zh'>在 Python 中用语法树解析 EDIFACT</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Edison provides a Python toolkit to parse EDIFACT messages using syntax trees, enabling more readable, verifiable parsing of standardized EDI data.</span><span class='zh'>Edison 提供了一个用语法树解析 EDIFACT 的 Python 工具包，使对标准化EDI数据的解析更加直观且可验证。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Edison provides a Python toolkit to parse EDIFACT messages using syntax trees, enabling more readable, verifiable parsing of standardized EDI data. This approach helps developers validate and transform large batch files used in travel, logistics, and commerce.</span><span class='zh'>Edison 提供了一个用语法树解析 EDIFACT 的 Python 工具包，使对标准化EDI数据的解析更加直观且可验证。该方法有助于在旅行业、物流和商业领域处理的大规模文件进行验证与转换。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/sshah03/recalc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Recalc – Spreadsheet where cells can be Claude Code or Codex terminals</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Recalc——单元格可以运行 Claude Code 或 Codex 的电子表格</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Recalc is a spreadsheet that can host a lead Claude Code or Codex session in a side panel while allowing each cell to act as a terminal session.</span><span class='zh'>Recalc 是一款电子表格应用，可在侧栏运行主 Claude Code 或 Codex 会话，并让每个单元格充当终端会话。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Recalc is a spreadsheet that can host a lead Claude Code or Codex session in a side panel while allowing each cell to act as a terminal session. Sessions can communicate through grid cells, named cells, or an MCP server, and can be continued in a regular terminal. The tool currently runs locally on macOS, with Linux support described as less tested.</span><span class='zh'>Recalc 是一款电子表格应用，可在侧栏运行主 Claude Code 或 Codex 会话，并让每个单元格充当终端会话。不同会话可以通过网格单元格、命名单元格或 MCP 服务器互相通信，也可以在普通终端中继续运行。目前该工具可在 macOS 本地运行，Linux 支持则尚未充分测试。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rkaehn/uniform" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: JavaScript web apps with WASM layout and GPU rendering in 280 KB</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 WASM 布局和 GPU 渲染构建 280 KB 的 JavaScript 网页应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Uniform is a JavaScript framework for web applications that uses WebAssembly for layout and GPU rendering, with a reported size of 280 KB.</span><span class='zh'>Uniform 是一个用于网页应用的 JavaScript 框架，使用 WebAssembly 处理布局并通过 GPU 渲染，体积据称为 280 KB。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Uniform is a JavaScript framework for web applications that uses WebAssembly for layout and GPU rendering, with a reported size of 280 KB. Its creator aims to avoid the platform limits of native frameworks, the HTML/CSS constraints of browser-engine approaches, and the size or language requirements of other cross-platform frameworks. The design bundles the UI framework with the application so developers can update it independently of platform release cycles.</span><span class='zh'>Uniform 是一个用于网页应用的 JavaScript 框架，使用 WebAssembly 处理布局并通过 GPU 渲染，体积据称为 280 KB。作者希望避免原生框架的平台限制、基于浏览器引擎方案所继承的 HTML/CSS 约束，以及其他跨平台框架的体积或语言要求。该设计将 UI 框架直接与应用打包，使开发者能够独立于平台发布周期更新它。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kauldron is a modular, scalable library for training machine learning models, aimed at simplifying large-scale training and experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>Kauldron 是一个模块化、可扩展的机器学习模型训练库，旨在简化大规模训练与实验流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kauldron is a modular, scalable library for training machine learning models, aimed at simplifying large-scale training and experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>Kauldron 是一个模块化、可扩展的机器学习模型训练库，旨在简化大规模训练与实验流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rbartoli/agent-usage-guard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: agent-usage-guard – a Claude Code mod to protect your usage window</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：agent-usage-guard——为 Claude Code 设计的使用时段保护插件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Claude Code modification adds an usage-window guard to prevent overuse or budget overruns, useful for teams running Claude in production.</span><span class='zh'>一个 Claude Code 的修改插件增设使用时段保护，帮助避免超出预算或滥用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Claude Code modification adds an usage-window guard to prevent overuse or budget overruns, useful for teams running Claude in production. It introduces lightweight enforcements for access windows and prompts.</span><span class='zh'>一个 Claude Code 的修改插件增设使用时段保护，帮助避免超出预算或滥用。适用于在生产环境中运行 Claude 的团队，提供对访问时间的轻量级约束。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/biolend83/tubehaus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TubeHaus – an open-source YouTube client with no Google account</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：TubeHaus——无需 Google 账户的开源 YouTube 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TubeHaus is a private, self-hosted YouTube client available as a Docker Compose web app and native apps for Android, Windows, macOS, Linux, and iPhone/iPad.</span><span class='zh'>TubeHaus 是一款私密、自托管的 YouTube 客户端，提供 Docker Compose 网页版，以及 Android、Windows、macOS、Linux 和 iPhone/iPad 原生应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TubeHaus is a private, self-hosted YouTube client available as a Docker Compose web app and native apps for Android, Windows, macOS, Linux, and iPhone/iPad. It requires no Google account and provides no tracking. The client preserves subscriptions and history on the user&#x27;s own server or device while retaining a YouTube-like layout and keyboard shortcuts.</span><span class='zh'>TubeHaus 是一款私密、自托管的 YouTube 客户端，提供 Docker Compose 网页版，以及 Android、Windows、macOS、Linux 和 iPhone/iPad 原生应用。它无需 Google 账户，也不进行跟踪。用户可以将订阅和历史记录保存在自己的服务器或设备上，同时保留类似 YouTube 的布局和键盘快捷键。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jakegarrett13-afk/UV-5RM-Radio-Mod" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>UV-5RM-Radio-Mod</span><span class='zh'>UV-5RM 无线电改装</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This custom firmware for Baofeng UV-5RM radios adds applications including texting and spectrum tools.</span><span class='zh'>这套 Baofeng UV-5RM 自定义固件新增了短信和频谱工具等应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This custom firmware for Baofeng UV-5RM radios adds applications including texting and spectrum tools. Its texting feature supports short messages between two radios with delivery confirmation and requires no phone, internet connection, or additional hardware. CHIRP will no longer communicate with the modified device, so channels must be programmed with the included applications.</span><span class='zh'>这套 Baofeng UV-5RM 自定义固件新增了短信和频谱工具等应用。短信功能支持两台无线电之间发送短消息并确认送达，不需要手机、互联网或额外硬件。改装后设备将无法再与 CHIRP 通信，因此必须使用随附应用配置频道。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Neighborhood-Grid/flatpoints" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: 3D to 2D, point clouds flattening (geometry images)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：3D 转 2D，展平点云（几何图像）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flatpoints applies geometry-image principles directly to point clouds.</span><span class='zh'>Flatpoints 将几何图像的原理直接应用于点云。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flatpoints applies geometry-image principles directly to point clouds. It maps each 3D point from a point cloud sampled on an unknown surface to a 2D pixel, arranging the points into a square grid. The resulting flat representation can serve as an image-like indexing structure.</span><span class='zh'>Flatpoints 将几何图像的原理直接应用于点云。它把从未知曲面采样得到的每个三维点映射为二维像素，并将这些点排列到方形网格中。生成的平面表示可以作为类似图像的索引结构。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This project is a model zoo of blood-glucose forecasters trained and tested on the author’s own data and on the public Metabonet dataset. It includes 15 forecasting models, including models developed by the author. Most of the repository’s code was generated with AI.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个血糖预测模型集合，使用作者自己的数据和公开的 Metabonet 数据集进行训练与测试。项目包含 15 个预测模型，其中包括作者自行开发的模型。代码仓库中的大部分代码由 AI 生成。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Netflix/vmaf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Netflix vmaf: Perceptual video quality assessment based on multi-method fusion</span><span class='zh'>Netflix VMAF：基于多方法融合的主观感知视频质量评估</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Netflix VMAF blends several perceptual quality metrics into a single score, improving correlation with viewer experience across encoding settings.</span><span class='zh'>Netflix VMAF 将多种感知质量指标融合为一个综合分数，在不同编码设置下与用户观感的相关性更高，提升了流媒体的质量评估与编解码评估能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Netflix VMAF blends several perceptual quality metrics into a single score, improving correlation with viewer experience across encoding settings. The project shows how multi-method fusion enhances streaming QA and codec evaluation.</span><span class='zh'>Netflix VMAF 将多种感知质量指标融合为一个综合分数，在不同编码设置下与用户观感的相关性更高，提升了流媒体的质量评估与编解码评估能力。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1959</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jjba23/sift" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sift: LSP, Lint, Format Lisp, Scheme and Emacs Lisp</span><span class='zh'>Sift：用于 Lisp、Scheme 和 Emacs Lisp 的 LSP、Lint 与格式化工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sift is an idempotent formatter, rule-based linter, and language server for Scheme, Emacs Lisp, and Common Lisp.</span><span class='zh'>Sift 是面向 Scheme、Emacs Lisp 和 Common Lisp 的幂等格式化器、基于规则的 linter 和语言服务器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sift is an idempotent formatter, rule-based linter, and language server for Scheme, Emacs Lisp, and Common Lisp. It reports malformed Lisp source and rewrites whitespace while preserving comments and read-time forms in their original positions. Formatting an already formatted file produces no further changes, a property asserted across the test suite.</span><span class='zh'>Sift 是面向 Scheme、Emacs Lisp 和 Common Lisp 的幂等格式化器、基于规则的 linter 和语言服务器。它会报告格式错误的 Lisp 源码并重写空白，同时保留注释和读取时形式的原始位置。对已经格式化的文件再次格式化不会产生变化，该特性在整个测试套件中都有断言验证。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>12</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50460263' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50417478" data-id="50417478" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Code" data-score="12" data-comments="10" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50417478" data-time="1794173193">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/SafeScript/safescript" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1960</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SafeScript/safescript" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: SafeScript, a safe version of JavaScript for executing untrusted code</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：SafeScript——用于执行不可信代码的安全版 JavaScript</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SafeScript is a strict computational subset of JavaScript designed to execute untrusted code in multiple environments.</span><span class='zh'>SafeScript 是 JavaScript 的严格计算子集，旨在让不可信代码能够在多种环境中安全执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SafeScript is a strict computational subset of JavaScript designed to execute untrusted code in multiple environments. It excludes features including eval, new, globals, and prototype access, then compiles to plain JavaScript and PHP. The compiler is about 48 KB minified, or roughly 12 KB gzipped, and uses .safe files intended to be locally verifiable.</span><span class='zh'>SafeScript 是 JavaScript 的严格计算子集，旨在让不可信代码能够在多种环境中安全执行。它排除了 eval、new、全局变量和原型访问等功能，并可编译为普通 JavaScript 和 PHP。编译器压缩后约 48 KB、gzip 后约 12 KB，使用 .safe 文件格式，并支持本地验证。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>12</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50417478' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>10</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50405688" data-id="50405688" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="10" data-comments="1" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50405688" data-time="1794067672">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Datastructures-JS is a JavaScript data-structures project with TypeScript support.</span><span class='zh'>Datastructures-JS 是一个支持 TypeScript 的 JavaScript 数据结构项目。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Datastructures-JS is a JavaScript data-structures project with TypeScript support. The project also provides an associated planning board.</span><span class='zh'>Datastructures-JS 是一个支持 TypeScript 的 JavaScript 数据结构项目。项目还提供了配套的规划看板。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/wolveflake/wolvekafka" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1990</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/wolveflake/wolvekafka" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: WolveKafka – Consume Kafka with a plain HTTP endpoint</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：WolveKafka——通过纯 HTTP 接口消费 Kafka</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WolveKafka lets applications consume Kafka through an HTTP handler in any programming language.</span><span class='zh'>WolveKafka 允许应用使用任意编程语言，通过 HTTP 处理器消费 Kafka。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WolveKafka lets applications consume Kafka through an HTTP handler in any programming language. It manages consumer groups, batching, retries, offsets, and dead-letter queues. The project includes a quickstart, examples, deployment guidance, and comparison material.</span><span class='zh'>WolveKafka 允许应用使用任意编程语言，通过 HTTP 处理器消费 Kafka。它负责管理消费者组、批处理、重试、偏移量和死信队列。项目还提供快速入门、示例、部署指南和对比资料。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/ori-drs/drs-vpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1452842016/74145be6-6bc1-412d-b748-7023227c2aca' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">1994</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ori-drs/drs-vpt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: DRS-VPT. Localize a photo in a 3D point cloud without an initial pose</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：DRS-VPT——无需初始位姿，在三维点云中定位照片</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DRS-VPT estimates a camera’s six-degree-of-freedom pose from a single photo and a 3D point cloud without an initial pose or known camera intrinsics.</span><span class='zh'>DRS-VPT 可以根据一张照片和一个三维点云估计相机的六自由度位姿，无需初始位姿或已知相机内参。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DRS-VPT estimates a camera’s six-degree-of-freedom pose from a single photo and a 3D point cloud without an initial pose or known camera intrinsics. The same model works across driving LiDAR scans, indoor iPhone photos, and structure-from-motion maps. The authors provide the code, model, weights, and an online demo.</span><span class='zh'>DRS-VPT 可以根据一张照片和一个三维点云估计相机的六自由度位姿，无需初始位姿或已知相机内参。同一个模型可用于驾驶场景的 LiDAR 扫描、室内 iPhone 照片和运动恢复结构地图。作者提供了代码、模型、权重以及在线演示。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/davide-troiani/moeshift" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2004</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/davide-troiani/moeshift" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>M4 Pro vs. M5 Pro (24GB): Qwen 3.8 Flash Next at 374 Tok/S Prefill with MoEShift</span><span class='zh'>M4 Pro 对比 M5 Pro（24GB）：Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 使用 MoEShift 达到 374 Tok/s 预填充速度</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MoEShift is an Apple Silicon inference engine for memory-efficient, high-performance mixture-of-experts models.</span><span class='zh'>MoEShift 是一款面向 Apple Silicon 的高性能、节省内存的混合专家模型推理引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MoEShift is an Apple Silicon inference engine for memory-efficient, high-performance mixture-of-experts models. On a Mac mini with an M4 Pro and 24 GB of unified memory, it runs the 125-billion-parameter Qwen 3.8 Flash Next using under 10 GB of peak process memory. The project reports roughly half the peak memory usage of Strata and 2.6× faster long-prompt processing.</span><span class='zh'>MoEShift 是一款面向 Apple Silicon 的高性能、节省内存的混合专家模型推理引擎。在配备 M4 Pro 和 24GB 统一内存的 Mac mini 上，它运行 1250 亿参数的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 时，进程峰值内存低于 10GB。项目称，与 Strata 相比，它的峰值内存占用约少一半，长提示处理速度快 2.6 倍。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/09/top_stories_11092026'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>9</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50432586' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50474106" data-id="50474106" data-tags="Show HN|Open Source|Programming|Code" data-score="8" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50474106" data-time="1794516784">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/cerulion-inc/cerulion" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2009</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cerulion-inc/cerulion" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Cerulion – ROS 2-compatible robotics runtime with multi-process replay</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Cerulion——支持 ROS 2、带多进程回放的机器人运行时</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cerulion is a ROS 2-compatible robotics runtime written in Rust.</span><span class='zh'>Cerulion 是一个用 Rust 编写、兼容 ROS 2 的机器人运行时。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cerulion is a ROS 2-compatible robotics runtime written in Rust. It aims to reduce inter-process communication latency without requiring a single process or restricting messages to plain-old-data types. Its multi-process graphs can be deterministically resimulated, and existing ROS 2 nodes and launch files can run alongside native Cerulion Rust or Python code.</span><span class='zh'>Cerulion 是一个用 Rust 编写、兼容 ROS 2 的机器人运行时。它旨在降低进程间通信延迟，同时不要求将程序合并为单进程，也不限制消息必须使用普通数据类型。其多进程图可以进行确定性重演，现有 ROS 2 节点和 launch 文件也能与原生 Cerulion Rust 或 Python 代码并行运行。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/12/top_stories_11122026'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>8</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50474106' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50145628" data-id="50145628" data-tags="Show HN|Code" data-score="8" data-comments="2" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50145628" data-time="1792358653">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2014</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/omnipty-terminal/omnipty" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: OmniPTY – Opinionated terminal emulator for macOS and Linux</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OmniPTY – 面向 macOS 与 Linux 的定向终端仿真器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN: OmniPTY is an opinionated terminal emulator for macOS and Linux, delivering a streamlined, plugin-friendly terminal experience with optimized PTY handling.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OmniPTY 是面向 macOS 与 Linux 的定向终端仿真器，提供简洁的终端体验、插件友好、优化的 PTY 处理，面向追求速度与控制的高阶用户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN: OmniPTY is an opinionated terminal emulator for macOS and Linux, delivering a streamlined, plugin-friendly terminal experience with optimized PTY handling. Aimed at power users who value speed and control.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：OmniPTY 是面向 macOS 与 Linux 的定向终端仿真器，提供简洁的终端体验、插件友好、优化的 PTY 处理，面向追求速度与控制的高阶用户。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/Koppar-AI/koppar-lte-modem" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2025</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Koppar-AI/koppar-lte-modem" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A USB-C LTE modem that gives my computer a phone line</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一款通过 USB-C 为电脑提供电话线路的 LTE 调制解调器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This open hardware board gives a computer LTE data, SMS, and voice calls over USB-C.</span><span class='zh'>这款开源硬件板通过 USB-C 为电脑提供 LTE 数据、短信和语音通话功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This open hardware board gives a computer LTE data, SMS, and voice calls over USB-C. With a nano-SIM and one USB-C cable, a computer can route call audio to a headset, hand calls to a script or AI agent, and send texts. The four-layer KiCad board was designed from a one-page specification and reviewed against component datasheets.</span><span class='zh'>这款开源硬件板通过 USB-C 为电脑提供 LTE 数据、短信和语音通话功能。插入 nano-SIM 并连接一根 USB-C 线后，电脑可以把通话音频发送到耳机，也可以将通话交给脚本或 AI 代理处理，并发送短信。这块四层 KiCad 电路板根据一页规格说明设计，并逐项对照元件数据手册进行审核。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/10/31/top_stories_10312026'><span class='en'>Oct 31</span><span class='zh'>10月31日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>7</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50317331' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>2</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50328070" data-id="50328070" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="6" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50328070" data-time="1793556709">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/TanmayArya-1p/async-a-sync" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2027</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/TanmayArya-1p/async-a-sync" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Implicit futures for C – the first access to a buffer is the await</span><span class='zh'>C 的隐式 future：首次访问缓冲区就是 await</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Async-a-sync is a compiler and runtime system built on Fil-C that lets ordinary blocking-looking C code run asynchronously underneath.</span><span class='zh'>Async-a-sync 是构建在 Fil-C 之上的编译器和运行时系统，让表面上阻塞的普通 C 代码在底层异步运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Async-a-sync is a compiler and runtime system built on Fil-C that lets ordinary blocking-looking C code run asynchronously underneath. It introduces implicit asynchronous futures without adding syntax, with the first access to a buffer acting as the await point.</span><span class='zh'>Async-a-sync 是构建在 Fil-C 之上的编译器和运行时系统，让表面上阻塞的普通 C 代码在底层异步运行。它无需新增语法即可引入隐式异步 future，并将首次访问缓冲区作为 await 点。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/02/top_stories_11022026'><span class='en'>Nov 2</span><span class='zh'>11月2日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>6</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50328070' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50371886" data-id="50371886" data-tags="Show HN|Programming|Hardware|Code" data-score="6" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50371886" data-time="1793828089">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/RenderBear/haci" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2028</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RenderBear/haci" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: My compact, bio inspired distributed edge detection algorithm</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我的紧凑型仿生分布式边缘检测算法</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The project is a bio-inspired edge-detection algorithm modeled on the visual cortex.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一种受视觉皮层启发的仿生边缘检测算法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The project is a bio-inspired edge-detection algorithm modeled on the visual cortex. Its current benchmark results fall between traditional methods such as Canny and Sobel and modern deep neural networks, while retaining the interpretability of the former. The author plans to improve video frame rates and performance across different hardware, and is seeking collaborators.</span><span class='zh'>该项目是一种受视觉皮层启发的仿生边缘检测算法。目前的基准测试结果介于 Canny、Sobel 等传统方法与现代深度神经网络之间，同时保留了传统方法的可解释性。作者计划提升视频处理帧率和不同硬件上的性能，并寻找合作伙伴。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/spaces/huggingface/moe-blogpost" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The MoE Training Handbook</span><span class='zh'>MoE 训练手册</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This hands-on guide explains how to train Mixture-of-Experts models.</span><span class='zh'>这份实践指南介绍了如何训练混合专家模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This hands-on guide explains how to train Mixture-of-Experts models. It provides a navigable table of contents and covers the practical steps involved in building and training MoE systems.</span><span class='zh'>这份实践指南介绍了如何训练混合专家模型。页面提供可点击的目录，并涵盖构建和训练 MoE 系统所需的实际步骤。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/OmniJev/Flide" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Flide: Make Jev-like decision models up to 9.5x faster</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Flide，让 Jev 类决策模型最高提速 9.5 倍</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flide is an inference engine designed specifically for Jev-like decision models.</span><span class='zh'>Flide 是一款专为 Jev 类决策模型设计的推理引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flide is an inference engine designed specifically for Jev-like decision models. The project claims up to a 9.5x speedup over vLLM and SGLang and currently supports more than 30 popular local Jev-like models. It can be installed with pip using the package name flide.</span><span class='zh'>Flide 是一款专为 Jev 类决策模型设计的推理引擎。项目称其速度最高可达 vLLM 和 SGLang 的 9.5 倍，目前支持 30 多种热门本地 Jev 类模型。用户可以通过 pip 安装名为 flide 的软件包。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/08/top_stories_11082026'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>5</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50421155' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50410253" data-id="50410253" data-tags="Show HN|AI|Hardware|Code" data-score="4" data-comments="1" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410253" data-time="1794109684">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/AmSach/maple" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ff6600' data-tag='Show HN'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2047</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AmSach/maple" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Maple – Sub-2-BPW Moe Pruning to Run Gemma 4 26B at 45 TPS on a 6GB GPU</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Maple——次于 2 BPW 的 MoE 剪枝，让 Gemma 4 26B 在 6GB GPU 上达到 45 TPS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Maple is a structural-compression method for mixture-of-experts language models.</span><span class='zh'>Maple 是一种面向混合专家语言模型的结构化压缩方法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Maple is a structural-compression method for mixture-of-experts language models. It compresses Gemma 4 26B-A4B from 9.92 GB to 4.75 GB, equivalent to 1.48 effective bits per parameter. The project reports 100% in-VRAM execution on 6 GB laptop GPUs at 45.4 tokens per second.</span><span class='zh'>Maple 是一种面向混合专家语言模型的结构化压缩方法。它将 Gemma 4 26B-A4B 从 9.92 GB 压缩到 4.75 GB，相当于每参数 1.48 个有效比特。项目报告称，该模型可在 6GB 笔记本 GPU 上完全载入显存运行，速度达到每秒 45.4 个 token。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/07/top_stories_11072026'><span class='en'>Nov 7</span><span class='zh'>11月7日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>4</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50410253' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-50300049" data-id="50300049" data-tags="Programming|Open Source|Code" data-score="4" data-comments="0" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50300049" data-time="1793336787">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/skorotkiewicz/radio-in-a-box" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Radio-in-a-box: Self-hosted internet radio with Icecast, Liquidsoap and Rust CLI</span><span class='zh'>Radio-in-a-box：使用 Icecast、Liquidsoap 和 Rust CLI 的自托管网络广播箱</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A self-hosted radio stack that combines Icecast, Liquidsoap, and a Rust-based CLI to simplify running your own Internet radio.</span><span class='zh'>自托管的网络广播堆栈，将 Icecast、Liquidsoap 与 Rust CLI 组合在一起，简化自建互联网电台的部署。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A self-hosted radio stack that combines Icecast, Liquidsoap, and a Rust-based CLI to simplify running your own Internet radio. The project emphasizes portability, reproducibility, and a minimal maintenance footprint for hobbyists.</span><span class='zh'>自托管的网络广播堆栈，将 Icecast、Liquidsoap 与 Rust CLI 组合在一起，简化自建互联网电台的部署。强调可移植性、可重复性与低维护成本，面向爱好者。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">2059</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/awake-client/awake" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: A Git friendly open API client</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一个 Git 友好的开放 API 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Awake is an API client created after its developers encountered problems with Postman and Bruno around environments, workspaces, pre-request authentication, and performance on large collections.</span><span class='zh'>Awake 是一款 API 客户端，源于开发者在使用 Postman 和 Bruno 时遇到的环境、工作区、请求前认证及大型集合性能问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Awake is an API client created after its developers encountered problems with Postman and Bruno around environments, workspaces, pre-request authentication, and performance on large collections. The project began as a side project and became an everyday tool for its creators. It is presented as a Git-friendly open API client.</span><span class='zh'>Awake 是一款 API 客户端，源于开发者在使用 Postman 和 Bruno 时遇到的环境、工作区、请求前认证及大型集合性能问题。该项目最初是一个业余项目，后来成为创建者日常使用的工具。它被定位为一款对 Git 友好的开放 API 客户端。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Show HN' style='--c:#ff6600'>Show HN</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/11/10/top_stories_11102026'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>3</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50447169' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>0</a></span></div></div></article></main><footer class='foot wrap'>Hacker News Daily · <span class='en'>Source</span><span class='zh'>来源</span>: <a href='https://news.ycombinator.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>news.ycombinator.com</a> · <a href='/hackernews/feed.xml'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M4 11a9 9 0 0 1 9 9M4 4a16 16 0 0 1 16 16'/><circle cx='5' cy='19' r='1'/></svg> RSS</a></footer><nav class='tabbar'><a class='on' href='/hackernews/2026/11/13/top_stories_11132026'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M3 10.5 12 3l9 7.5V21h-6v-6H9v6H3z'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Today</span><span class='zh'>今日</span></span></a><a class='' href='/hackernews/#archive'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><rect x='3' y='4' width='18' height='18' rx='2'/><path d='M16 2v4M8 2v4M3 10h18'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Archive</span><span class='zh'>往期</span></span></a><a class='' href='/hackernews/trends/'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M3 3v18h18'/><path d='m7 15 4-4 3 3 6-7'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Trends</span><span class='zh'>趋势</span></span></a><a class='' href='/hackernews/favorites/'><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='m12 2 3.1 6.3 6.9 1-5 4.9 1.2 6.8L12 17.8 5.8 21l1.2-6.8-5-4.9 6.9-1z'/></svg><span><span class='en'>Saved</span><span class='zh'>收藏</span></span></a></nav>
